# EgoLife — A1_JAKE Day 1 — First 6 Hours of Recorded Transcript

- **Target duration:** `06:00:00.000` of concatenated recorded transcript time.
- **Selection rule:** Day-1 transcript chunks are ordered chronologically; wall-clock gaps with no transcript file are skipped. Within each chunk, time is measured from its first subtitle cue.
- **Cues included:** `7,893`.
- **First source cue:** `11000000` at `00:09:47,100`.
- **Last included source cue:** `20000000` cue `169` at `00:27:58,933 --> 00:28:00,166`.
- **Boundary rule:** include cues whose start is before the target boundary.
- **Text:** preserves the dataset speaker labels, Chinese transcript, and provided English translation when present. ASCII comma spacing is normalized to comma+space after transport parsing.
- **Recording gap:** the repository has no Jake Day-1 transcript chunks for `15:00` or `16:00`; those wall-clock hours are not counted toward recorded-time duration.

- `00:09:47,100 → 00:09:50,766` **Jake:** 好然后一个秒表 / Okay, then we need a stopwatch.

- `00:12:49,766 → 00:12:52,866` **Jake:** 这个是等大家陷入沉思的时候再用 / We'll use this when everyone is deep in thought.

- `00:13:42,766 → 00:13:47,266` **Jake:** 然后会在这导到这个硬盘里边 / Will transfer the data to this hard drive.

- `00:14:10,866 → 00:14:13,000` **Jake:** 现在一直接着这个第一个硬盘 / Now always connecting to this first hard drive.
- `00:14:13,566 → 00:14:16,400` **Jake:** 对然后这个硬盘它有两根线 / Yes, then this hard drive has two cables.
- `00:14:16,400 → 00:14:19,166` **Jake:** 一个是一个是接电脑的 / One is for connecting to the computer.

- `00:39:23,566 → 00:39:26,066` **Lucia:** 然后这个东西它原本是有材料包的 / And this thing originally comes with a materials kit.

- `00:39:29,333 → 00:39:31,200` **Lucia:** 就是既然是世界地球日的话 / Since it’s Earth Day

- `00:44:58,766 → 00:45:00,966` **Tasha:** 然后如果用培养皿水培的话 / And if you use a petri dish for hydroponics

- `00:52:02,200 → 00:52:04,133` **Lucia:** 嗯对这是这样子的 / Hmm, yes, it's like this.
- `00:52:04,133 → 00:52:06,533` **Lucia:** 对对对这块就这样挺好 / Yes, yes, this piece fits nicely.
- `00:52:06,533 → 00:52:07,866` **Tasha:** 这样子挺好的 / This fits nicely.
- `00:52:51,966 → 00:52:53,633` **Katrina:** 所有快递都在外面了吗 / Are all the packages outside?

- `00:52:55,533 → 00:52:56,700` **Jake:** 今天还到了几个 / Today, a few more arrived.
- `00:52:56,700 → 00:53:00,300` **Jake:** 但是还都会有管家过来送 / But the housekeeper will still come to deliver them.
- `00:53:01,766 → 00:53:02,366` **Shure:** 还有管家 / There's a housekeeper?
- `00:53:02,366 → 00:53:04,166` **Tasha:** 那他能把我的送来吗 / Can he deliver mine too?
- `00:53:04,933 → 00:53:05,566` **Jake:** 什么东西 / What thing?
- `00:53:06,966 → 00:53:09,166` **Jake:** 把什么东西培养皿 / What thing? The petri dish?

- `00:53:17,800 → 00:53:19,533` **Jake:** 能来送 / ...come to deliver.
- `00:53:24,766 → 00:53:27,666` **Tasha:** 好这游戏三个人玩刚刚好我们的 / Alright, this game is perfect for three people.
- `00:53:34,300 → 00:53:35,700` **Alice:** 掌管120组 / Managing 120 groups.

- `00:54:00,366 → 00:54:03,766` **Lucia:** 这里是不是应该稍微弧度再推一点 / Shouldn't we push this curve a bit more?

- `00:54:34,400 → 00:54:40,200` **Alice:** 不过感觉这个搁哪儿都行啊只不过这个头对着他的屁股好像也挺严丝合缝的 / But I feel like this can go anywhere, and this head fits snugly against its back.

- `00:27:44,800 → 00:27:46,333` **Jake:** 那我们放哪呢 / So, where should we put it?
- `00:27:46,833 → 00:27:48,333` **Jake:** 我放哪比较合适 / Where's the best place to put it?
- `00:27:48,566 → 00:27:49,466` **Alice:** 削皮刀 / Peeler
- `00:27:49,800 → 00:27:53,066` **Tasha:** 放这呗我觉得应该放烤箱上面 / Just put it here. I think it should go on top of the oven.
- `00:27:51,266 → 00:27:54,966` **Jake:** 这儿这个嗯 / Here, this, hmm
- `00:27:53,066 → 00:27:55,933` **Tasha:** 呃微波炉上面会比较合适 / Uh, on top of the microwave would be better.
- `00:27:53,600 → 00:27:54,700` **Alice:** 微波炉上面 / On top of the microwave
- `00:27:56,800 → 00:27:58,133` **Shure:** 咱这边只有微波炉 / We only have a microwave here.
- `00:27:59,066 → 00:28:00,400` **Jake:** 买烤箱了 / We bought an oven.
- `00:28:00,400 → 00:28:03,366` **Tasha:** 温度计烤箱温度计 / Thermometer, oven thermometer
- `00:28:05,166 → 00:28:06,266` **Alice:** 黑巧吗 / Dark chocolate?
- `00:28:06,400 → 00:28:08,900` **Tasha:** 对黑巧做那种熔浆蛋糕 / Yes, dark chocolate for making those molten lava cakes.
- `00:28:07,633 → 00:28:09,766` **Jake:** 嘿租的租的租的 / Hey, rented, rented, rented
- `00:28:09,333 → 00:28:12,066` **Katrina:** 好专业啊 / So professional.
- `00:28:12,066 → 00:28:13,533` **Alice:** 这提拉米苏那个对对对提拉米苏的叉 / This tiramisu, right, right, the fork for tiramisu.
- `00:28:13,533 → 00:28:16,133` **Tasha:** 还有提拉米苏的盒子哪呢 / Where's the tiramisu box?
- `00:28:16,133 → 00:28:19,700` **Tasha:** 嗯这伯爵红茶包 / Uh, this Earl Grey tea bag
- `00:28:19,233 → 00:28:20,800` **Jake:** 这些东西昨天应该都扫一遍 / We should have scanned all these things yesterday.
- `00:28:22,100 → 00:28:22,900` **Jake:** 扫一天 / Scan for a day
- `00:28:25,566 → 00:28:27,500` **Tasha:** 这个是那个咖啡酒 / This is that coffee liqueur
- `00:28:27,500 → 00:28:29,933` **Tasha:** 咖啡酒 / Coffee liqueur
- `00:28:28,833 → 00:28:31,833` **Jake:** 就是办完我们一起 / Once we're done, we can all...
- `00:28:29,933 → 00:28:31,333` **Katrina:** 你会调酒吗 / Can you make cocktails?
- `00:28:31,566 → 00:28:32,766` **Shure:** 我啊 / Me?
- `00:28:32,666 → 00:28:34,500` **Jake:** 看起来可以会 / Looks like you can.
- `00:28:34,533 → 00:28:37,933` **Jake:** 哈哈哈是吧可以会看起来是这样 / Haha, right? Looks like you can.
- `00:28:37,133 → 00:28:38,066` **Lucia:** 最最简单的 / The simplest one.
- `00:28:38,066 → 00:28:38,966` **Lucia:** 买一瓶威士忌 / Buy a bottle of whiskey.
- `00:28:38,966 → 00:28:40,200` **Lucia:** 买买几瓶可乐 / Buy a few bottles of cola.
- `00:28:40,666 → 00:28:42,466` **Lucia:** 然后兑一块就完事了 / And mix them together, done.
- `00:28:42,900 → 00:28:44,166` **Katrina:** 咱有多少个杯子呀 / How many cups do we have?
- `00:28:44,166 → 00:28:45,766` **Katrina:** 有20个杯 / We have 20 cups.
- `00:28:45,966 → 00:28:48,133` **Shure:** 有上面6个下面6个 12个 / Six on top, six below, 12 in total.
- `00:28:50,533 → 00:28:51,466` **Shure:** 我靠这什么 / Whoa, what is this?
- `00:28:51,466 → 00:28:52,366` **Jake:** 我们架上去吧
- `00:28:57,466 → 00:28:59,000` **Shure:** 咱们经费这么充足吗 / Do we have that much budget?
- `00:28:59,000 → 00:29:01,766` **Shure:** 我感觉我是不是应该花花呀 / Should I be spending more?
- `00:29:01,766 → 00:29:03,933` **Katrina:** 咱都是他的助手 / We're all his assistants.
- `00:29:04,366 → 00:29:06,700` **Jake:** 对这边主要花了 / Yeah, we mainly spent a lot here.
- `00:29:08,600 → 00:29:11,466` **Jake:** 花了很有用的很多费用 / Spent a lot on very useful things.

- `00:29:11,466 → 00:29:12,966` **Jake:** 非常有用 / Very useful.
- `00:29:15,000 → 00:29:16,733` **Tasha:** 啊对我也要买柠檬 / Ah, right, I need lemons too.
- `00:29:18,033 → 00:29:22,266` **Jake:** 对下午要买东西就记一下菜单吧 / Yeah, just write down the shopping list for the afternoon.
- `00:29:22,266 → 00:29:24,766` **Jake:** 或者单写写在这个白板上面 / Or you can write it on this whiteboard.
- `00:29:24,766 → 00:29:26,766` **Jake:** 这个白板现在你们都可以写吧 / You can all write on this whiteboard now, right?
- `00:29:24,866 → 00:29:26,866` **Jake:** 这个白板现在你们都可以写吧 / You can all write on this whiteboard now, right?
- `00:29:26,766 → 00:29:27,566` **Jake:** 比较好写 / It's pretty easy to write on.
- `00:29:26,866 → 00:29:27,666` **Jake:** 比较好写 / It's pretty easy to write on.
- `00:29:27,900 → 00:29:29,800` **Shure:** 这白板拍一下 / Take a picture of the whiteboard.
- `00:29:29,366 → 00:29:30,500` **Jake:** 拍拍完了 / Take a picture, done.
- `00:29:30,500 → 00:29:31,666` **Jake:** 我 再拍一下 / I'll take another picture.
- `00:29:30,766 → 00:29:31,100` **Shure:** 然后擦 / Then erase it.
- `00:29:32,266 → 00:29:32,666` **Shure:** 擦掉吧 / Erase it.
- `00:29:32,666 → 00:29:34,100` **Katrina:** 我刚拍了还有 / I just took a picture, there's still some left.
- `00:29:35,400 → 00:29:36,600` **Jake:** 都可以调监控的 / You can check the surveillance footage.
- `00:29:36,800 → 00:29:37,600` **Katrina:** 提拉米菲 / Tiramisu.
- `00:29:38,900 → 00:29:40,133` **Lucia:** 哦那个手指饼干 / Oh, those ladyfingers.
- `00:29:40,133 → 00:29:43,300` **Jake:** 对就这么拍一下拍完了 / Yeah, just take a picture, it's done.
- `00:29:41,966 → 00:29:43,800` **Tasha:** 淀粉肉松 / Cornstarch and pork floss.
- `00:29:43,800 → 00:29:47,400` **Shure:** 哇真的好赛博呀 / Wow, this is so cyberpunk.
- `00:29:47,400 → 00:29:49,166` **Shure:** 看一眼我拍完你的 / Let me see, I took a picture of yours.
- `00:29:49,166 → 00:29:52,000` **Jake:** 哈哈哈我记住你了 / Hahaha, I remember you now.
- `00:29:52,500 → 00:29:53,800` **Tasha:** 但这家特别贵 / But this store is super expensive.
- `00:29:54,166 → 00:29:55,600` **Tasha:** 还好经费充足 / Good thing we have a big budget.
- `00:29:57,533 → 00:29:58,966` **Katrina:** 哇你看看啊 / Wow, check it out.
- `00:29:58,966 → 00:30:02,466` **Katrina:** 大手套都可以买点饮料 / We can even buy some drinks with these big gloves.
- `00:30:02,700 → 00:30:04,133` **Jake:** 这啥呀酒 / What's this, alcohol?
- `00:30:04,200 → 00:30:04,933` **Jake:** 很多酒 / A lot of alcohol.
- `00:30:04,933 → 00:30:05,966` **Jake:** 可以 / Sure.
- `00:30:05,966 → 00:30:06,566` **Alice:** 要啥酱吗 / Do you need any sauce?
- `00:30:08,733 → 00:30:09,866` **Tasha:** 切蛋糕胚的 / For cutting cake layers.
- `00:30:10,766 → 00:30:12,533` **Katrina:** 然后这个冰 / And this ice.
- `00:30:12,766 → 00:30:13,700` **Lucia:** 这个怎么用啊 / How do you use this?
- `00:30:13,866 → 00:30:17,700` **Tasha:** 就是你把这个固定器冷冻还是有冷藏 / You either freeze or refrigerate this holder.
- `00:30:17,866 → 00:30:19,666` **Tasha:** 比如说你不是切了一个蛋糕了吗 / Like, say you just cut a cake.
- `00:30:19,666 → 00:30:20,466` **Shure:** 其实也可以 / Actually, that works too.
- `00:30:20,466 → 00:30:24,300` **Tasha:** 然后就把这两个固定比如说就固定在第几层 / Then you just fix these two holders, like, fix them on whichever layer.
- `00:30:25,300 → 00:30:27,100` **Tasha:** 然后呢用这个刀 / And then, use this knife.
- `00:30:27,100 → 00:30:28,100` **Alice:** 这样横着过去 / Cut it across like this.
- `00:30:28,100 → 00:30:30,933` **Tasha:** 对然后他就会切出来很平整的面 / Yeah, and it will cut out a very even surface.
- `00:30:30,933 → 00:30:32,566` **Shure:** 哎不是再往下一个 / Oh, no, a bit lower.
- `00:30:33,566 → 00:30:34,700` **Shure:** 哦那不是平格 / Oh, that's not level.
- `00:30:35,766 → 00:30:36,500` **Alice:** 手套吗 / Gloves?
- `00:30:36,600 → 00:30:37,700` **Tasha:** 无菌手套 / Sterile gloves.
- `00:30:39,100 → 00:30:41,533` **Shure:** 外卖买那边也没多少 / There aren't many left from the takeout.
- `00:30:41,033 → 00:30:42,333` **Jake:** 走挂上去 / Let's hang it up.
- `00:30:42,833 → 00:30:43,800` **Katrina:** 超市里有的话 / If the supermarket has it
- `00:30:43,800 → 00:30:44,466` **Shure:** 三四块钱 / It’s like three or four bucks.
- `00:30:44,700 → 00:30:45,500` **Tasha:** 黄豆粉 / Soybean flour.
- `00:30:54,733 → 00:30:56,466` **Tasha:** 没了哇 / Out of stock, huh?
- `00:30:57,100 → 00:30:58,500` **Lucia:** 好丰盛的材料 / So many ingredients.
- `00:30:59,300 → 00:31:00,100` **Jake:** 我去 / Oh my.
- `00:31:35,133 → 00:31:36,100` **Lucia:** 这个是我的 / This is mine.
- `00:31:36,100 → 00:31:37,300` **Lucia:** 我的眼镜盒 / My glasses case.
- `00:31:43,400 → 00:31:46,766` **Lucia:** 哎这个水是没开的吧 / Hey, this water isn’t opened, right?
- `00:31:46,766 → 00:31:48,533` **Lucia:** 没开的那我可以开一瓶吗 / If it’s not opened, can I open one?
- `00:31:48,533 → 00:31:49,733` **Lucia:** 啊这瓶开过了 / Ah, this one’s opened.
- `00:31:49,966 → 00:31:51,266` **Jake:** 对这瓶开过 / Yeah, this one’s opened.
- `00:31:51,966 → 00:31:52,966` **Lucia:** 这瓶好像没开 / This one seems unopened.
- `00:31:52,133 → 00:31:54,000` **Jake:** 这瓶没开 / This one isn’t opened.
- `00:31:52,966 → 00:31:54,533` **Lucia:** 这瓶没开这瓶没开 / This one isn’t opened, this one isn’t opened.
- `00:31:56,700 → 00:31:58,266` **Jake:** 要不贴个名字吧 / How about we put a label on it?
- `00:31:58,533 → 00:31:59,566` **Jake:** 有那个小贴纸 / Do we still have those little stickers?
- `00:31:59,800 → 00:32:02,233` **Jake:** 还在吗 / Are they still around?
- `00:32:02,433 → 00:32:04,000` **Jake:** 嗯已经 找不着了 / Hmm, can’t find them anymore.
- `00:32:05,400 → 00:32:06,066` **Alice:** 这好好看 / This looks nice.
- `00:32:06,066 → 00:32:07,400` **Alice:** 你可以在这折个角 / You can fold a corner here.
- `00:32:08,200 → 00:32:08,733` **Lucia:** 有道理 / Makes sense.
- `00:32:08,733 → 00:32:10,400` **Lucia:** 但是如果大家都折了一个角的话 / But if everyone folds a corner
- `00:32:10,400 → 00:32:11,266` **Alice:** 不一样的角 / Different corners.
- `00:32:11,533 → 00:32:13,333` **Jake:** 那那能记住哈 / That way you can remember.
- `00:32:13,333 → 00:32:15,100` **Alice:** 过来有个人是一个斜面 / Someone will have a diagonal one.
- `00:32:14,400 → 00:32:15,666` **Jake:** 写一下写一下吧 / Write it down, write it down.
- `00:32:15,933 → 00:32:16,966` **Lucia:** 我把外面就撕下来 / I'll just tear it off from the outside.
- `00:32:16,966 → 00:32:17,800` **Lucia:** 给他叠个花 / And fold it into a flower for him.
- `00:32:17,100 → 00:32:19,666` **Jake:** 就在这在这写个字吧 / Just write something right here.
- `00:32:21,866 → 00:32:22,333` **Jake:** 哦你还要 / Oh, you still need it?
- `00:32:22,333 → 00:32:23,333` **Lucia:** 笔在这里 / The pen is here.
- `00:32:23,333 → 00:32:26,333` **Lucia:** 我先简单在这个白的地方随手写一下 / I'll just quickly jot something down on this white part.
- `00:32:23,333 → 00:32:27,200` **Shure:** 对我明天早上要答辩一下 / Yeah, I need to defend my thesis tomorrow morning.
- `00:32:25,733 → 00:32:27,333` **Jake:** 好吧 / Alright then.
- `00:32:26,466 → 00:32:27,200` **Alice:** 划一刀 / Make a cut.
- `00:32:27,400 → 00:32:28,900` **Katrina:** 你们必须这么早吗 / Do you really need to do it this early?
- `00:32:29,400 → 00:32:31,466` **Shure:** 我们还有个模拟答辩 / We still have a mock defense.
- `00:32:31,466 → 00:32:32,666` **Shure:** 很奇怪 / It's quite strange.
- `00:32:32,666 → 00:32:34,000` **Lucia:** 啊你们还有模拟答辩 / Oh, you have a mock defense too?
- `00:32:34,000 → 00:32:35,000` **Katrina:** 论文还没写 / I haven't even finished my thesis.
- `00:32:39,800 → 00:32:41,800` **Shure:** 哦你是软件工程的是吧 / Oh, you're in software engineering, right?
- `00:32:41,800 → 00:32:43,000` **Katrina:** 嗯好 / Yeah, that's right.
- `00:32:44,566 → 00:32:45,533` **Shure:** 帮我改改代码吧 / Help me debug some code, will you?
- `00:32:45,533 → 00:32:47,800` **Shure:** 到时候哈哈哈 / We'll see, haha.
- `00:32:47,800 → 00:32:48,900` **Katrina:** 帮你改代码 / I'll help you with the code.
- `00:32:48,900 → 00:32:50,666` **Katrina:** 我自己代码还没跑完呢 / I haven't even finished running my own code yet.
- `00:32:52,166 → 00:32:52,966` **Katrina:** 哇 / Wow.
- `00:32:57,266 → 00:32:58,800` **Shure:** 出去搞杯咖啡喝 / Let's go grab a coffee.
- `00:32:59,166 → 00:33:01,166` **Shure:** 在外面坐一坐晒晒太阳 / Sit outside and enjoy the sun.
- `00:33:01,600 → 00:33:02,700` **Shure:** 无敌了 / It's unbeatable.
- `00:33:03,400 → 00:33:04,300` **Jake:** 点外卖吗 / Want to order takeout?
- `00:33:05,433 → 00:33:06,600` **Jake:** 吃午饭吗 / How about lunch?
- `00:33:07,166 → 00:33:09,300` **Jake:** 一点一点不饿是吧 / Not hungry at all, huh?
- `00:33:09,500 → 00:33:12,933` **Jake:** 对我们再录40分钟就结束了这一轮 / Alright, we'll finish this round in 40 more minutes.
- `00:33:13,933 → 00:33:15,466` **Jake:** 然后40分钟能干嘛呢 / What can we do in 40 minutes?
- `00:33:15,466 → 00:33:15,966` **Jake:** 点个外 / Order some food.
- `00:33:15,966 → 00:33:18,300` **Jake:** 就是有40分钟外卖差不多能到吧 / It should arrive in about 40 minutes, right?
- `00:33:18,300 → 00:33:20,533` **Jake:** 对吧现在点一下 / Let's order now.
- `00:33:20,533 → 00:33:24,066` **Jake:** 然后大概准备一下下午买点啥 / And then figure out what to get for the afternoon.
- `00:33:24,066 → 00:33:24,933` **Jake:** 在这写一写 / Write something here.
- `00:33:24,933 → 00:33:25,866` **Jake:** 拉倒了 / Forget about it.
- `00:33:25,866 → 00:33:27,266` **Tasha:** 做东西就是在一楼做是吗 / Are we making stuff on the first floor?
- `00:33:27,266 → 00:33:27,933` **Jake:** 在一楼做 / Yeah, on the first floor.

- `00:33:29,500 → 00:33:30,766` **Jake:** 对在一楼做 / Yes, do it on the first floor.
- `00:33:30,766 → 00:33:32,100` **Jake:** 然后在这摆这个 / Then put this here.
- `00:33:32,100 → 00:33:35,900` **Jake:** 这个就当就是甜点的那个台 / This will be the dessert table.
- `00:33:35,966 → 00:33:36,733` **Lucia:** 工作台工作台 / A workbench, a workbench.
- `00:33:36,233 → 00:33:40,766` **Jake:** 工作台 和甜点的那个台子放在也是这边是吧对 / The workbench and the dessert table are both placed here, right?
- `00:33:41,100 → 00:33:41,900` **Shure:** 不是就是他 / No, it's just...
- `00:33:41,900 → 00:33:42,866` **Jake:** 对是 / Yes, it is.
- `00:33:42,866 → 00:33:43,766` **Jake:** 这就是一个厨房 / This is a kitchen.
- `00:33:44,400 → 00:33:45,200` **Jake:** 全是你的 / It's all yours.
- `00:33:45,566 → 00:33:46,400` **Tasha:** 谢谢 / Thank you.
- `00:33:46,400 → 00:33:49,566` **Tasha:** 全开放的那如果那一天 / It's all open, so if one day...
- `00:33:47,566 → 00:33:52,433` **Jake:** 对上面是活动室这边是会议室和厨房合理吧 / Yes, upstairs is the activity room, here is the meeting room and the kitchen. Makes sense, right?
- `00:33:52,600 → 00:33:54,466` **Shure:** 我也带稍微带一点 / I'll bring a little bit too.
- `00:33:54,466 → 00:33:56,966` **Tasha:** 把所有的模具都放到这个袋子里 / Put all the molds into this bag.
- `00:33:57,466 → 00:33:59,200` **Shure:** 咖啡机就要很大的钱 / The coffee machine is going to cost a lot.
- `00:34:10,533 → 00:34:12,266` **Lucia:** 这个哦哦这是 / This, oh, oh, this is...
- `00:34:12,266 → 00:34:13,466` **Lucia:** 这是某位同学的眼镜 / This is someone's glasses.
- `00:34:13,466 → 00:34:17,100` **Lucia:** 我还以为是我们戴的这个太像了是的 / I thought it was ours. They look so similar, right?
- `00:34:18,800 → 00:34:19,966` **Shure:** 混淆你们用的 / Mixing up with yours.
- `00:34:21,633 → 00:34:22,633` **Jake:** 把这个擦掉了 / Wipe this off.
- `00:34:22,633 → 00:34:24,000` **Jake:** 你们想写就写 / You can write if you want.
- `00:34:24,300 → 00:34:26,066` **Lucia:** 眼镜中混入了一个叛徒 / A traitor snuck into the glasses.
- `00:34:27,700 → 00:34:32,333` **Shure:** AI中混入了一个傻子哈哈哈 / An idiot snuck into the AI, hahaha.
- `00:34:34,333 → 00:34:36,000` **Lucia:** 哎这个白板没有背面吗 / Hey, doesn't this whiteboard have a back side?
- `00:34:36,000 → 00:34:37,133` **Lucia:** 只有一面 / Only one side?
- `00:34:37,133 → 00:34:37,600` **Jake:** 有背面 / It has a back side.
- `00:34:37,600 → 00:34:38,533` **Jake:** 背面是个黑板 / The back side is a blackboard.
- `00:34:38,666 → 00:34:39,200` **Lucia:** 啊OK / Ah, OK.
- `00:34:39,500 → 00:34:41,300` **Jake:** 对可以用粉笔写 / Yes, you can write with chalk.
- `00:34:42,333 → 00:34:45,100` **Jake:** 他还给红笔粉笔对 / He also gave red pens and chalk, right.

- `00:42:47,066 → 00:42:49,333` **Jake:** 没有哎有是是这个吗 / No, wait, is it this one?
- `00:42:49,566 → 00:42:50,400` **Tasha:** 那这不就一个吗 / Isn't this just one?
- `00:42:50,400 → 00:42:51,266` **Jake:** 那就没有了 / Then that's it.
- `00:42:51,300 → 00:42:51,866` **Tasha:** 那好吧 / Alright then.
- `00:42:51,866 → 00:42:52,366` **Jake:** 那还是这个吧 / Let's stick with this one.

- `00:42:53,233 → 00:42:53,833` **Tasha:** 好的
- `00:42:56,866 → 00:42:59,566` **Tasha:** 喝的不用了吧就 / No drinks needed, I guess.
- `00:42:57,766 → 00:43:01,800` **Jake:** 好没了 还有吗 / Okay, that's it. Anything else?
- `00:43:00,066 → 00:43:01,133` **Tasha:** 没了 / Nope.
- `00:43:01,866 → 00:43:02,333` **Jake:** 别的呢 / Anything else?
- `00:43:02,533 → 00:43:03,800` **Jake:** 这些小东西 / These little things?
- `00:43:03,800 → 00:43:05,133` **Tasha:** 那我跟着他们吃吧 / I'll just eat with them.
- `00:43:05,133 → 00:43:06,566` **Tasha:** 他们会点套餐吧 / They will order the set, right?
- `00:43:09,000 → 00:43:10,933` **Lucia:** 巧克力是不是有两包 / Are there two packs of chocolate?
- `00:43:10,933 → 00:43:13,600` **Alice:** 我都都不是一个是可可粉一个是可可豆 / No, one is cocoa powder and the other is cocoa beans.
- `00:43:11,900 → 00:43:13,666` **Jake:** 来点 / Let's have some.
- `00:43:16,066 → 00:43:17,666` **Alice:** 哇都点好了吗 / Wow, everything's ordered?
- `00:43:17,666 → 00:43:18,466` **Jake:** 点了三个 / Ordered three.
- `00:43:22,266 → 00:43:26,000` **Tasha:** 呵呵但是被阳光晒的话他会不会坏 / Haha, but if it's exposed to sunlight, will it spoil?
- `00:43:25,133 → 00:43:28,133` **Jake:** 好那我随便给你点了啊 / Okay, I'll just order something for you.
- `00:43:26,066 → 00:43:26,466` **Lucia:** 哦 / Oh.
- `00:43:26,466 → 00:43:32,700` **Lucia:** 那我们要不然就放在啊放在这边这个地方不是不是很容易晒的 / Why don't we put it here, this spot doesn't get much sun.
- `00:43:28,833 → 00:43:32,333` **Tasha:** 粉类可以放粉类可以放在这里没关系 / Powdered stuff can go here, it's fine.
- `00:43:33,200 → 00:43:35,366` **Tasha:** 巧克力我都搁那个那里边 / I always put the chocolate in there.
- `00:43:33,466 → 00:43:36,600` **Alice:** 巧克力我都搁那个那里边了给给我吧 / I put the chocolate in there already, give it to me.
- `00:43:35,566 → 00:43:36,800` **Tasha:** 巧克力给我吧 / Give me the chocolate.
- `00:43:38,166 → 00:43:39,933` **Tasha:** 巧克力可以放冰箱 / Chocolate can go in the fridge.
- `00:43:48,866 → 00:43:49,700` **Jake:** 给你一个 / Here, take one.
- `00:44:02,066 → 00:44:05,966` **Lucia:** 这个是放在外面还是要放到冰箱去 / Should this stay outside or go in the fridge?
- `00:44:06,633 → 00:44:07,933` **Jake:** 你要套餐吗还是单点 / Do you want a set meal or just individual items?

- `00:44:09,400 → 00:44:10,200` **Jake:** 你要喝的吗 / Do you want a drink?
- `00:44:11,266 → 00:44:13,000` **Jake:** 不要喝的嗯 / No drink, got it.
- `00:44:15,533 → 00:44:16,500` **Tasha:** 要不这也放冰箱吧 / Maybe this should go in the fridge too.
- `00:44:16,500 → 00:44:17,300` **Tasha:** 没地方放 / There's no place to put it.
- `00:44:17,300 → 00:44:20,366` **Lucia:** 反正冰箱里东西少空间多 / Anyway, there's not much in the fridge, lots of space.

- `00:44:28,266 → 00:44:30,566` **Lucia:** 这个稍微往边上挪一点好 / Just move this over a little.
- `00:44:41,300 → 00:44:42,966` **Jake:** 来吧到你了 / Alright, your turn.
- `00:44:43,966 → 00:44:45,800` **Lucia:** 让我看看大家都点了什么 / Let me see what everyone ordered.
- `00:44:53,366 → 00:44:56,333` **Shure:** 怎么选一堆然后全擦了呢 / How come you chose a bunch and then erased them all?
- `00:44:53,366 → 00:44:56,366` **Lucia:** 这个榴莲芝士看着好让人心动但是 / This durian cheese looks tempting but...
- `00:44:56,333 → 00:44:56,933` **Lucia:** 一般来说 / Usually...
- `00:44:57,100 → 00:44:59,733` **Lucia:** 榴榴莲味的东西都有一点奇奇怪怪的 / Durian flavored stuff is kind of weird.
- `00:44:59,733 → 00:45:00,500` **Jake:** 嗯 / Hmm.
- `00:45:07,766 → 00:45:10,200` **Shure:** 我呢还有一大瓶伏特加 / I still have a big bottle of vodka.
- `00:45:15,933 → 00:45:17,766` **Tasha:** 温度放不下了 / The temperature's too high, can't fit it.
- `00:45:20,966 → 00:45:22,466` **Lucia:** 哎让我参考一下 / Let me take a look.
- `00:45:22,500 → 00:45:24,600` **Shure:** 伏特加都比较白的 / Vodka is usually clear.
- `00:45:24,666 → 00:45:25,900` **Alice:** 还有一个托盘 / There's another tray.
- `00:45:25,900 → 00:45:27,733` **Tasha:** 哎这其实还有个托盘 / Oh, there's actually another tray.
- `00:45:27,733 → 00:45:30,800` **Shure:** 你对啥都基本上喝不出它本来那味 / With anything, you can't really taste its original flavor.

- `00:45:32,700 → 00:45:34,800` **Tasha:** 那那要不把这这个给挪开 / So, should we move this away?
- `00:45:34,800 → 00:45:36,900` **Tasha:** 把那个水壶也给挪开 / And move the kettle too.
- `00:45:48,900 → 00:45:49,700` **Shure:** 这是什么呀 / What's this?
- `00:45:49,700 → 00:45:52,400` **Shure:** 小潜水光倒了 / The mini diver fell over.

- `00:46:05,133 → 00:46:05,933` **Jake:** 挺好 / Pretty good.

- `00:46:16,400 → 00:46:19,400` **Jake:** 塔莎 / Tasha.
- `00:46:21,000 → 00:46:23,133` **Jake:** 是j还是p人 / Are you a J or a P?
- `00:46:22,500 → 00:46:23,133` **Tasha:** P人 / P.
- `00:46:23,133 → 00:46:24,466` **Jake:** p人p人 / P, P.

- `00:46:28,766 → 00:46:29,833` **Jake:** yfp对 / YFP, right.

- `00:46:48,500 → 00:46:50,566` **Jake:** j区p区 / J zone, P zone.
- `00:46:54,666 → 00:46:55,733` **Jake:** 看到这个是不是很不爽 / Doesn't this annoy you?
- `00:46:55,733 → 00:46:57,666` **Jake:** 这些东西都要把它干掉 / We need to get rid of all these things.

- `00:47:03,466 → 00:47:04,533` **Tasha:** 这个也是搁这吧 / This one too, just put it here.

- `00:47:31,733 → 00:47:33,533` **Alice:** 哎你放那挡着 / Hey, you're blocking it by putting that there.

- `00:47:51,300 → 00:47:53,200` **Shure:** 他俩应该配料都差不多 / Their ingredients should be about the same.

- `00:48:01,733 → 00:48:03,300` **Jake:** 我先把这个填起来 / I'll fill this up first.
- `00:48:08,100 → 00:48:11,366` **Shure:** 哎呀哎哎呀哎呀 / Oh my, oh my, oh my.
- `00:48:15,333 → 00:48:21,233` **Shure:** 哈哈哈哈 / Hahahaha.
- `00:48:16,933 → 00:48:18,133` **Jake:** 哈哈哈哈 / Hahahaha.
- `00:48:21,233 → 00:48:23,000` **Jake:** 你是真多才多艺 / You're really multi-talented.
- `00:48:23,133 → 00:48:24,700` **Lucia:** 这是怎么发出的声音 / How did you make that sound?

- `00:48:37,133 → 00:48:37,900` **Jake:** 环保环保 / Environmentally friendly, environmentally friendly.

- `00:48:53,466 → 00:48:55,866` **Shure:** 点吧觉得差不多 / Order it if you think it's enough.

- `00:49:05,800 → 00:49:09,700` **Shure:** coffee没有coffee会死的 / Without coffee, I'll die.
- `00:49:15,966 → 00:49:17,333` **Jake:** 我们要哎 / We want, hey...
- `00:49:17,333 → 00:49:19,600` **Jake:** 肯德基是不是不用餐具点俩吧 / Does KFC need utensils? Order two more.

- `00:49:24,166 → 00:49:26,066` **Jake:** 哦那就点3个 / Oh, then let's order three.
- `00:49:26,066 → 00:49:27,133` **Jake:** 因为剩下的三个是吧 / Because there are three left, right?
- `00:49:27,133 → 00:49:28,266` **Tasha:** 啊什么东西 / Huh, what are you talking about?
- `00:49:28,266 → 00:49:30,133` **Lucia:** 哎不记得剩下几个啊 / Ah, I don't remember how many are left.
- `00:49:30,133 → 00:49:30,933` **Alice:** 餐具 / Tableware.
- `00:49:35,533 → 00:49:36,500` **Alice:** 动作太大 / Too much movement.
- `00:49:40,066 → 00:49:41,666` **Jake:** 好滴点了 / Okay, ordered.
- `00:49:42,766 → 00:49:44,133` **Jake:** 大家等饭吃吧 / Everyone, wait for the food.
- `00:49:45,133 → 00:49:48,000` **Lucia:** 我们我们要调几种酒啊 / How many kinds of drinks are we making?
- `00:49:51,000 → 00:49:52,800` **Shure:** 能调啥 / What can we mix?
- `00:49:52,800 → 00:49:55,566` **Lucia:** 基酒的话威士忌和龙舌兰也可以考虑一下 / For base spirits, we could consider whiskey and tequila.

- `00:50:11,100 → 00:50:13,700` **Jake:** 但是一词多义 / But the word has multiple meanings.
- `00:50:11,733 → 00:50:13,466` **Lucia:** 如果买了老母的话不买龙舌兰 / If we buy rum, we don't need tequila.
- `00:50:13,466 → 00:50:13,700` **Lucia:** 也行 / That's fine too.
- `00:50:13,700 → 00:50:16,166` **Lucia:** 我觉得风味还是有一点相似之处的 / I think the flavors are somewhat similar.
- `00:50:16,700 → 00:50:18,766` **Jake:** 大概去个两次吧应该 / Probably go twice, I guess.
- `00:50:19,000 → 00:50:21,766` **Jake:** 多的话就只能靠剧组带了 / If there's more, we'll have to rely on the crew.

- `00:50:33,366 → 00:50:40,666` **Tasha:** 把厨具洗一下吧清水冲就行我觉得要冲的只有 / Wash the kitchenware with clean water, I think you only need to rinse them.

- `00:50:38,300 → 00:50:38,866` **Jake:** 好 / Okay.

- `00:50:46,866 → 00:50:48,466` **Lucia:** 也可以用气泡酒 / We could also use sparkling wine.

- `00:51:03,066 → 00:51:06,066` **Jake:** 这个要怎么洗 / How should we wash this?

- `00:51:11,200 → 00:51:13,166` **Lucia:** 我们我们好像还有果粒橙是吧 / We still have some orange juice, right?

- `00:06:30,666 → 00:06:33,900` **Shure:** 5:20睡觉 13:14准时醒 / Sleep at 5:20, wake up at 13:14.

- `00:06:42,500 → 00:06:47,766` **Jake:** 那我是不是该快醒了啊我是不是快醒了啊还是已经醒了啊现在 / So should I wake up now? Am I almost awake or am I already awake?

- `00:06:50,700 → 00:06:54,200` **Jake:** 我是不是应该弄个钟买个钟买个表呢买个表 / Should I get a clock? Buy a clock, or buy a watch? Buy a watch.

- `00:11:31,500 → 00:11:32,866` **Jake:** 来看一会 / Come, take a look

- `00:11:39,333 → 00:11:39,933` **Jake:** 好看 / Looks good
- `00:11:39,933 → 00:11:40,566` **Alice:** 好看 / Looks good
- `00:11:40,566 → 00:11:40,966` **Jake:** 好看 / Looks good
- `00:11:45,666 → 00:11:47,666` **Shure:** 来给AI喂点抖音 / Let's feed the AI some TikTok
- `00:11:48,200 → 00:11:49,166` **Tasha:** 哈哈 / Haha
- `00:11:50,400 → 00:11:51,366` **Tasha:** 自己想看 / Wants to watch it
- `00:11:51,000 → 00:11:52,833` **Jake:** 评论评论评论 / Comment, comment, comment

- `00:11:56,633 → 00:11:58,500` **Jake:** 说你整我呢 / Says you're messing with me
- `00:12:00,633 → 00:12:01,766` **Jake:** 天天小考 / Daily quizzes

- `00:12:38,133 → 00:12:39,066` **Jake:** 哎呦 卧槽 / Oops, damn
- `00:12:40,033 → 00:12:40,700` **Jake:** 沙雕 / Silly
- `00:12:42,966 → 00:12:44,033` **Shure:** 成谢谢您 / Alright, thank you
- `00:12:45,166 → 00:12:47,533` **Jake:** 好好嘞 / Alright then
- `00:12:49,466 → 00:12:50,266` **Jake:** 哎呀 / Oh dear
- `00:12:56,466 → 00:12:56,900` **Shure:** 掉出来了 / It fell out
- `00:12:56,900 → 00:12:58,466` **Jake:** 哎呀怎么掉出来了 / Oh no, how did it fall out?
- `00:13:07,133 → 00:13:08,766` **Jake:** 会是老板第一个下车 / The boss will be the first to get off
- `00:13:09,966 → 00:13:11,633` **Jake:** 一般老板是第一个下车吗 / Is the boss usually the first to get off?
- `00:13:12,633 → 00:13:13,933` **Shure:** 不知道 / I don't know
- `00:13:12,633 → 00:13:13,933` Lucia: 不是应该先 / Shure: I don't know
- `00:13:13,933 → 00:13:14,600` **Lucia:** 司机先下车 / The driver gets off first
- `00:13:14,600 → 00:13:16,600` **Lucia:** 然后给你把门拉开这种 / And then I'll open the door for you.
- `00:13:17,166 → 00:13:19,433` **Jake:** 一般老板应该 不会第一个下车 / Usually, the boss wouldn't be the first to get out of the car.
- `00:13:19,433 → 00:13:21,233` **Shure:** 万一遇刺什么的是吧 / In case of an assassination, right?
- `00:13:21,233 → 00:13:24,300` **Jake:** 对第一个下车第一个被崩 / Yeah, the first to get out is the first to get shot.
- `00:13:22,200 → 00:13:24,300` **Shure:** 第一个下了之后喽 / After getting out first...
- `00:13:24,300 → 00:13:25,100` **Shure:** 保镖 / Bodyguards.
- `00:13:26,966 → 00:13:27,866` **Jake:** 怎么过去啊 / How do we get across?
- `00:13:27,866 → 00:13:29,133` **Jake:** 怎么停在这么个地方啊 / Why did we stop here?
- `00:13:29,566 → 00:13:30,300` **Tasha:** 跨过去 / Just step over it.
- `00:13:29,966 → 00:13:30,866` **Shure:** 我们只要 / We just need to...
- `00:13:31,033 → 00:13:32,200` **Jake:** 你跨一下 / Step over it.
- `00:13:32,200 → 00:13:32,700` **Shure:** 助跑一下 / Get a running start.
- `00:13:33,133 → 00:13:34,766` **Lucia:** 助跑一下哈哈哈 / Get a running start, haha.
- `00:13:33,466 → 00:13:34,500` **Shure:** 迟到了 / We're late.
- `00:13:35,266 → 00:13:36,466` **Shure:** 这怎么给停这地方 / Why did we stop here?
- `00:13:35,933 → 00:13:37,933` **Lucia:** 我好像有一点点晕 / I feel a bit dizzy.
- `00:13:36,466 → 00:13:37,400` **Shure:** 给差评 / Give a bad review.
- `00:13:37,400 → 00:13:38,333` **Jake:** 给差评 / Give a bad review.
- `00:13:38,966 → 00:13:39,766` **Jake:** 咋整啊 / What now?
- `00:13:40,866 → 00:13:41,900` **Jake:** 那边是可以走吗 / Can we walk over there?
- `00:13:42,500 → 00:13:43,666` **Jake:** 那那那过得去吗 / Can we get through there?
- `00:13:43,666 → 00:13:44,166` **Jake:** 过不去 / No, we can't.
- `00:13:44,166 → 00:13:44,900` **Tasha:** 盒马在哪 / Where's Hema?
- `00:13:46,066 → 00:13:46,900` **Jake:** 绕一圈吧 / Let's go around.
- `00:13:49,866 → 00:13:50,866` **Jake:** 问问吧 / Let's ask.
- `00:13:50,466 → 00:13:51,233` **Shure:** 盒马 / Hema?
- `00:13:51,233 → 00:13:53,433` **Lucia:** 那个有点高吧感觉跨不过去啊 / That looks too high, I don't think we can step over it.
- `00:13:51,700 → 00:13:53,733` **Shure:** 师傅那个盒马鲜生在哪 / Excuse me, where is Hema Fresh?
- `00:13:54,066 → 00:13:55,533` **Shure:** 从从这边绕过去是吗 / We need to go around from here, right?
- `00:13:56,100 → 00:13:56,866` **Shure:** 好的 / Got it.
- `00:13:56,866 → 00:13:57,466` **Shure:** 谢谢你 / Thank you.
- `00:13:57,466 → 00:13:59,133` **Jake:** 这个行人还得这么绕 / Pedestrians have to go all the way around.
- `00:14:02,100 → 00:14:02,933` **Lucia:** 哎不是 / Hey, wait.
- `00:14:03,466 → 00:14:04,600` **Lucia:** 等一下同志们 / Hold on, everyone.
- `00:14:04,633 → 00:14:07,066` **Lucia:** 腿长的人已经先过去了 / The long-legged people have already crossed.
- `00:14:08,133 → 00:14:10,500` **Lucia:** 呵呵是这是是是 / Haha, yes, that's right.
- `00:14:08,966 → 00:14:10,800` **Jake:** 我靠 / Oh my god.
- `00:14:11,166 → 00:14:12,166` **Shure:** 这这边绕一下吧 / Let's go around this way.
- `00:14:12,166 → 00:14:13,533` **Shure:** 还是 / Or...
- `00:14:12,633 → 00:14:13,533` **Katrina:** 这边过去 / Go this way.
- `00:14:13,533 → 00:14:13,966` **Katrina:** 在那边 / Over there.
- `00:14:13,966 → 00:14:14,833` **Shure:** 对走吧走吧 / Yes, let's go, let's go.
- `00:14:14,833 → 00:14:15,866` **Jake:** 头盔要不给他吧 / Should we give him the helmet?
- `00:14:15,866 → 00:14:16,666` **Jake:** 他跑过了 / He ran past.
- `00:14:16,666 → 00:14:19,866` **Lucia:** 哈哈腿长的人已经先过去了 / Haha, the person with long legs already went ahead.
- `00:14:20,066 → 00:14:21,233` **Jake:** 跑过了他们 / He ran past them.
- `00:14:25,866 → 00:14:26,866` **Jake:** 你也要去 / Are you going too?
- `00:14:26,866 → 00:14:28,933` **Lucia:** 不行我今天穿这衣服我翻不了 / No, I can't climb in this outfit today.
- `00:14:27,466 → 00:14:29,400` **Jake:** 你今天你今天不太行 / You're not in good shape today.
- `00:14:31,000 → 00:14:31,700` **Jake:** 下次一定 / Next time for sure.
- `00:14:40,866 → 00:14:42,633` **Shure:** 外面大门没有门禁 / The main gate outside doesn't have access control.
- `00:15:08,300 → 00:15:09,266` **Jake:** 人呢 / Where is everyone?
- `00:15:18,900 → 00:15:20,600` **Jake:** 补一个 / Let's fix one.
- `00:15:21,500 → 00:15:23,400` **Alice:** 底下那个底掉了没法往上弄了 / The bottom part fell off, can't put it back.
- `00:15:23,433 → 00:15:24,566` **Tasha:** 对啊 / Yeah.
- `00:15:25,500 → 00:15:27,533` **Jake:** 就翻的时候掉了是吗 / It fell off when you flipped it, right?
- `00:15:28,966 → 00:15:30,833` **Jake:** 这唱的还挺好 / This singing is pretty good.
- `00:15:35,433 → 00:15:37,933` **Jake:** 来带大家看看这个 / Come, let me show you this.
- `00:15:39,166 → 00:15:40,666` **Jake:** 北京大超市 / A big supermarket in Beijing.
- `00:15:44,066 → 00:15:45,000` **Jake:** 他们已经上去了 / They've already gone up.
- `00:15:45,466 → 00:15:46,700` **Tasha:** 为什么不等我们 / Why didn't they wait for us?
- `00:15:46,866 → 00:15:47,766` **Jake:** 这么快的 / So fast.
- `00:15:54,766 → 00:15:56,133` **Jake:** 好惨呐 / So miserable.
- `00:15:57,066 → 00:15:57,800` **Jake:** 他们是上去了 / They went up.
- `00:16:01,600 → 00:16:04,700` **Jake:** 这怎么上来这么快啊 / How did they get up so quickly?
- `00:16:04,800 → 00:16:05,733` **Lucia:** 什么情况 / What's going on?
- `00:16:06,233 → 00:16:08,066` **Lucia:** 你们怎么已经到了啊 / How did you guys get here so fast?
- `00:16:08,266 → 00:16:09,566` **Jake:** 我们还找你们呢 / We were still looking for you.
- `00:16:10,566 → 00:16:11,366` **Jake:** 怎么丢了呀 / How did we lose you?
- `00:16:11,366 → 00:16:12,566` **Katrina:** 系了个鞋带而已 / I was just tying my shoelaces.
- `00:16:12,566 → 00:16:14,700` **Shure:** 我们我们跟着轩导的步伐来着 / We were following the guide's pace.
- `00:16:14,700 → 00:16:16,100` **Jake:** 哦对 / Oh right.
- `00:16:16,100 → 00:16:16,933` **Jake:** 殊途同归 / All roads lead to the same destination.
- `00:16:16,966 → 00:16:17,700` **Shure:** 对 / Yeah.
- `00:16:17,700 → 00:16:19,300` **Katrina:** 柯达照相 / Kodak photo.
- `00:16:23,266 → 00:16:26,900` **Shure:** 我们之前去那nar的时候买了一次性的胶圈 / When we went to that nar place, we bought a disposable ring.
- `00:16:26,900 → 00:16:28,600` **Katrina:** 哦我之前也想买 / Oh, I wanted to buy one too.
- `00:16:28,600 → 00:16:29,166` **Tasha:** 伤心 / So sad.
- `00:16:28,966 → 00:16:31,300` **Katrina:** 我以为我们宿舍毕业旅行会去就 / I thought our dorm graduation trip would be there.
- `00:16:30,333 → 00:16:31,966` **Alice:** 买一套工具自己做 / Buy a set of tools and do it yourself.
- `00:16:31,300 → 00:16:33,500` **Katrina:** 新疆那地方就打算买一个 / I plan to buy one in Xinjiang.
- `00:16:32,333 → 00:16:33,466` **Tasha:** 早知道不翻了 / If I knew earlier, I wouldn't have turned it over.
- `00:16:34,066 → 00:16:34,666` **Alice:** 这翻掉了 / It flipped over.
- `00:16:34,700 → 00:16:37,066` **Katrina:** 我特别喜欢那种感觉那拍出来的 / I really like the feeling of those pictures.
- `00:16:37,733 → 00:16:40,566` **Katrina:** 那你有冲洗出来吗还是直打 / Did you develop them or just print directly?
- `00:16:41,366 → 00:16:43,233` **Shure:** 啊没没洗成那什么 / Ah, I didn't manage to develop them.
- `00:16:42,600 → 00:16:44,033` **Katrina:** 就是导出电子版是吧 / So you just exported the digital version, right?
- `00:16:43,966 → 00:16:47,000` **Shure:** 对弄电子版然后再是胶片 / Yes, export the digital version and then print on film.
- `00:16:50,000 → 00:16:53,266` **Shure:** 就是你透过阳光是能看到那个影子的 / You can see the shadow through the sunlight.
- `00:16:53,300 → 00:16:54,300` **Katrina:** 好浪漫啊 / So romantic.
- `00:16:54,700 → 00:16:55,266` **Shure:** 是吧 / Right?
- `00:17:06,166 → 00:17:07,166` **Jake:** 这是啥地方 / Where is this?
- `00:17:07,166 → 00:17:09,866` **Alice:** 找到一双长睫毛的大眼睛哎 / Found a pair of big eyes with long eyelashes.
- `00:17:09,866 → 00:17:11,233` **Alice:** 找到了在那 / Found them, over there.
- `00:17:11,233 → 00:17:11,766` **Shure:** 哇 / Wow.
- `00:17:12,766 → 00:17:14,500` **Alice:** 哎让ai来一下 / Hey, let AI come over.
- `00:17:12,766 → 00:17:14,866` **Lucia:** 不好意思又踩到你了 / Sorry, I stepped on you again.
- `00:17:16,300 → 00:17:17,066` **Katrina:** 海澜之家 / HLA.
- `00:17:17,066 → 00:17:19,233` **Katrina:** 海澜之家就在我们家旁边 / HLA is right next to our house.
- `00:17:19,266 → 00:17:21,733` **Shure:** 海澜之家男人的衣柜 / HLA, the wardrobe for men.
- `00:17:20,733 → 00:17:21,733` **Jake:** 这得多瞎啊 / How blind must you be?
- `00:17:21,733 → 00:17:22,666` **Jake:** 他看不到啊 / He can't see it.
- `00:17:22,666 → 00:17:24,700` **Alice:** 还有呢 / What else?
- `00:17:24,900 → 00:17:27,166` **Alice:** 怎么有股有毒的味道 / Why does it smell toxic?
- `00:17:27,166 → 00:17:29,066` **Tasha:** 用这个眼镜去测试你看 / Use these glasses to test, look.
- `00:17:29,066 → 00:17:30,500` **Tasha:** 做测试这 / Do the test here.
- `00:17:29,066 → 00:17:30,633` **Lucia:** 感觉刚装修完似得 / Feels like it's just been renovated.
- `00:17:30,500 → 00:17:32,466` **Tasha:** 在车站中找到海澜之家 / Found HLA at the station.
- `00:17:30,666 → 00:17:31,900` **Alice:** 哦原来在这等着我们呢 / Oh, that's where they were waiting for us.
- `00:17:31,900 → 00:17:33,900` **Alice:** 软广 / Product placement.
- `00:17:33,900 → 00:17:34,833` **Tasha:** 还真没找着 / I really didn't find it.
- `00:17:39,266 → 00:17:39,933` **Jake:** 怎么找啊 / How do you find it?
- `00:17:39,933 → 00:17:41,166` **Jake:** 海澜之家是啥啊 / What's HLA?
- `00:17:42,266 → 00:17:43,000` **Alice:** 海澜之家 / HLA.
- `00:17:42,266 → 00:17:45,133` **Lucia:** 就是一个卖男装的品牌 / It's a brand that sells men's clothing.
- `00:17:45,166 → 00:17:47,633` **Jake:** 那他是什么找他的logo吗 / So he's looking for their logo?
- `00:17:49,066 → 00:17:50,100` **Jake:** 他logo是什么样的 / What's their logo like?
- `00:17:51,066 → 00:17:52,800` **Alice:** 有嘛区别呢 / Does it make any difference?
- `00:17:53,066 → 00:17:54,500` **Alice:** 让AI来找一下 / Let AI find it.
- `00:17:53,333 → 00:17:56,000` **Lucia:** 怎么觉得没有都一样啊 / Why does it feel like they're all the same?
- `00:17:56,000 → 00:17:57,500` **Jake:** 都不知道要找什么 / I don't even know what to look for.
- `00:17:58,066 → 00:17:59,566` **Tasha:** 难度可能是进阶的 / Maybe it's at an advanced level.
- `00:17:59,566 → 00:18:01,266` **Alice:** 他可能就是 / He might just...
- `00:18:00,100 → 00:18:00,833` **Jake:** 他应该 / He probably...
- `00:18:01,266 → 00:18:03,266` **Alice:** 为了给你留下一个深刻的印象 / ...want to leave a deep impression on you.
- `00:18:03,466 → 00:18:04,266` **Jake:** 可能他就没有 / Maybe he just doesn't...
- `00:18:04,266 → 00:18:05,400` **Tasha:** 然后就一直在找海澜 / And then he keeps looking for Hailan.
- `00:18:05,400 → 00:18:06,600` **Jake:** 对一个一个找 / Yeah, one by one.
- `00:18:06,933 → 00:18:08,100` **Alice:** 然后你就特别特别想知道 / Then you really, really want to know...
- `00:18:08,100 → 00:18:09,633` **Alice:** 海澜之家到底长啥样 / ...what Hailan Home actually looks like.
- `00:18:09,566 → 00:18:10,000` **Jake:** 对 / Yeah.
- `00:18:09,733 → 00:18:11,366` **Alice:** 然后他的目的就达到了 / Then his goal is achieved.
- `00:18:11,500 → 00:18:12,000` **Alice:** 是这样吗 / Is that right?
- `00:18:11,733 → 00:18:12,966` **Jake:** 所以应该长啥样 / So, what should it look like?
- `00:18:13,066 → 00:18:13,866` **Alice:** 我也不知道 / I don't know either.
- `00:18:14,500 → 00:18:16,833` **Jake:** 海澜之家是自己有个logo是长这样吗 / Does Hailan Home have its own logo like this?
- `00:18:15,000 → 00:18:16,033` **Katrina:** 海澜的马场 / Hailan's ranch.
- `00:18:16,833 → 00:18:17,500` **Jake:** 还是啥呀 / Or what?
- `00:18:17,500 → 00:18:18,433` **Alice:** 海澜之家 / Hailan Home.
- `00:18:18,466 → 00:18:21,600` **Alice:** 我就记得一个h一个l他们家的门面 / I just remember an H and an L, their storefront.
- `00:18:19,300 → 00:18:21,066` **Jake:** 他logo长啥样不知道哇 / You don't know what their logo looks like?
- `00:18:22,400 → 00:18:23,766` **Lucia:** 啊好像是 / Ah, I think so.
- `00:18:25,366 → 00:18:28,000` **Lucia:** 不了解因为因为咱们平常也不买男装 / Not sure, because we usually don't buy men's clothes.
- `00:18:26,266 → 00:18:29,566` **Alice:** 我感觉里面就是没有我感觉都一样 / I feel like inside, everything looks the same.
- `00:18:29,200 → 00:18:31,066` **Jake:** 对我也不买男装 / Yeah, I don't buy men's clothes either.
- `00:18:31,500 → 00:18:32,900` **Tasha:** 那他穿什么 / Then what does he wear?
- `00:18:32,100 → 00:18:33,133` **Alice:** 怪怪的 / That's weird.
- `00:18:32,900 → 00:18:34,233` **Jake:** 就这个哈哈哈哈 / Just this, hahaha.
- `00:18:35,733 → 00:18:38,466` **Tasha:** 你看喜茶标志很明显 / Look, the HeyTea logo is very obvious.
- `00:18:38,300 → 00:18:40,233` **Jake:** 这个确实哎 / Yeah, that's true.
- `00:18:38,466 → 00:18:39,566` **Katrina:** 对 / Right.
- `00:18:40,233 → 00:18:43,000` **Lucia:** 那可能是上面确实有个标志只是没找到 / Maybe there's a logo up there, we just didn't find it.
- `00:18:45,233 → 00:18:47,166` **Tasha:** 还测吗哈哈 / Are we still testing? Haha.
- `00:18:47,233 → 00:18:48,666` **Alice:** 这有一股有毒的味道 / It smells kind of toxic here.
- `00:18:48,666 → 00:18:50,700` **Alice:** 感觉在这闻久了要晕倒 / I feel like I'll faint if I stay here long enough.
- `00:18:53,600 → 00:18:54,933` **Lucia:** 有种没装修完的 / Feels like it's not fully renovated.
- `00:18:54,966 → 00:18:57,000` **Tasha:** 是两位男生推购物车吗 / Are the two guys pushing shopping carts?
- `00:18:57,000 → 00:18:57,600` **Jake:** 行啊 / Sure.
- `00:18:57,566 → 00:18:58,766` **Tasha:** 推两辆是吗 / Pushing two carts?
- `00:18:58,766 → 00:19:00,233` **Jake:** 行啊没问题 / Sure, no problem.
- `00:19:00,966 → 00:19:04,266` **Tasha:** 一一边采购酒一边采购食材是吗 / Are we buying both the alcohol and the groceries at the same time?
