- `00:25:01,333 → 00:25:01,766` **Jake:** 啊就自己吃 / Oh, just eat it ourselves.
- `00:25:04,233 → 00:25:04,933` **Jake:** 好让他切吧 / Alright, let them cut it.
- `00:25:07,433 → 00:25:09,733` **Jake:** 给他拍一个特写一个切的 / Take a close-up shot of him cutting it.
- `00:25:10,266 → 00:25:12,733` **Tasha:** 你看他可以呼叫店员帮你切 / You see, you can call the staff to help you cut it.
- `00:25:12,766 → 00:25:13,933` **Jake:** 好切 / Alright, cut it.
- `00:25:17,166 → 00:25:20,333` **Tasha:** 挑的话挑那种刺比较尖的 / If you're picking, choose the one with sharper thorns.
- `00:25:21,300 → 00:25:23,266` **Jake:** 挑比较大格子比较大 / Pick the one with bigger grids.
- `00:25:23,266 → 00:25:24,066` **Jake:** 这个我会 / I can do this.
- `00:25:24,066 → 00:25:25,166` **Jake:** 这个我在新加坡 / I did this in Singapore.
- `00:25:25,466 → 00:25:26,766` **Jake:** 这个我在新加坡切过 / I cut this in Singapore.
- `00:25:26,766 → 00:25:28,300` **Tasha:** 你觉得这两个哪个好 / Which one do you think is better?
- `00:25:28,933 → 00:25:31,300` **Jake:** 这个我看看 / Let me have a look.
- `00:25:31,833 → 00:25:32,766` **Alice:** 还有点扎呢 / It's still a bit prickly.
- `00:25:34,400 → 00:25:35,400` **Alice:** 这个是不是更熟 / Is this one more ripe?
- `00:25:34,833 → 00:25:35,666` **Jake:** 这个吧 / This one.
- `00:25:35,400 → 00:25:36,700` **Alice:** 我也不知道 / I don't know either.
- `00:25:36,700 → 00:25:38,333` **Alice:** 绿不拉几差不多吧 / It's pretty green, isn't it?
- `00:25:38,333 → 00:25:39,466` **Alice:** 差不多这个吧 / Maybe this one.
- `00:25:40,500 → 00:25:42,233` **Jake:** 看谁胖一点哇 / Let's see who's fatter.
- `00:25:44,833 → 00:25:46,300` **Jake:** 这个吧就这个吧 / This one, let's go with this one.
- `00:25:47,466 → 00:25:48,266` **Alice:** 您好 / Hello.
- `00:25:49,133 → 00:25:49,933` **Jake:** 您好 / Hello.
- `00:25:51,066 → 00:25:53,400` **Jake:** 切，谢谢您 / Cut it, thank you.
- `00:25:58,166 → 00:25:59,366` **Jake:** 这就下班了 / Done for the day.
- `00:25:59,466 → 00:26:00,300` **Jake:** 这才几点 / What time is it?
- `00:26:01,033 → 00:26:03,166` **Jake:** 那我们现在谁跟我说来着 / Who was talking to me just now?
- `00:26:09,433 → 00:26:10,533` **Tasha:** 那他什么时候开始切 / When will he start cutting?
- `00:26:20,500 → 00:26:22,633` **Tasha:** 行吧那我们买个这个吧 / Alright, let's buy this one.
- `00:26:23,600 → 00:26:24,633` **Jake:** 行吧 / Okay.
- `00:26:26,500 → 00:26:27,500` **Tasha:** 你挑一个 / You pick one.
- `00:26:27,966 → 00:26:31,033` **Jake:** 行吧那就大家信他一回 / Alright, let's trust him this time.
- `00:26:31,033 → 00:26:33,233` **Tasha:** 买一个这个才8块钱 / Buying this one only costs 8 yuan.
- `00:26:33,900 → 00:26:37,233` **Tasha:** 我觉得小台芒是芒果里面最甜的 / I think mini Thai mangoes are the sweetest.
- `00:26:37,233 → 00:26:37,900` **Jake:** 好 / Okay.
- `00:26:40,566 → 00:26:41,366` **Tasha:** 你挑挑哪个 / You pick one.
- `00:26:41,366 → 00:26:43,266` **Tasha:** 是6个的啊 / Is it the one with 6?
- `00:26:43,266 → 00:26:46,500` **Tasha:** 我刚看到这个是6个但他这有霉点 / I just saw this one has 6, but it has mold spots.
- `00:26:46,500 → 00:26:47,333` **Tasha:** 所以要挑一个另一个6个的 / So, pick another one with 6.
- `00:26:48,566 → 00:26:49,700` **Alice:** 小台芒的吗 / The mini Thai mangoes?
- `00:26:48,566 → 00:26:51,533` **Tasha:** 发挥你的眼睛对 / Use your eyes, yes.
- `00:26:51,533 → 00:26:53,133` **Alice:** 小台芒比较甜比较软 / Mini Thai mangoes are sweeter and softer.
- `00:26:53,133 → 00:26:54,200` **Tasha:** 对小台芒 / Yes, mini Thai mangoes.
- `00:26:54,200 → 00:26:54,700` **Jake:** 这是6个吗 / Are there 6?
- `00:27:03,200 → 00:27:04,866` **Tasha:** OK看看水果就买齐了吧 / OK, let's see if we have all the fruits now.
- `00:27:06,166 → 00:27:07,300` **Tasha:** 下一步是 / The next step is
- `00:27:09,366 → 00:27:11,633` **Tasha:** 牛奶无菌蛋牛奶 / Milk, pasteurized egg milk
- `00:27:21,433 → 00:27:23,333` **Alice:** 这是你外送的 / This is your delivery
- `00:27:23,533 → 00:27:24,733` **Jake:** 对我在拍他 / Yep, I'm filming him
- `00:27:28,700 → 00:27:29,500` **Jake:** 给个特写 / Give a close-up
- `00:27:35,700 → 00:27:36,666` **Alice:** 这款还挺好喝的 / This one's pretty good
- `00:27:36,766 → 00:27:38,266` **Tasha:** 我我也觉着 / I, I think so too
- `00:27:42,633 → 00:27:44,066` **Tasha:** 椰子水拿一瓶吧 / Let's grab a bottle of coconut water
- `00:27:44,466 → 00:27:45,766` **Tasha:** 但在哪呢 / But where is it?
- `00:27:45,266 → 00:27:47,133` **Alice:** 椰子水买那个吧 / Let's buy that coconut water
- `00:27:46,933 → 00:27:49,666` **Tasha:** 这个哎这个 / This one, hey this one
- `00:27:49,666 → 00:27:50,900` **Tasha:** 这个贵了一点 / This one's a bit expensive
- `00:27:52,900 → 00:27:54,333` **Alice:** 这个就是那个便宜的 / This one is the cheaper one
- `00:27:54,633 → 00:27:55,166` **Tasha:** 这个是便 / This is chea...
- `00:27:55,166 → 00:27:56,766` **Tasha:** 是便宜的啊 / Oh, it's cheap
- `00:27:56,766 → 00:27:57,700` **Tasha:** 他们都是一升 / They're all one liter
- `00:27:57,700 → 00:27:58,766` **Alice:** 这怎么是8块9了 / How is this 8.9 yuan?
- `00:27:58,766 → 00:28:00,033` **Tasha:** 对他们都是一升 / Yeah, they're all one liter
- `00:28:00,033 → 00:28:01,300` **Tasha:** 但他只要8块9 / But this one is only 8.9 yuan
- `00:28:01,300 → 00:28:02,366` **Lucia:** 我猜的 / I'm just guessing
- `00:28:03,466 → 00:28:04,033` **Alice:** 果汁哦 / Juice, oh
- `00:28:04,066 → 00:28:06,033` **Alice:** 果汁是他们买 / They bought the juice
- `00:28:06,033 → 00:28:06,900` **Tasha:** 还有东西吗 / Anything else?
- `00:28:07,466 → 00:28:09,366` **Tasha:** 牛奶无菌蛋 / Milk, pasteurized eggs
- `00:28:09,033 → 00:28:10,100` **Alice:** 牛奶买了吗 / Did you buy the milk?
- `00:28:10,100 → 00:28:11,600` **Tasha:** 牛奶买了 / Yeah, bought the milk
- `00:28:10,100 → 00:28:11,866` **Alice:** 两盒够不够 / Are two cartons enough?
- `00:28:11,900 → 00:28:12,733` **Tasha:** 够了吧 / Should be enough
- `00:28:14,800 → 00:28:16,200` **Alice:** 无菌蛋 / Pasteurized eggs
- `00:28:14,800 → 00:28:16,233` **Tasha:** 鸡蛋鸡蛋 / Eggs, eggs
- `00:28:17,800 → 00:28:19,033` **Jake:** 淡奶油在什么地方 / Where's the heavy cream?
- `00:28:18,666 → 00:28:20,066` **Tasha:** 淡奶油在 / Heavy cream is...
- `00:28:19,666 → 00:28:20,433` **Jake:** 在很里边吗 / Is it deep inside?
- `00:28:21,766 → 00:28:23,566` **Alice:** 奶油应该在酸奶这边 / The cream should be near the yogurt
- `00:28:23,566 → 00:28:24,333` **Alice:** 是差不多的地吧 / It's around the same area
- `00:28:24,300 → 00:28:27,500` **Tasha:** 我觉得差不多在冰柜这里 / I think it's probably in the freezer section
- `00:28:31,033 → 00:28:32,100` **Jake:** 要不问问他嘞 / How about we ask him?
- `00:28:39,766 → 00:28:42,600` **Jake:** 你这有淡奶油卖吗 / Do you sell heavy cream here?
- `00:28:45,466 → 00:28:47,833` **Jake:** 您家哦还不是一家 / Is it your store? Oh, not your store
- `00:28:49,400 → 00:28:50,500` **Jake:** 哈喽 / Hello
- `00:28:50,333 → 00:28:51,100` **Lucia:** 哈喽 / Hello
- `00:28:54,333 → 00:28:55,100` **Shure:** 你平时喝酒吗 / Do you usually drink alcohol?
- `00:28:58,366 → 00:28:59,900` **Lucia:** 你问我们俩的谁 / Who are you asking, the two of us?
- `00:29:00,100 → 00:29:00,900` **Lucia:** 里面的谁 / Which one of us?
- `00:29:00,100 → 00:29:01,066` **Shure:** 你们俩 / Both of you
- `00:29:01,833 → 00:29:03,266` **Jake:** 我来瞅瞅你们买的啥 / Let me see what you guys bought
- `00:29:03,266 → 00:29:06,133` **Jake:** 你们看起来进度一般了 / You guys seem to be making average progress
- `00:29:06,866 → 00:29:07,533` **Katrina:** 嗯没找到饮料 / Yeah, we couldn't find the drinks
- `00:29:07,533 → 00:29:09,300` **Shure:** 是要我们大买特买那个意思吗 / Are you saying we should buy a lot?
- `00:29:09,300 → 00:29:10,033` **Lucia:** 他买了椰子水 / He bought coconut water
- `00:29:10,033 → 00:29:11,600` **Lucia:** 刚好我们不用买了啊 / So we don't need to buy any
- `00:29:11,600 → 00:29:12,633` **Katrina:** 哦他们买了椰子水 / Oh, they bought coconut water
- `00:29:12,666 → 00:29:15,233` **Katrina:** 是要用椰奶还是椰子水啊 / Do we need coconut milk or coconut water?
- `00:29:15,233 → 00:29:16,733` **Lucia:** 呃其实最好是椰奶 / Uh, actually, coconut milk would be better
- `00:29:16,733 → 00:29:19,033` **Lucia:** 但是咱们也没有那么那个啥嘛 / But we don't really have that
- `00:29:19,466 → 00:29:21,500` **Shure:** 椰奶和椰子水完全两个味啊 / Coconut milk and coconut water taste completely different
- `00:29:23,533 → 00:29:28,000` **Tasha:** 我暂时还不在你 / I'm not with you right now
- `00:29:28,300 → 00:29:29,366` **Tasha:** 要不明天再 / How about tomorrow?
- `00:29:29,666 → 00:29:30,600` **Tasha:** 我明天再去拿行吗 / Can I go get it tomorrow?
- `00:29:32,600 → 00:29:34,000` **Tasha:** 好谢谢 / Okay, thank you
- `00:29:51,900 → 00:29:52,100` **Alice:** 买两个 / Buy two
- `00:29:54,533 → 00:29:56,400` **Tasha:** 你看网上才卖12块五 / Look, it's only 12.50 online
- `00:29:58,466 → 00:29:59,366` **Tasha:** 是这个 / This one
- `00:29:59,400 → 00:30:00,666` **Jake:** 淡奶油找着了吗 / Did you find the whipping cream?
- `00:30:01,466 → 00:30:02,733` **Tasha:** 好吧它是安佳 / Okay, it's Anchor
- `00:30:02,100 → 00:30:03,466` **Jake:** 就这个 / This one
- `00:30:02,766 → 00:30:03,733` **Tasha:** 它是雀巢 / It's Nestlé
- `00:30:04,166 → 00:30:05,466` **Alice:** 安佳会比较贵 / Anchor is more expensive
- `00:30:05,766 → 00:30:07,100` **Tasha:** 贵了10块钱一瓶 / It's 10 yuan more per bottle
- `00:30:19,800 → 00:30:26,033` **Jake:** 淡奶油他在一伙那 我们觉得这不够 / The whipping cream is with the group, we think it's not enough
- `00:30:26,466 → 00:30:28,433` **Jake:** 太少了还有多的吧 / It's too little, is there more?
- `00:30:28,833 → 00:30:29,766` **Jake:** 这种类太少了 / This kind is too little
- `00:30:31,900 → 00:30:33,500` **Jake:** 那行吧谢谢您 / Alright, thanks
- `00:30:43,266 → 00:30:46,133` **Jake:** 这还能小时达吗还是明天达 / Can this be delivered within the hour or by tomorrow?
- `00:30:46,733 → 00:30:47,866` **Tasha:** 是啊我要看看嘛 / Yeah, let me check
- `00:30:55,533 → 00:30:57,566` **Jake:** 你这可以选的诶 / You can choose this
- `00:31:00,100 → 00:31:01,366` **Tasha:** 一件 / Just one
- `00:31:45,533 → 00:31:46,366` **Jake:** 我帮你拿 / I'll help you carry it
- `00:31:50,266 → 00:31:50,966` **Jake:** 不用谢 / No problem
- `00:31:54,533 → 00:31:56,100` **Alice:** 这好可爱这是什么 / This is so cute, what is it?
- `00:31:54,900 → 00:31:56,733` **Lucia:** 如果少了可能还要再买 / If it's not enough, we might need to buy more.
- `00:31:57,466 → 00:31:58,466` **Jake:** 那就网上买 / Then just buy it online.
- `00:31:59,900 → 00:32:00,866` **Lucia:** 可能吧 / Maybe.
- `00:32:00,866 → 00:32:02,600` **Jake:** 行啊可以网上下个单呗 / Sure, we can place an order online.
- `00:32:02,333 → 00:32:03,066` **Jake:** 不要紧啊 / No problem.
- `00:32:03,066 → 00:32:03,266` **Lucia:** 对 / Right.
- `00:32:03,066 → 00:32:04,833` **Jake:** 然后下单 / Then place an order.
- `00:32:03,266 → 00:32:07,066` **Lucia:** 如果22号的那天的话可以先在网上买 / If it's on the 22nd, we can buy it online first.
- `00:32:08,600 → 00:32:10,266` **Jake:** 22号那一天 / On the 22nd.
- `00:32:09,266 → 00:32:11,500` **Tasha:** 不是说那天要做蛋糕吗 / Didn't you say we were going to bake a cake that day?
- `00:32:16,733 → 00:32:17,400` **Tasha:** 谢谢 / Thanks.
- `00:32:17,400 → 00:32:19,233` **Jake:** 厉害你这个 / You're amazing.
- `00:32:19,466 → 00:32:21,700` **Jake:** 这戴个帽子就把头发挡住了 / Just put on a hat and it hides your hair.
- `00:32:25,266 → 00:32:26,133` **Tasha:** 然后要什么来着 / What else do we need?
- `00:32:26,100 → 00:32:27,266` **Alice:** 对无菌蛋 / Sterilized eggs.
- `00:32:30,266 → 00:32:30,733` **Tasha:** 鸡蛋 / Eggs.
- `00:32:38,766 → 00:32:39,833` **Tasha:** 糖盐 / Sugar and salt.
- `00:32:40,300 → 00:32:44,100` **Tasha:** 奶粉咖啡冻奶油薄荷保鲜膜 / Milk powder, coffee, whipped cream, mint, cling film.
- `00:32:44,100 → 00:32:45,666` **Tasha:** 蛋挞皮草莓 / Tart crusts, strawberries.
- `00:32:46,666 → 00:32:48,233` **Tasha:** 美文纸为什么也是我们买 / Why do we have to buy the paper too?
- `00:32:48,333 → 00:32:50,300` **Tasha:** 金针菇3-4个 / 3-4 enoki mushrooms.
- `00:32:50,300 → 00:32:51,466` **Tasha:** 松花蛋娃娃菜 / Century eggs, baby bok choy.
- `00:32:52,766 → 00:32:53,466` **Tasha:** 记住了吗 / Got it?
- `00:32:53,466 → 00:32:54,033` **Tasha:** 走吧 / Let's go.
- `00:32:54,033 → 00:32:55,166` **Jake:** 娃娃菜走 / Baby bok choy, let's go.
- `00:32:58,266 → 00:32:59,733` **Tasha:** 诶鸡蛋 / Hey, eggs.
- `00:33:04,833 → 00:33:07,033` **Tasha:** 买鸡蛋送松花蛋 / Buy eggs and get century eggs for free.
- `00:33:07,466 → 00:33:08,000` **Tasha:** 刚好哎 / Perfect.
- `00:33:08,033 → 00:33:10,033` **Jake:** 诶刚好啊 / Perfect timing.
- `00:33:12,666 → 00:33:13,666` **Alice:** 鲜鸡蛋吧 / Fresh eggs?
- `00:33:14,066 → 00:33:16,466` **Tasha:** 啊鲜鸡蛋也可以吧 / Yeah, fresh eggs are fine too.
- `00:33:16,733 → 00:33:18,266` **Jake:** 你们家离高邮远吗 / Is your home far from Gaoyou?
- `00:33:19,466 → 00:33:20,600` **Jake:** 高邮 / Gaoyou.
- `00:33:20,900 → 00:33:22,733` **Jake:** 啊你们家离那远 / Is your home far from there?
- `00:33:22,733 → 00:33:24,333` **Alice:** 高邮鸭蛋哦 / Gaoyou salted duck eggs.
- `00:33:24,333 → 00:33:25,066` **Alice:** 咸鸭蛋 / Salted duck eggs.
- `00:33:24,333 → 00:33:25,700` **Tasha:** 不远 / Not far.
- `00:33:25,666 → 00:33:27,466` **Tasha:** 你们家也不远啊 / Your home isn't far either.
- `00:33:27,666 → 00:33:29,233` **Jake:** 啊对确实 / Yeah, that's true.
- `00:33:30,100 → 00:33:33,000` **Alice:** 哦那个是可生食的哦 / Oh, that one can be eaten raw.
- `00:33:33,033 → 00:33:36,533` **Tasha:** 这呢我觉得可生食的买一点就行了 / I think it's enough to buy a little of the edible one.
- `00:33:36,533 → 00:33:38,400` **Tasha:** 然后再买一盒这个也可以了 / And then we can buy a box of this too.
- `00:33:38,466 → 00:33:39,000` **Alice:** 行啊 / Sure.
- `00:33:40,900 → 00:33:42,233` **Tasha:** 这个就买 / Let's buy this.
- `00:33:42,266 → 00:33:45,166` **Tasha:** 呃做那个提拉米苏 / Uh, to make Tiramisu.
- `00:33:44,600 → 00:33:46,100` **Alice:** 提拉米苏用 / For the Tiramisu?
- `00:33:45,400 → 00:33:47,233` **Tasha:** 对然后别的都可以用 / Yes, and everything else can be used.
- `00:33:47,366 → 00:33:49,900` **Tasha:** 用非无菌的蛋 / Use non-sterilized eggs.
- `00:33:50,333 → 00:33:50,766` **Jake:** 好 / Okay.
- `00:33:50,333 → 00:33:52,066` **Alice:** 熟的就行 / Cooked ones are fine.
- `00:33:52,300 → 00:33:54,100` **Jake:** 无菌蛋无菌蛋是什么意思 / What's a sterilized egg?
- `00:33:54,100 → 00:33:55,866` **Tasha:** 无菌蛋就是你现在直接可以吃下去 / A sterilized egg is one you can eat right away.
- `00:33:55,900 → 00:33:57,466` **Jake:** 就是他为什么叫无菌蛋 / Why is it called a sterilized egg?
- `00:33:57,466 → 00:33:58,166` **Tasha:** 因为有 / Because it...
- `00:33:58,166 → 00:34:00,333` **Tasha:** 因为他就是不会有细菌吧 / It probably doesn't have bacteria.
- `00:34:00,333 → 00:34:01,233` **Tasha:** 可能 / Maybe.
- `00:34:01,233 → 00:34:03,333` **Alice:** 是什么沙门氏菌什么的 / Like Salmonella or something.
- `00:34:03,733 → 00:34:05,566` **Jake:** 他就是这个鸡比较牛逼 / Is it because the chicken is amazing?
- `00:34:05,566 → 00:34:06,366` **Jake:** 还是 / Or...
- `00:34:06,666 → 00:34:08,866` **Alice:** 就是他的那个饲养流程比较那啥 / It's probably because of the strict feeding process.
- `00:34:08,900 → 00:34:09,666` **Alice:** 控制比较严 / Strict control.
- `00:34:09,666 → 00:34:13,633` **Jake:** 刚生下来然后就被保存起来了之类的 / As soon as they're born, they're preserved or something.
- `00:34:13,666 → 00:34:14,900` **Alice:** 具体的不是很清楚 / I'm not very sure about the details.
- `00:34:14,900 → 00:34:16,433` **Jake:** OK OK行 / OK, OK, got it.
- `00:34:16,466 → 00:34:19,166` **Tasha:** 无菌蛋牛奶白糖盐 / Sterilized eggs, milk, sugar, salt.
- `00:34:20,466 → 00:34:21,600` **Alice:** 调味料那边 / Over there with the seasonings.
- `00:34:23,766 → 00:34:25,100` **Alice:** 哦那边是蔬菜 / Oh, the vegetables are over there.
- `00:34:27,166 → 00:34:28,833` **Alice:** 买点菌菇类的 / Let's buy some mushrooms.
- `00:34:43,766 → 00:34:44,866` **Jake:** 买 买 / Buy, buy.
- `00:34:44,900 → 00:34:46,066` **Alice:** 为什么娃娃菜这么贵 / Why is napa cabbage so expensive?
- `00:34:45,300 → 00:34:46,066` **Jake:** 你要买吗 / Do you want to buy it?
- `00:34:48,233 → 00:34:49,733` **Jake:** 高山鲜 / It's fresh from the mountains.
- `00:34:48,900 → 00:34:49,933` **Alice:** 没有普通的吗 / Don't they have regular ones?
- `00:34:50,266 → 00:34:50,900` **Jake:** 没有 / No.
- `00:34:54,333 → 00:34:55,666` **Tasha:** 老板你有想吃的吗 / Boss, do you have anything you want to eat?
- `00:34:57,100 → 00:34:58,633` **Alice:** 这是大白菜啊 / This is napa cabbage, right?
- `00:35:02,233 → 00:35:06,000` **Jake:** 我把我想吃的人都招过来了 会做吗 / I gathered everyone I wanted to eat with. Can you cook it?
- `00:35:06,400 → 00:35:08,333` **Tasha:** 就买这个娃娃菜买了吗 / Did you buy the napa cabbage?
- `00:35:06,600 → 00:35:09,466` **Jake:** 会做好吃的菜的人都招过来了  没有没有 / I gathered people who can cook well. No, no.
- `00:35:07,100 → 00:35:08,500` **Jake:** 这玩意他买了吗 / Did he buy this thing?
- `00:35:08,733 → 00:35:09,966` **Alice:** 没有但是我感觉好贵啊他 / No, but I feel like it's so expensive.
- `00:35:12,900 → 00:35:17,466` **Tasha:** 但是看着也挺好嫩的怎么这么贵去我家地里摘吧 / But it looks pretty fresh, why is it so expensive? Let's just pick some from my garden.
- `00:35:17,333 → 00:35:19,766` **Alice:** 主要是他一煮他就很小啦 / The main thing is, once you cook it, it shrinks a lot.
- `00:35:24,100 → 00:35:25,433` **Tasha:** 那拿点上海青 / Then get some Shanghai Bok Choy.
- `00:35:25,466 → 00:35:27,666` **Tasha:** 上海青才一块钱哈哈 / Shanghai Bok Choy is just one yuan, haha.
- `00:35:28,966 → 00:35:30,100` **Alice:** 但是他没那个味 / But it doesn't have that flavor.
- `00:35:31,733 → 00:35:32,866` **Alice:** 让我找一下 / Let me look for it.
- `00:35:35,266 → 00:35:36,500` **Alice:** 要不然买成这个 / Or we could buy this.
- `00:35:36,766 → 00:35:39,300` **Alice:** 我们我们可以我们把帮 / We, we can, we can help...
- `00:35:39,566 → 00:35:40,666` **Jake:** 为什么这个不行 / Why can't we use this?
- `00:35:41,100 → 00:35:42,266` **Alice:** 因为那个比较贵 / Because that one is more expensive.
- `00:35:45,600 → 00:35:48,100` **Jake:** 他为什么贵啊 / Why is it expensive?
- `00:35:48,100 → 00:35:49,233` **Jake:** 这个多少钱 / How much is this?
- `00:35:49,766 → 00:35:51,133` **Alice:** 白菜能贵到哪 / How expensive can cabbage be?
- `00:35:51,666 → 00:35:55,833` **Alice:** 一千 1千克的话 / One kilogram is...
- `00:35:55,100 → 00:35:55,833` **Alice:** 白菜 / Cabbage.
- `00:36:01,566 → 00:36:03,666` **Tasha:** 没有价格怎么回事 / Why is there no price tag?
- `00:36:09,966 → 00:36:11,933` **Alice:** 这是杭白菜吗 / Is this Hangzhou Bok Choy?
- `00:36:11,166 → 00:36:13,466` **Tasha:** 你去那扫个码就知道了 / Just scan the code over there and you'll know.
- `00:36:37,466 → 00:36:39,033` **Alice:** 这个5块钱哎 / This is 5 yuan.
- `00:36:39,666 → 00:36:40,866` **Jake:** 那个十一块是吧 / That one is 11 yuan, right?
- `00:36:41,166 → 00:36:42,100` **Alice:** 那个十一 / That one is 11.
- `00:36:42,100 → 00:36:44,333` **Alice:** 并且两份也不太够 / And even two portions aren't enough.
- `00:36:44,533 → 00:36:44,966` **Alice:** 买个这个 / Let's buy this one.
- `00:36:45,900 → 00:36:46,833` **Jake:** 买鸡蛋了 / Did we buy eggs?
- `00:36:46,866 → 00:36:47,966` **Jake:** 买了无菌蛋 / We bought sterilized eggs.
- `00:36:49,833 → 00:36:51,666` **Shure:** 我们买了两个火锅底料 / We bought two hot pot soup bases.
- `00:36:51,966 → 00:36:52,933` **Jake:** 火锅底料 / Hot pot soup base.
- `00:36:53,033 → 00:36:54,566` **Katrina:** 没有桃汁啊 / There's no peach juice?
- `00:36:56,900 → 00:37:00,033` **Jake:** 我们好像我们我们分到了个 / It seems like we got assigned some...
- `00:37:00,033 → 00:37:01,400` **Jake:** 什么奇怪的任务来着 / Weird task.
- `00:37:01,833 → 00:37:02,833` **Alice:** 什么 / What?
- `00:37:02,333 → 00:37:05,900` **Jake:** 就和吃的没关系美纹纸美纹纸是什么东西 / Something unrelated to food. What's masking tape?
- `00:37:02,833 → 00:37:04,566` **Alice:** 美纹纸 / Masking tape?
- `00:37:04,566 → 00:37:06,200` **Alice:** 美纹纸这有吗 / Do they have masking tape here?
- `00:37:04,566 → 00:37:06,466` **Katrina:** 美文纸美文纸 / Masking tape, masking tape.
- `00:37:05,900 → 00:37:08,200` **Lucia:** 美纹纸这这里面能买到吗 / Can we buy masking tape here?
- `00:37:08,333 → 00:37:09,700` **Jake:** 美纹纸是什么东西 / What is masking tape?
- `00:37:10,100 → 00:37:12,100` **Katrina:** 就贴墙上不留痕的那贴纸 / It's the tape you stick on walls that doesn't leave a mark.
- `00:37:12,533 → 00:37:13,566` **Katrina:** 要不直接上网买吧 / How about we just buy it online?
- `00:37:13,266 → 00:37:14,366` **Alice:** 再买两个 / Let's buy two more.
- `00:37:13,566 → 00:37:16,066` **Katrina:** 跟那海报一起嘛嗯 / Together with that poster, right?
- `00:37:14,033 → 00:37:15,600` **Katrina:** 跟那个美纹纸很便宜 / And that masking tape is pretty cheap.
- `00:37:14,333 → 00:37:15,333` **Jake:** 好 / Okay.
- `00:37:15,833 → 00:37:19,733` **Tasha:** 嗯啊我觉得文具店也会有 / Hmm, I think the stationery store might have it too.
- `00:37:17,466 → 00:37:20,700` **Katrina:** 冰块模具还没买 / We haven't bought the ice cube mold yet.
- `00:37:27,100 → 00:37:29,333` **Katrina:** 盒马是不是主营饮食啊 / Is Hema mainly for food and drinks?
- `00:37:27,400 → 00:37:29,266` **Alice:** 要不然我们买大白菜吧 / Or we could buy napa cabbage.
- `00:37:45,533 → 00:37:46,400` **Jake:** 跳舞了 / Time to dance.
- `00:37:49,066 → 00:37:50,366` **Jake:** 踩人舞 / Step dance.
- `00:37:56,900 → 00:37:58,166` **Alice:** 槐花你吃过吗 / Have you ever eaten locust flower?
- `00:37:58,533 → 00:37:59,733` **Tasha:** 吃春菜 / Eating spring vegetables.
- `00:37:59,766 → 00:38:01,300` **Katrina:** 直接买柠檬也行 / We could just buy lemons.
- `00:38:01,166 → 00:38:02,100` **Alice:** 对槐花你吃过吗 / Yeah, have you tried locust flower?
- `00:38:02,100 → 00:38:02,533` **Tasha:** 没有 / No.
- `00:38:02,566 → 00:38:04,533` **Alice:** 这可以炒可以做包子 / You can stir-fry it or make buns with it.
- `00:38:05,400 → 00:38:07,766` **Alice:** 很好吃甜甜的 / It's really tasty, sweet.
- `00:38:06,900 → 00:38:07,800` **Jake:** 啥意思啊 / What do you mean?
- `00:38:08,333 → 00:38:09,333` **Jake:** 我研究研究 / I'll look into it.
- `00:38:09,333 → 00:38:12,800` **Alice:** 就是这个是一种可以吃的东西 / It's something you can eat.
- `00:38:11,966 → 00:38:13,733` **Tasha:** 金针菇买了吗 / Did you buy enoki mushrooms?
- `00:38:13,300 → 00:38:14,100` **Alice:** 买了买了 / Got them, got them.
- `00:38:14,300 → 00:38:15,533` **Jake:** 这为什么撒了呀 / Why did it spill?
- `00:38:15,600 → 00:38:17,100` **Alice:** 从缝里掉出来了 / It fell through the crack.
- `00:38:16,233 → 00:38:17,733` **Tasha:** 那你吃啊快别浪费 / Then eat it quickly, don't waste it.
- `00:38:17,533 → 00:38:18,766` **Alice:** 不是这不能生吃 / No, you can't eat it raw.
- `00:38:18,600 → 00:38:20,433` **Jake:** 哈哈哈下毒 / Haha, poisoning.
- `00:38:19,966 → 00:38:21,033` **Alice:** 没事不要告诉他 / It's fine, don't tell him.
- `00:38:21,033 → 00:38:21,900` **Alice:** 让他吃也行 / Let him eat it.
- `00:38:22,800 → 00:38:24,033` **Jake:** 那他吃了会怎么样呢 / What will happen if he eats it?
- `00:38:23,866 → 00:38:25,100` **Alice:** 不好吃 / It won't taste good.
- `00:38:25,266 → 00:38:26,533` **Jake:** 主要是不好吃是吧 / The main thing is it won't taste good, right?
- `00:38:26,666 → 00:38:29,100` **Katrina:** 青柠更适合还是柠檬更适合 / Is lime better or lemon better?
- `00:38:27,100 → 00:38:29,933` **Tasha:** 调味料还有胡椒粉味精盐 / Seasonings, we have pepper, MSG, and salt.
- `00:38:29,100 → 00:38:29,566` **Shure:** 都来 / Get them all.
- `00:38:29,600 → 00:38:29,800` **Alice:** 盐 / Salt.
- `00:38:29,966 → 00:38:31,900` **Tasha:** 还有盐哎 / We also have salt.
- `00:38:31,900 → 00:38:33,300` **Alice:** 嗯得找一个专门那啥的 / Yeah, we need to find a specific one.
- `00:38:33,100 → 00:38:35,566` **Jake:** 槐花呀 / Locust flower, huh?
- `00:38:34,833 → 00:38:35,733` **Alice:** 你吃过吗 / Have you tried it before?
- `00:38:35,466 → 00:38:38,400` **Tasha:** 豆芽豆芽可以加进去吗 / Can we add bean sprouts?
- `00:38:37,100 → 00:38:38,400` **Jake:** 我感觉还真吃过 / I think I have tried it.
- `00:38:38,566 → 00:38:40,666` **Alice:** 豆芽不可以没啥味 / Bean sprouts don’t have much flavor.
- `00:38:41,900 → 00:38:42,366` **Tasha:** gogogo / Go, go, go!
- `00:38:49,266 → 00:38:50,433` **Jake:** 葱姜蒜有吗 / Do we have scallions, ginger, and garlic?
- `00:38:51,066 → 00:38:51,966` **Alice:** 葱姜蒜 / Scallions, ginger, and garlic?
- `00:38:51,766 → 00:38:53,433` **Tasha:** 葱姜蒜超市不送 / The supermarket doesn’t deliver those.
- `00:38:54,666 → 00:38:57,833` **Jake:** 啊超市不送 / Ah, the supermarket doesn’t deliver them?
- `00:38:56,766 → 00:38:58,700` **Tasha:** 会送吧怎 / They should, right?
- `00:38:58,066 → 00:38:58,866` **Jake:** 怎么送啊 / How would they deliver?
- `00:38:58,866 → 00:39:00,100` **Tasha:** 要葱姜蒜吗 / Do you want scallions, ginger, and garlic?
- `00:39:01,166 → 00:39:04,266` **Jake:** 怎么就就我们买这些东西他就会送葱姜蒜 / How come if we buy these things, they would deliver scallions, ginger, and garlic?
- `00:39:04,466 → 00:39:05,000` **Tasha:** 是啊 / Yeah.
- `00:39:07,266 → 00:39:08,300` **Katrina:** 这是干什么的 / What’s this for?
- `00:39:08,966 → 00:39:11,466` **Tasha:** 我只是觉得他会送 / I just think they would deliver.
- `00:39:11,666 → 00:39:13,033` **Alice:** 葱怎么卖啊 / How much do scallions cost?
- `00:39:12,666 → 00:39:15,266` **Jake:** 我我我我我 / I, I, I, I...
- `00:39:14,966 → 00:39:15,766` **Alice:** 蒜 / Garlic?
- `00:39:16,466 → 00:39:17,866` **Jake:** surprise闻所未闻 / Surprise, never heard of it.
- `00:39:17,900 → 00:39:19,166` **Jake:** shocked / Shocked.
- `00:39:18,366 → 00:39:19,666` **Tasha:** 真的假的不是 / Really? No way.
- `00:39:19,166 → 00:39:19,833` **Jake:** 不不知道 / I don’t know.
- `00:39:19,666 → 00:39:21,700` **Lucia:** 你在江苏是不是从来没逛过菜市场 / Haven’t you ever been to a vegetable market in Jiangsu?
- `00:39:22,333 → 00:39:24,400` **Jake:** 菜市场逛过这种超市 / I’ve been to vegetable markets like this.
- `00:39:23,466 → 00:39:24,833` **Tasha:** 菜市场送不送 / Do vegetable markets deliver?
- `00:39:24,866 → 00:39:25,833` **Jake:** 菜市场可以送 / Vegetable markets can deliver.
- `00:39:26,233 → 00:39:26,766` **Jake:** 超市不一定 / Supermarkets, not necessarily.
- `00:39:26,833 → 00:39:27,500` **Alice:** 买吗 / Should we buy it?
- `00:39:27,266 → 00:39:29,733` **Tasha:** 买吧 药 药是吗 / Let’s buy it. Medicine, right?
- `00:39:32,533 → 00:39:33,366` **Alice:** 我再想想 / Let me think again.
- `00:39:34,666 → 00:39:36,666` **Alice:** 需要但是可以不用这么多 / We need it, but not that much.
- `00:39:37,966 → 00:39:39,466` **Alice:** 但是几个人啊 / But how many people?
- `00:39:39,466 → 00:39:42,333` **Tasha:** 我觉得蒜和葱就一个就行了呵呵 / I think one garlic and one scallion are enough, haha.
- `00:39:44,033 → 00:39:45,100` **Tasha:** 这要大葱吗 / Do we need big scallions?
- `00:39:45,100 → 00:39:45,900` **Tasha:** 还是小葱 / Or small scallions?
- `00:39:45,900 → 00:39:47,100` **Alice:** 姜可以不要哎 / We can skip the ginger.
- `00:39:47,400 → 00:39:48,433` **Alice:** 蒜是出味的 / Garlic gives flavor.
- `00:39:47,966 → 00:39:48,900` **Jake:** 来让人过 / Let’s get someone over here.
- `00:39:48,400 → 00:39:50,133` **Tasha:** 行那那那就要着吧 / Alright, let’s get it.
- `00:39:49,100 → 00:39:50,133` **Alice:** 哦不好意思 / Oh, sorry.
- `00:39:50,833 → 00:39:51,666` **Alice:** 看看加 / Let me check.
- `00:39:52,766 → 00:39:54,433` **Alice:** 呃在哪呢 / Uh, where is it?
- `00:39:55,866 → 00:39:59,833` **Alice:** 蒜葱葱也用不了那么多呀 / We don't need that much garlic and green onions.
- `00:39:57,466 → 00:39:59,333` **Tasha:** 哎那明天有火锅的话 / Hey, if we are having hot pot tomorrow...
- `00:39:59,366 → 00:40:01,666` **Tasha:** 要不要买火锅底料和肉菜啊 / Should we buy hot pot base and meat?
- `00:40:00,733 → 00:40:02,466` **Jake:** 他们买了底料 / They bought the base.
- `00:40:02,666 → 00:40:04,033` **Jake:** 肉他们好像没买 / But I think they didn't buy meat.
- `00:40:04,266 → 00:40:05,366` **Tasha:** 那要不要买肉 / Should we buy meat?
- `00:40:05,233 → 00:40:06,100` **Jake:** 可以买肉 / Yeah, we can buy meat.
- `00:40:07,100 → 00:40:07,900` **Jake:** 回头对一下 / Let's check later.
- `00:40:07,900 → 00:40:09,700` **Jake:** 可以先 / We can first...
- `00:40:10,166 → 00:40:12,533` **Alice:** 那个您好我们如果买的话会送葱姜蒜 / Hi, if we buy it, will you give us garlic, ginger, and green onions?
- `00:40:11,533 → 00:40:12,733` **Katrina:** 为什么呢 / Why?
- `00:40:12,533 → 00:40:13,866` **Alice:** 送多少呢大概 / How much will you give approximately?
- `00:40:16,166 → 00:40:17,400` **Alice:** 就是消费 / Just for spending...
- `00:40:17,400 → 00:40:19,866` **Alice:** 不会送吗不会送吗 / Won't you give us? Won't you give us?
- `00:40:22,033 → 00:40:25,466` **Tasha:** 有的时候会员满9.9会送啊 / Sometimes members get it for free when they spend 9.9.
- `00:40:26,100 → 00:40:27,466` **Alice:** 没有没有被送过哎 / No, I've never gotten it for free.
- `00:40:27,466 → 00:40:28,466` **Jake:** 啊什么意思啊 / What do you mean?
- `00:40:29,300 → 00:40:32,600` **Jake:** 我们还我们还满不了9.9 / We haven't even spent 9.9 yet.
- `00:40:32,600 → 00:40:34,866` **Tasha:** 呃主要是我们没有会员啊 / Uh, mainly because we are not members.
- `00:40:35,366 → 00:40:36,900` **Alice:** 我好像有会员 / I think I am a member.
- `00:40:37,266 → 00:40:40,766` **Tasha:** 你有吗不知道这今天会不会送 / Are you? Not sure if they'll give it today.
- `00:40:43,300 → 00:40:44,266` **Tasha:** 有被送过 / I have gotten it before.
- `00:40:44,766 → 00:40:46,200` **Alice:** 有被送过但不一定是吧 / I've gotten it before, but not always.
- `00:40:45,966 → 00:40:47,833` **Tasha:** 他好像每天 / It seems like they do it daily.
- `00:40:46,266 → 00:40:48,866` **Alice:** 但是这也用不了这么多 / But we don't need that much.
- `00:40:48,866 → 00:40:52,166` **Tasha:** 嗯没关系做火锅还要用的 / Hmm, it's okay, we can use it for the hot pot.
- `00:40:52,166 → 00:40:52,966` **Tasha:** 拿着吧 / Just take it.
- `00:40:52,166 → 00:40:53,666` **Alice:** 确实拿吧 / Sure, let's take it.
- `00:40:54,466 → 00:40:55,266` **Jake:** 好 / Alright.
- `00:40:59,233 → 00:41:00,333` **Alice:** 你吃板栗仁吗 / Do you eat chestnuts?
- `00:41:00,333 → 00:41:01,333` **Alice:** 可以放进去 / We can add them in.
- `00:41:01,666 → 00:41:03,166` **Tasha:** 呃都可以 / Uh, anything is fine.
- `00:41:04,100 → 00:41:05,633` **Alice:** 我怕他弄不熟 / I'm afraid they might not cook through.
- `00:41:05,900 → 00:41:06,600` **Tasha:** 那算了吧 / Then forget it.
- `00:41:07,400 → 00:41:08,800` **Alice:** 因为这个比较难煮 / Because it's hard to cook them.
- `00:41:57,866 → 00:41:59,233` **Jake:** 哎呀撞着了 / Oops, bumped into it.
- `00:41:59,566 → 00:42:01,133` **Lucia:** 哎刚好碰到你们 / Oh, just ran into you guys.
- `00:42:01,100 → 00:42:02,533` **Katrina:** 买完了 / Done shopping.
- `00:42:02,300 → 00:42:03,100` **Jake:** 你们 / You guys...
- `00:42:02,600 → 00:42:03,233` **Katrina:** 有两个没有 / There are two missing.
- `00:42:03,266 → 00:42:05,166` **Katrina:** 没有有两 / We don't have two.
- `00:42:05,166 → 00:42:06,033` **Alice:** 调料 / Seasonings.
- `00:42:06,400 → 00:42:07,100` **Jake:** 买肉了吗 / Did you buy the meat?
- `00:42:07,233 → 00:42:08,733` **Jake:** 火锅什么的他们要 / They want hot pot stuff.
- `00:42:08,666 → 00:42:10,666` **Katrina:** 那去去买火锅的食材是吧 / So, we're buying hot pot ingredients, right?
- `00:42:10,266 → 00:42:11,266` **Jake:** 那你们去吧 / Yeah, you guys go.
- `00:42:10,666 → 00:42:11,866` **Alice:** 我们买葱蒜了 / We bought green onions and garlic.
- `00:42:11,900 → 00:42:12,533` **Katrina:** 啊葱蒜 / Ah, green onions and garlic.
- `00:42:12,533 → 00:42:14,900` **Katrina:** 那我们去买火锅食材 / Then we'll go buy hot pot ingredients.
- `00:42:13,800 → 00:42:15,066` **Jake:** 肉在那去吧 / The meat is over there, go ahead.
- `00:42:15,366 → 00:42:15,733` **Shure:** 好 / Okay.
- `00:42:18,100 → 00:42:19,666` **Shure:** 烤肉的也要买一下 / We also need to buy barbecue stuff.
- `00:42:18,966 → 00:42:21,300` **Tasha:** 那我们的进度好慢啊他们都买完了 / We're so slow, they've already finished shopping.
- `00:42:20,333 → 00:42:21,233` **Jake:** 烤肉 / Barbecue.
- `00:42:22,400 → 00:42:24,300` **Jake:** 嗯对 / Yes, right.
- `00:42:23,233 → 00:42:24,533` **Shure:** 等下次好呃 / Next time, uh...
- `00:42:24,533 → 00:42:26,266` **Shure:** 先买火锅的食材 / Let's buy the hot pot ingredients first.
- `00:42:26,266 → 00:42:27,100` **Jake:** 先火锅吧 / Hot pot first.
- `00:42:27,100 → 00:42:28,400` **Jake:** 烤肉是要过一阵的 / Barbecue can wait.
- `00:42:28,300 → 00:42:29,666` **Jake:** 你你没炭呢还 / You don't have charcoal yet.
- `00:42:29,233 → 00:42:30,300` **Jake:** 他们还在买火锅 / They're still buying hot pot stuff.
- `00:42:30,333 → 00:42:32,233` **Shure:** 这些都美团都可以送 / Everything can be delivered by Meituan.
- `00:42:30,400 → 00:42:32,400` **Jake:** 一起嘛 / Together.
- `00:42:31,333 → 00:42:32,766` **Lucia:** 放两天应该没事了 / It should be fine for a couple of days.
- `00:42:32,866 → 00:42:34,966` **Lucia:** 但如果我们烧烤是最后一天烤的话 / But if we barbecue on the last day...
- `00:42:33,600 → 00:42:36,400` **Jake:** 那都美团送的 / Everything can be delivered by Meituan.
- `00:42:35,833 → 00:42:39,500` **Shure:** 但是肉的话可以先买 / But we can buy the meat first.
- `00:42:37,733 → 00:42:38,533` **Jake:** 肉 / Meat.
- `00:42:39,466 → 00:42:41,100` **Jake:** 放得下不小冰箱 / Can it fit in the small fridge?
- `00:42:41,266 → 00:42:43,700` **Katrina:** 就买买火锅的食材 / Just buy the hot pot ingredients.
- `00:42:43,100 → 00:42:44,233` **Shure:** 在这呢火锅的肉 / Here's the hot pot meat.
- `00:42:43,300 → 00:42:45,900` **Jake:** 对对万对 / Yeah, yeah, that's right.
- `00:43:26,366 → 00:43:28,233` **Alice:** 这已经到熟食区了 / We're already in the deli section.
- `00:43:41,333 → 00:43:42,100` **Alice:** 盐 / Salt.
- `00:43:41,333 → 00:43:43,666` **Tasha:** 加碘哪 有人不能吃碘吗 / Iodized, right? Can anyone not eat iodine?
- `00:43:42,800 → 00:43:43,533` **Alice:** 盐来了 / Salt is here.
- `00:43:45,666 → 00:43:46,466` **Alice:** 碘 / Iodine.
- `00:43:47,266 → 00:43:48,733` **Alice:** 没人不能吃碘吧 / No one can't eat iodine, right?
- `00:43:47,833 → 00:43:48,966` **Tasha:** 那 那调温量 / Then, adjust the temperature.
- `00:44:04,400 → 00:44:05,466` **Alice:** 黑胡椒 / Black pepper.
- `00:44:16,033 → 00:44:17,066` **Alice:** 辣椒粉 / Chili powder.
- `00:44:24,333 → 00:44:26,533` **Alice:** 买葱姜蒜还是买葱姜蒜粉呢 / Should we buy green onions, ginger, and garlic or their powders?
- `00:44:28,600 → 00:44:30,233` **Tasha:** 诶葱姜蒜粉好像可以啊 / Hey, the powders might work.
- `00:44:32,100 → 00:44:33,333` **Tasha:** 但是算了吧 / But never mind.
- `00:44:33,366 → 00:44:35,233` **Tasha:** 因为那个不是还要也要用 / Because we still need to use them too.
- `00:44:36,400 → 00:44:37,333` **Alice:** 糖你买了没 / Did you buy the sugar?
- `00:44:37,366 → 00:44:38,666` **Tasha:** 哦对糖在这呢 / Oh right, the sugar is here.
- `00:44:39,566 → 00:44:41,633` **Lucia:** 这个便宜的还是买那个贵的 / Should we buy the cheap one or the expensive one?
- `00:44:41,600 → 00:44:42,866` **Jake:** 有啥差别吗这个糖 / Is there any difference in the sugar?
- `00:44:42,766 → 00:44:45,533` **Tasha:** 你看这5.9 这5.5 / Look, this one is 5.9, this one is 5.5.
- `00:44:45,533 → 00:44:48,566` **Tasha:** 好吧可是这个很绵这个很粗 / Okay, but this one is fine and this one is coarse.
- `00:44:48,866 → 00:44:49,900` **Jake:** 所以哪个好啊 / So which one is better?
- `00:44:51,033 → 00:44:52,066` **Jake:** 贵4毛钱 / It's 4 cents more.
- `00:44:53,133 → 00:44:54,300` **Jake:** 我觉得这种比较正常 / I think this one is more normal.
- `00:44:53,400 → 00:44:55,733` **Jake:** 500 这个400克OK / 500 grams, this one is 400 grams, okay.
- `00:44:56,133 → 00:44:57,266` **Tasha:** 算了就这个吧 / Never mind, let's go with this one.
- `00:44:57,266 → 00:44:58,033` **Jake:** 就这个吧 / Let's go with this one.
- `00:44:59,066 → 00:45:00,033` **Jake:** 这个棉 / This one is fine.
- `00:44:59,266 → 00:45:01,133` **Tasha:** 糖盐胡椒粉 / Sugar, salt, pepper.
- `00:45:01,866 → 00:45:03,133` **Alice:** 你得买绵白糖 / You need to buy fine sugar.
- `00:45:03,300 → 00:45:04,133` **Tasha:** 买了绵白糖 / I bought fine sugar.
- `00:45:05,300 → 00:45:07,633` **Tasha:** 糖盐胡椒粉味精味精是吧 / Sugar, salt, pepper, MSG, right?
- `00:45:06,766 → 00:45:09,466` **Tasha:** 你还要味精这呢 / You still want MSG, here it is.
- `00:45:46,700 → 00:45:48,133` **Alice:** 怎么没有味精呢 / Why is there no MSG?
- `00:46:13,566 → 00:46:14,000` **Tasha:** 鸡精 / Chicken essence.
- `00:46:14,000 → 00:46:17,000` **Tasha:** 哦还是买了鸡精是吧没有味精是吧 / Oh, we bought chicken essence, not MSG, right?
- `00:46:14,766 → 00:46:16,200` **Alice:** 没有没有味精 / No, no MSG.
- `00:46:18,333 → 00:46:21,433` **Jake:** 没有味精    好的 / No MSG, okay.
- `00:46:18,633 → 00:46:20,600` **Tasha:** 差不多味精和鸡精差不多 / MSG and chicken essence are similar.
- `00:46:21,133 → 00:46:23,566` **Alice:** 味精会健康一点 / MSG is a bit healthier.
- `00:46:23,333 → 00:46:24,566` **Tasha:** 把鸡精挑 / Remove the chicken essence.
- `00:46:24,966 → 00:46:26,766` **Alice:** 鸡精的添加剂多一点 / Chicken essence has more additives.
- `00:46:26,900 → 00:46:28,633` **Jake:** 哦没关系 / Oh, it's fine.
- `00:46:29,466 → 00:46:30,500` **Jake:** 活到现在了 / We've lived this long.
- `00:46:33,700 → 00:46:36,066` **Tasha:** 金针菇松花蛋娃娃菜都买了 / We bought enoki mushrooms, preserved eggs, and baby cabbage.
- `00:46:36,233 → 00:46:37,233` **Alice:** 我买过一个午餐肉 / I bought a can of luncheon meat.
- `00:46:37,233 → 00:46:38,166` **Alice:** 但我不知道够不够 / But I'm not sure if it's enough.
- `00:46:38,900 → 00:46:39,733` **Tasha:** 草莓买了 / Got the strawberries.
- `00:46:39,733 → 00:46:40,666` **Tasha:** 奶油买了 / Got the cream.
- `00:46:40,666 → 00:46:41,666` **Tasha:** 薄荷保鲜膜 / Mint and cling film.
- `00:46:41,666 → 00:46:43,066` **Tasha:** 蛋挞没买呢 / Didn't get the egg tarts.
- `00:46:59,666 → 00:47:00,533` **Tasha:** 我已经开始渴了 / I'm already getting thirsty.
- `00:47:00,500 → 00:47:01,533` **Tasha:** 听到这个声音 / Hearing this sound.
- `00:47:03,566 → 00:47:04,433` **Jake:** 买一会喝 / Buy some to drink later.
- `00:47:06,366 → 00:47:09,366` **Tasha:** 这买什么来着 咖啡豆 / What did we buy again? Coffee beans.
- `00:47:06,700 → 00:47:07,500` **Jake:** 喝 / Drink.
- `00:47:10,266 → 00:47:11,066` **Jake:** 咖啡豆 / Coffee beans.
- `00:47:11,366 → 00:47:11,833` **Tasha:** 他不知道 / He doesn't know.
- `00:47:11,833 → 00:47:13,166` **Tasha:** 磨咖啡要吗 / Do we need to grind the coffee?
- `00:47:12,133 → 00:47:14,266` **Jake:** 咖啡豆在哪买 / Where do we buy coffee beans?
- `00:47:14,500 → 00:47:15,066` **Tasha:** 不知道呀 / No idea.
- `00:47:15,066 → 00:47:16,633` **Alice:** 咖啡豆这不好买吧 / Coffee beans aren't easy to buy, right?
- `00:47:17,566 → 00:47:19,833` **Tasha:** 你好这里有咖啡豆卖吗 / Hi, do you sell coffee beans here?
- `00:47:31,633 → 00:47:33,600` **Alice:** 奶粉哦奶粉是他们买吧 / Oh, milk powder. They buy it, right?
- `00:47:35,000 → 00:47:36,000` **Alice:** 奶粉是他们买吗 / Do they buy the milk powder?
- `00:47:36,000 → 00:47:37,233` **Alice:** 我们是几到几啊 / What time slot are we?
- `00:47:37,400 → 00:47:39,366` **Tasha:** 我们是5-11 / We're from 5-11.
- `00:47:39,300 → 00:47:40,366` **Alice:** 那我们没有买奶粉 / Then we didn't buy the milk powder.
- `00:47:40,566 → 00:47:42,466` **Tasha:** 没事其实奶粉不重要 / It's okay, the milk powder isn't important.
- `00:47:42,466 → 00:47:44,766` **Tasha:** 我只想一小一小包而已 / I just wanted a small pack anyway.
- `00:47:53,000 → 00:47:55,233` **Jake:** 丑蛋糕他们在干嘛 / What are they doing with the ugly cake?
- `00:48:01,166 → 00:48:02,066` **Alice:** 你在找啥呀 / What are you looking for?
- `00:48:02,066 → 00:48:02,733` **Tasha:** 咖啡豆 / Coffee beans.
- `00:48:07,233 → 00:48:08,100` **Alice:** 是蓝胖子吧 / Is it the blue tin?
- `00:48:08,066 → 00:48:11,633` **Tasha:** 啊太贵了这一罐没必要 / Ah, this one is too expensive, not necessary.
- `00:48:15,066 → 00:48:16,500` **Jake:** 水果麦 / Fruit wheat.
- `00:48:15,300 → 00:48:16,333` **Alice:** 哎有咖啡粉 / Hey, there's coffee powder.
- `00:48:15,766 → 00:48:17,833` **Katrina:** 这还有一个冷藏过的 / There's also a refrigerated one.
- `00:48:19,066 → 00:48:21,966` **Katrina:** 哈喽你们买那个肉制品 / Hello, did you guys buy the meat products?
- `00:48:20,333 → 00:48:22,366` **Tasha:** 你觉得买哪个好 / Which one do you think is better to buy?
- `00:48:22,566 → 00:48:25,333` **Katrina:** 那个肉制品火锅全部就是我们管是吗 / Are we in charge of all the hotpot meat products?
- `00:48:23,566 → 00:48:24,100` **Tasha:** 5 59 / 5 59.
- `00:48:25,366 → 00:48:25,800` **Jake:** 对 / Yeah.
- `00:48:25,366 → 00:48:26,266` **Katrina:** 那你们买了什么 / Then what did you buy?
- `00:48:26,266 → 00:48:27,000` **Katrina:** 青菜吗哎 / Vegetables, right?
- `00:48:26,266 → 00:48:27,566` **Tasha:** 他要磨咖啡 / He wants to grind coffee.
- `00:48:27,000 → 00:48:27,733` **Jake:** 买了买了 / Got it, got it.
- `00:48:27,766 → 00:48:28,833` **Alice:** 那这种是不是 / So this one is...
- `00:48:28,066 → 00:48:31,300` **Katrina:** 好像没有买青菜 / Seems like we didn't buy any greens.
- `00:48:28,300 → 00:48:29,500` **Tasha:** 这是你要的咖啡豆吗 / Are these the coffee beans you wanted?
- `00:48:28,866 → 00:48:31,433` **Jake:** 买了不多就买一个 / We bought some, but not much, just one.
- `00:48:29,500 → 00:48:31,233` **Lucia:** 还是多啊 / Still seems like a lot.
- `00:48:31,233 → 00:48:32,000` **Alice:** 还是这种的 / Or maybe this kind?
- `00:48:31,433 → 00:48:34,133` **Jake:** 这个今天是他们要做上汤娃娃菜 / This is for the cabbage soup they're making today.
- `00:48:34,933 → 00:48:37,400` **Jake:** 专门给他上汤娃娃菜搞的蔬菜 / These vegetables are specifically for his cabbage soup.
- `00:48:37,066 → 00:48:38,466` **Shure:** 就是这个有点捞 / This one's a bit too much.
- `00:48:37,900 → 00:48:39,266` **Jake:** 应该没有别的菜了 / I think that's all the veggies.
- `00:48:38,300 → 00:48:41,133` **Katrina:** 也就是说火锅下的菜跟 / So, the veggies for the hot pot and...
- `00:48:40,200 → 00:48:41,133` **Jake:** 火锅没有菜 / There's no veggies for the hot pot.
- `00:48:41,400 → 00:48:43,000` **Jake:** 对火锅还没有菜 / Yeah, no veggies for the hot pot yet.
- `00:48:41,400 → 00:48:44,666` **Katrina:** 肉质都是我们挑 / We chose the meat ourselves.
- `00:48:44,666 → 00:48:45,966` **Katrina:** 那我们得多加一点 / We need to add a bit more.
- `00:48:45,966 → 00:48:47,633` **Katrina:** 感觉6个人不够吃哎 / Feels like it's not enough for six people.
- `00:48:46,233 → 00:48:48,900` **Jake:** 对对对 / Yeah, yeah, you're right.
- `00:48:47,866 → 00:48:48,433` **Alice:** 你买了吗 / Did you buy it?
- `00:48:48,433 → 00:48:51,500` **Shure:** 我买了  啊 对 / I bought it, yes.
- `00:48:48,933 → 00:48:50,100` **Lucia:** 你可以先看一下吗 / Can you check it first?
- `00:48:49,866 → 00:48:52,366` **Tasha:** 这个吗 那行那我就不不拿了 / This one? Okay, then I won't take it.
- `00:48:50,066 → 00:48:51,633` **Lucia:** 现在买的东西 / The stuff we bought now...
- `00:48:50,366 → 00:48:53,133` **Jake:** 好一会我们对一下等他们一起对一下 / Okay, we'll check it later together.
- `00:48:53,433 → 00:48:54,266` **Jake:** 盘算一下 / Let's calculate it.
- `00:48:53,700 → 00:48:55,333` **Katrina:** 还得买两盒肉哎 / We still need to buy two more boxes of meat.
- `00:48:54,266 → 00:48:57,933` **Jake:** 他们现在在套好 / They're preparing now.
- `00:48:55,700 → 00:48:57,900` **Lucia:** 好奇一下你们这边的东西 / I'm curious about your stuff here.
- `00:48:57,933 → 00:48:59,433` **Jake:** 他们在研究咖啡豆 / They're researching coffee beans.
- `00:48:58,933 → 00:48:59,933` **Tasha:** 保鲜膜 / Plastic wrap.
- `00:48:59,800 → 00:49:00,933` **Alice:** 保鲜膜 / Plastic wrap.
- `00:49:01,400 → 00:49:02,666` **Jake:** 咖啡豆 / Coffee beans.
- `00:49:01,733 → 00:49:06,100` **Lucia:** 我们刚才好像是拿了一盒速溶咖啡 / Earlier, we seemed to have taken a box of instant coffee.
- `00:49:06,066 → 00:49:08,233` **Shure:** 我跟他跟他说了 / I told him about it.
- `00:49:07,333 → 00:49:10,566` **Jake:** 哦速溶但是这个就慢 / Oh, instant coffee is slower though.
- `00:49:10,233 → 00:49:11,233` **Lucia:** 嗯？有泰拉 / Hmm? There’s Terra.
- `00:49:11,633 → 00:49:13,700` **Katrina:** 什么呀 / What?
- `00:49:12,066 → 00:49:13,166` **Shure:** 咖啡豆的话 / As for coffee beans...
- `00:49:13,200 → 00:49:14,066` **Lucia:** 就是这个 / It's this one.
- `00:49:15,666 → 00:49:18,366` **Lucia:** 好久不见了 从我回国 / Long time no see since I returned home.
- `00:49:16,333 → 00:49:17,700` **Katrina:** 你们拿椰奶了吗 / Did you get the coconut milk?
- `00:49:17,866 → 00:49:19,000` **Alice:** 有椰子水 / There's coconut water.
- `00:49:18,366 → 00:49:19,633` **Shure:** 购入一下 / Let's buy some.
- `00:49:20,766 → 00:49:23,933` **Lucia:** mitchposticks怎么还是从德国进口了 / Why are the mitchposticks still imported from Germany?
- `00:49:24,366 → 00:49:25,000` **Jake:** 呵呵 / Haha.
- `00:49:24,666 → 00:49:25,966` **Katrina:** 我们拿椰子汁了吗 / Did we get the coconut juice?
- `00:49:25,966 → 00:49:27,066` **Alice:** 你们需要吗 / Do you guys need it?
- `00:49:27,133 → 00:49:27,700` **Katrina:** 我在想 / I'm thinking.
- `00:49:27,700 → 00:49:29,766` **Katrina:** 咖啡是不是可以兑一点椰子汁啊 / Can we mix a bit of coconut juice with coffee?
- `00:49:29,900 → 00:49:31,500` **Katrina:** 生椰拿铁 / Like a coconut latte.
- `00:49:30,900 → 00:49:31,833` **Alice:** 椰奶吗 / Coconut milk?
- `00:49:31,500 → 00:49:34,066` **Katrina:** 嗯但是那种大的咖啡 / Yeah, but for a large coffee.
- `00:49:32,566 → 00:49:33,133` **Shure:** 可以 / Sure.
- `00:49:34,066 → 00:49:36,266` **Katrina:** 呃椰奶是不是只能放一天 / Uh, can coconut milk only last a day?
- `00:49:36,633 → 00:49:38,933` **Alice:** 不开封的话可以放几天 / If it's unopened, it can last a few days.
- `00:49:37,933 → 00:49:40,366` **Katrina:** 那就是开了那就得一天喝完呗 / So if we open it, we have to finish it in a day?
- `00:49:40,566 → 00:49:43,233` **Katrina:** 我没看他那种小盒的椰树你知道吗 / I didn't see those small boxes of coconut milk, you know?
- `00:49:43,233 → 00:49:44,900` **Katrina:** 他都是大盒的椰树 / They only have large boxes.
- `00:49:43,233 → 00:49:46,166` **Jake:** 螺蛳粉看到了 / I saw the snail noodles.
- `00:49:44,900 → 00:49:47,133` **Lucia:** 那个 / That one.
- `00:49:46,700 → 00:49:47,500` **Jake:** 真的 / Really.
- `00:49:48,766 → 00:49:50,933` **Katrina:** 你去问一下子吧你拿 / You should go ask, and take it.
- `00:49:53,800 → 00:49:54,866` **Jake:** 我们要对一下吗 / Should we match it?
- `00:49:55,333 → 00:49:56,433` **Lucia:** 感觉可以 / Sounds good.
- `00:49:56,433 → 00:49:57,466` **Jake:** 好那 / Alright then.
- `00:49:58,500 → 00:50:01,433` **Alice:** 其实那个大的你开封之后放两三天 / Actually, after you open the large one, it can last two or three days.
- `00:49:59,833 → 00:50:01,100` **Jake:** 那找个地方 / Let's find a place.
- `00:50:01,700 → 00:50:02,566` **Jake:** 我们先对一下吧 / Let's match it first.
- `00:50:03,433 → 00:50:05,366` **Lucia:** 对保鲜膜可以在超市买 / You can buy cling film at the supermarket.
- `00:50:06,566 → 00:50:09,666` **Jake:** 对一下我们找一个 / Let's match it first and find one.
- `00:50:10,466 → 00:50:11,633` **Alice:** 我一般买这种大的 / I usually buy the large ones.
- `00:50:11,633 → 00:50:15,233` **Tasha:** 买咖啡液也行 就不用咖啡粉就不用速溶咖啡调了 / You can buy coffee concentrate, so you don't need to use coffee powder or instant coffee.
- `00:50:11,700 → 00:50:12,900` **Alice:** 之后放两三天也没事 / After that, it can last two or three days.
- `00:50:12,900 → 00:50:15,066` **Alice:** 不用他那个变质的话会很明显 / If it spoils, it'll be very obvious.
- `00:50:15,233 → 00:50:16,500` **Katrina:** 要么直接叫外卖吧 / Or we could just order takeout.
- `00:50:15,666 → 00:50:18,233` **Shure:** 也行反正都都都行 / That works too, anything's fine.
- `00:50:19,400 → 00:50:21,466` **Jake:** 都行反正我也不喝咖啡 / Anything's fine, I don't drink coffee anyway.
- `00:50:21,833 → 00:50:22,633` **Shure:** 那你今天 / Then today...
- `00:50:21,933 → 00:50:24,166` **Jake:** 哈哈早上不行啊 / Haha, not in the morning.
- `00:50:22,633 → 00:50:24,566` **Katrina:** 不知道以为我们工作呢 / People might think we're working.
- `00:50:24,166 → 00:50:25,333` **Jake:** 早上喝了一下 / I had a bit in the morning.
- `00:50:25,666 → 00:50:26,833` **Shure:** 不行了是吧
- `00:50:26,200 → 00:50:27,866` **Jake:** 不行那要喝一下
- `00:50:27,166 → 00:50:28,533` **Lucia:** 其实还好
- `00:50:28,266 → 00:50:30,700` **Shure:** 我靠哥
- `00:50:29,800 → 00:50:31,200` **Jake:** 这两天应该就行了
- `00:50:31,200 → 00:50:32,300` **Jake:** 昨天也搞定了
- `00:50:31,933 → 00:50:33,466` **Shure:** 还行吧现在
- `00:50:32,633 → 00:50:34,700` **Jake:** 昨天拍破蛋搞定了
- `00:50:32,866 → 00:50:33,833` **Alice:** 肉在哪啊
- `00:50:35,000 → 00:50:35,833` **Jake:** 下面就是
- `00:50:35,833 → 00:50:36,833` **Jake:** 执行就行
- `00:50:36,966 → 00:50:38,733` **Tasha:** 再买点丸子和虾滑
- `00:50:37,333 → 00:50:40,500` **Shure:** 你身体还说得过去吗
- `00:50:38,733 → 00:50:40,866` **Katrina:** 丸子买了虾滑也买了
- `00:50:40,833 → 00:50:41,666` **Jake:** 强的很
- `00:50:40,866 → 00:50:41,966` **Katrina:** 虾滑在这哦
- `00:50:41,966 → 00:50:43,866` **Shure:** 真的吗 多久没体检了
- `00:50:43,300 → 00:50:44,633` **Katrina:** 我觉得还要拿两盒肉
- `00:50:45,366 → 00:50:46,300` **Katrina:** 青菜也没买哎
- `00:50:47,466 → 00:50:48,500` **Lucia:** 我们为什么买哦
- `00:50:48,500 → 00:50:50,100` **Lucia:** 哦哦蔬菜是吧 / Oh, oh, vegetables, right?
- `00:50:48,666 → 00:50:49,466` **Jake:** 确实 / Indeed.
- `00:50:50,133 → 00:50:51,900` **Lucia:** 蔬菜哦新到的咸鱼土豆 / Vegetables, oh, and the new salted fish potatoes.
- `00:50:52,300 → 00:50:53,766` **Katrina:** 你要吃土豆是吗 / You want to eat potatoes, right?
- `00:50:55,300 → 00:50:57,066` **Shure:** 土豆好土豆可以 / Potatoes are good, potatoes are fine.
- `00:50:56,066 → 00:50:58,300` **Jake:** 土豆可以啊 / Potatoes are fine.
- `00:50:57,066 → 00:50:57,833` **Lucia:** 其实我也喜欢 / Actually, I like them too.
- `00:50:58,566 → 00:51:01,133` **Katrina:** 走吧那我们蔬菜买肉跟青菜 / Let's go then, we'll buy vegetables, meat, and greens.
- `00:50:59,433 → 00:51:01,500` **Jake:** 咱不是还有那个削土豆皮的那个 / Don't we still have that potato peeler thing?
- `00:51:01,500 → 00:51:02,666` **Tasha:** 那个不是完了小土豆皮奥 / Isn't that for small potato skins?
- `00:51:02,833 → 00:51:03,933` **Tasha:** 芋头没有买 / We didn't buy taro.
- `00:51:04,833 → 00:51:06,266` **Jake:** 走 / Let's go.
- `00:51:05,066 → 00:51:06,333` **Shure:** 芋头是啥啊 / What's taro?
- `00:51:05,066 → 00:51:06,600` **Lucia:** 芋头放火锅吗 / Do you put taro in hotpot?
- `00:51:05,233 → 00:51:06,000` **Alice:** 芋头 / Taro.
- `00:51:06,633 → 00:51:08,266` **Tasha:** 不是芋泥 / Not taro paste.
- `00:51:08,400 → 00:51:09,566` **Lucia:** 啊okok / Ah, okay, okay.
- `00:51:09,566 → 00:51:10,166` **Shure:** 芋泥 / Taro paste.
- `00:51:10,300 → 00:51:11,400` **Jake:** 哎呀这什么东西 / Oh man, what is this?
- `00:51:10,566 → 00:51:12,300` **Lucia:** sorry没有跟上这个频道 / Sorry, I couldn't keep up with the channel.
- `00:51:12,066 → 00:51:12,700` **Shure:** 芋泥 / Taro mash
- `00:51:12,300 → 00:51:13,466` **Katrina:** 芋泥芋泥耶 / Taro mash, yay
- `00:51:12,700 → 00:51:14,333` **Alice:** 芋头放火锅也很好吃 / Taro is also great in hotpot.
- `00:51:14,466 → 00:51:15,466` **Alice:** 那个荔浦芋头 / That Lipu taro.
- `00:51:15,000 → 00:51:16,433` **Jake:** 酱香烤鸭 / Saucy roast duck.
- `00:51:15,466 → 00:51:17,133` **Tasha:** 对我是要买荔浦芋头 / Yeah, I need to buy Lipu taro.
- `00:51:17,133 → 00:51:18,233` **Tasha:** 走吧诶不对 / Let's go, oh wait.
- `00:51:17,933 → 00:51:18,900` **Shure:** 芋芋泥能波波吗 / Can taro mash make bubbles?
- `00:51:18,433 → 00:51:20,766` **Tasha:** 好那我们的爆皮刀就有两用了 / Great, then our peeler has two uses.
- `00:51:21,000 → 00:51:23,100` **Jake:** 哈哈哈走 / Hahaha, let's go.
- `00:51:22,000 → 00:51:25,433` **Katrina:** 哈哈少来这种 / Haha, stop with that.
- `00:51:26,866 → 00:51:28,300` **Jake:** 哎呀 / Oh no.
- `00:51:27,500 → 00:51:28,200` **Alice:** 咋了 / What happened?
- `00:51:31,566 → 00:51:32,766` **Tasha:** 你喜欢吃吗 / Do you like it?
- `00:51:32,833 → 00:51:33,566` **Jake:** 吃什么呀 / Like what?
- `00:51:33,633 → 00:51:34,166` **Tasha:** 这个 / This.
- `00:51:34,366 → 00:51:35,066` **Jake:** 螺蛳粉啊 / Snail noodles?
- `00:51:35,066 → 00:51:38,133` **Jake:** 嗯呃能吃就没 / Hmm, well, I can eat it.
- `00:51:38,233 → 00:51:40,000` **Jake:** 那么没一定要吃 / But I don't really have to.
- `00:51:40,000 → 00:51:41,600` **Jake:** 对没那么想吃 / Not really craving it.
- `00:51:41,700 → 00:51:42,500` **Jake:** doyouknow / Do you know?
- `00:51:43,500 → 00:51:44,700` **Jake:** 就是就是 / I mean, I mean
- `00:51:44,733 → 00:51:46,700` **Jake:** 没得吃可以吃一吃 / If there's nothing else, I can eat it.
- `00:51:46,333 → 00:51:48,900` **Tasha:** 谁去买蔬菜他们去买蔬菜 / Who's buying veggies? Are they?
- `00:51:47,700 → 00:51:48,566` **Alice:** 我们啊 / We are.
- `00:51:48,566 → 00:51:49,800` **Jake:** 他们他们买蔬菜 / They're buying veggies.
- `00:51:50,200 → 00:51:51,066` **Jake:** 他们买蔬菜 / They're buying veggies.
- `00:51:50,766 → 00:51:52,466` **Alice:** 我们菜不是在那边吗 / Aren't the veggies over there?
- `00:51:51,066 → 00:51:54,500` **Jake:** 我我对啊你们他们 / I, I, yeah, you, they are.
- `00:51:52,566 → 00:51:54,466` **Tasha:** 他们可能先买肉吧 / Maybe they're buying meat first.
- `00:51:54,466 → 00:51:56,833` **Jake:** 他们买帝王蟹是吧 / They're buying king crab, right?
- `00:51:56,866 → 00:51:58,633` **Tasha:** 这个也在资金范围内吗 / Is that within the budget?
- `00:51:57,133 → 00:51:58,066` **Jake:** 你们买海鲜 / You guys buy seafood.
- `00:51:58,066 → 00:52:00,833` **Jake:** 哈哈不不你们买海鲜吗 / Haha, no, no, are you buying seafood?
- `00:51:58,933 → 00:52:02,866` **Lucia:** 哇哦 我可以吃这个吗 / Wow, can I eat this?
- `00:52:02,866 → 00:52:05,133` **Jake:** 哈哈哈打打招呼吧 / Hahaha, say hi.
- `00:52:05,166 → 00:52:06,866` **Jake:** 要不就拍一拍吧要不 / Or just wave.
- `00:52:06,866 → 00:52:08,433` **Jake:** 你好 / Hello.
- `00:52:08,433 → 00:52:09,833` **Lucia:** 扫描一下 / Scan it.
- `00:52:10,166 → 00:52:12,133` **Jake:** 1, 699也还行吧 / 1, 699 is okay, right?
- `00:52:12,733 → 00:52:14,000` **Tasha:** 那来一只吧 / Then let's have one.
- `00:52:12,900 → 00:52:13,866` **Lucia:** 真的吗可以吃吗 / Really? Can we eat it?
- `00:52:13,866 → 00:52:15,233` **Lucia:** 哈哈哈来一只吧 / Hahaha, let's have one.
- `00:52:15,233 → 00:52:16,566` **Shure:** 你千万不要口嗨 / Don't just talk big.
- `00:52:16,566 → 00:52:17,700` **Shure:** 我们会当真的好不好 / We'll take it seriously, alright?
- `00:52:17,700 → 00:52:20,300` **Lucia:** 对对对 / Yeah, yeah, yeah.
- `00:52:18,933 → 00:52:20,300` **Jake:** 我就说也还行 / I just said it's okay.
- `00:52:20,300 → 00:52:21,733` **Jake:** 我没多说什么 / I didn't say much.
- `00:52:20,300 → 00:52:23,000` **Tasha:** 有没有那个转架还是干嘛 / Do we have that rotator thing or what?
- `00:52:22,000 → 00:52:26,200` **Jake:** 哈哈哈我没哈哈哈 / Hahaha, I didn't hahaha.
- `00:52:22,700 → 00:52:25,233` **Lucia:** 也还行我们就把这个野字去掉吧啊 / It's okay, let's just remove the word "wild", alright?
- `00:52:26,666 → 00:52:27,766` **Lucia:** 把还字也去掉 / And remove the word "okay".
- `00:52:27,066 → 00:52:29,500` **Shure:** 蘸料你们买了吗 / Did you buy the dipping sauce?
- `00:52:29,066 → 00:52:29,766` **Jake:** 行 / Sure.
- `00:52:29,633 → 00:52:32,066` **Lucia:** 对对对我们自动识别成型 / Yeah, yeah, we automatically recognize and form it.
- `00:52:32,200 → 00:52:34,433` **Jake:** 好好 / Alright, alright.
- `00:52:32,633 → 00:52:33,833` **Shure:** 蘸料你们买了吗 / Did you buy the dipping sauce?
- `00:52:34,433 → 00:52:35,400` **Alice:** 蘸料没有 / No dipping sauce.
- `00:52:34,733 → 00:52:36,733` **Lucia:** 没有买蘸料 / Didn't buy dipping sauce.
- `00:52:39,233 → 00:52:41,233` **Shure:** 你们都吃什么碟的火锅一般 / What kind of dipping sauce do you usually have with hot pot?
- `00:52:41,466 → 00:52:42,233` **Alice:** 麻酱 / Sesame sauce.
- `00:52:42,433 → 00:52:44,966` **Shure:** 麻酱醋麻酱 / Sesame sauce, vinegar, sesame sauce.
- `00:52:42,433 → 00:52:45,700` **Tasha:** 我要醋 / I want vinegar.
- `00:52:44,466 → 00:52:45,266` **Lucia:** 芝麻酱 / Sesame sauce.
- `00:52:50,933 → 00:52:53,100` **Tasha:** 你吃什么碟呢 醋碟 / What kind of dipping sauce do you use? Vinegar?
- `00:52:53,066 → 00:52:54,900` **Shure:** 你吃火锅一般吃什么蘸料 / What dipping sauce do you usually have with hot pot?
- `00:52:54,300 → 00:52:54,800` **Alice:** 麻酱 / Sesame sauce.
- `00:52:54,800 → 00:52:57,400` **Jake:** 呃麻酱吧 / Uh, sesame sauce.
- `00:52:54,933 → 00:52:55,866` **Alice:** 油碟 / Oil sauce.
- `00:52:57,400 → 00:52:58,233` **Jake:** 你吃什么碟 / What sauce do you use?
- `00:52:58,133 → 00:52:59,433` **Tasha:** 你背叛了江苏人 / You betrayed the people of Jiangsu.
- `00:52:58,966 → 00:53:03,000` **Lucia:** 那我们现在醋和麻酱还有什么别的需要吗 / So we have vinegar and sesame sauce, do we need anything else?
- `00:53:01,066 → 00:53:03,200` **Jake:** 其实我在江苏都没怎么吃过火锅 / Actually, I hardly ever had hot pot in Jiangsu.
- `00:53:03,200 → 00:53:05,933` **Jake:** 我都是上了大学才 / I only started in college.
- `00:53:07,833 → 00:53:09,000` **Jake:** 你是醋碟吗 / You use vinegar sauce?
- `00:53:09,433 → 00:53:09,966` **Tasha:** 对 / Yeah.
- `00:53:11,766 → 00:53:15,933` **Tasha:** 我们不应该都和喝恒硕香醋长大的吗 / Weren't we all supposed to grow up drinking Hengshuo vinegar?
- `00:53:15,066 → 00:53:17,066` **Shure:** 杨老板吃什么 / What does Jake use?
- `00:53:15,933 → 00:53:18,200` **Jake:** 呃就麻将吧 / Uh, just sesame sauce.
- `00:53:19,800 → 00:53:21,000` **Jake:** 或者当回江苏人吧 / Or I'll be a Jiangsu person again.
- `00:53:21,000 → 00:53:22,500` **Jake:** 要不吃醋碟也行 / How about having vinegar sauce instead?
- `00:53:22,966 → 00:53:24,000` **Tasha:** 对啊醋碟 / Yeah, vinegar sauce.
- `00:53:23,300 → 00:53:24,166` **Alice:** 这是熟食了 / This is cooked food.
- `00:53:24,166 → 00:53:25,466` **Tasha:** 对应该在那吧 / Yeah, it should be over there.
- `00:53:26,700 → 00:53:29,066` **Lucia:** 也可以其实火锅还挺省钱的 / Actually, hot pot can be quite economical.
- `00:53:28,500 → 00:53:32,733` **Lucia:** 嗯只要一堆食材往里一煮就好了哈 / Yeah, just throw a bunch of ingredients in and cook them.
- `00:53:29,800 → 00:53:35,466` **Jake:** 对是的粽子也很省钱哈哈哈哈 / Yeah, right. Zongzi is also very economical, hahaha.
- `00:53:41,066 → 00:53:42,066` **Jake:** 买点方便面吧 / How about buying some instant noodles?
- `00:53:42,166 → 00:53:44,033` **Jake:** 算了算了哈哈哈 / Never mind, never mind, hahaha.
- `00:53:44,000 → 00:53:45,200` **Katrina:** 后面两天经费不足 / We're short on budget for the next two days.
- `00:53:45,233 → 00:53:46,233` **Katrina:** 天天方便面 / Instant noodles every day.
- `00:53:49,700 → 00:53:52,366` **Tasha:** 你觉得芋头会在哪有呢 / Where do you think we can find taro?
- `00:53:53,966 → 00:53:55,066` **Katrina:** 找到醋了吗 / Did you find the vinegar?
- `00:53:55,066 → 00:53:55,866` **Jake:** 干啥去啦 / Where did you go?
- `00:53:56,333 → 00:53:57,200` **Shure:** 没找到反正 / Couldn't find it anyway.
- `00:54:01,000 → 00:54:03,033` **Shure:** 你好调料的那些在哪里 / Excuse me, where are the condiments?
- `00:54:15,000 → 00:54:16,533` **Alice:** 这种是不是也可以涮火锅 / Can this also be used for hot pot?
- `00:54:18,866 → 00:54:20,800` **Alice:** 这种魔芋之类的 / This kind of konjac or something.
- `00:54:22,233 → 00:54:24,500` **Tasha:** 但是他们会不会买豆制品呢 / But would they buy tofu products?
- `00:54:24,933 → 00:54:25,666` **Alice:** 不知道那 / I don't know.
- `00:54:26,433 → 00:54:28,833` **Tasha:** 那那那我觉得要来一个豆腐 / Well, well, well, I think we need some tofu.
- `00:54:27,233 → 00:54:28,933` **Jake:** 豆腐 / Tofu.
- `00:54:30,300 → 00:54:31,866` **Jake:** 包浆豆腐 / Wrapped tofu.
- `00:54:31,700 → 00:54:32,766` **Alice:** 我需要魔芋 / I need konjac.
- `00:54:34,633 → 00:54:38,766` **Tasha:** 你喜欢吃这种干豆腐还是吃 / Do you prefer dried tofu or...
- `00:54:37,966 → 00:54:38,766` **Alice:** 内酯豆腐 / Lactone tofu.
- `00:54:38,966 → 00:54:39,966` **Tasha:** 内酯豆腐 / Lactone tofu.
- `00:54:41,066 → 00:54:43,033` **Tasha:** 这个呢浆水豆腐哎 / How about this one, water tofu?
- `00:54:44,066 → 00:54:45,400` **Jake:** 这都什么差别 / What's the difference between these?
- `00:54:45,433 → 00:54:46,500` **Jake:** 这个这个一般 / This one is just so-so.
- `00:54:46,700 → 00:54:48,066` **Jake:** 我感觉这个有点硬 / I feel this one is a bit hard.
- `00:54:48,066 → 00:54:49,400` **Alice:** 就是那种老豆腐 / It's just old tofu.
- `00:54:48,966 → 00:54:50,066` **Tasha:** 那就这种吧 / Then let's go with this one.
- `00:54:50,300 → 00:54:51,000` **Jake:** 啊可以 / Ah, okay.
- `00:54:51,000 → 00:54:51,800` **Jake:** 看起来不错 / Looks good.
- `00:54:51,566 → 00:54:54,933` **Tasha:** 鲜豆腐 豆腐皮呢 / Fresh tofu. What about tofu skin?
- `00:54:53,333 → 00:54:54,933` **Alice:** 魔芋你要吃那种 / Do you want to eat that konjac?
- `00:54:56,266 → 00:54:56,766` **Tasha:** 魔芋 / Konjac.
- `00:54:56,766 → 00:54:57,566` **Alice:** 对 / Right.
- `00:54:57,933 → 00:54:59,300` **Tasha:** 这个魔芋好奇怪 / This konjac looks weird.
- `00:54:59,866 → 00:55:01,233` **Alice:** 这魔芋就是这种的 / This konjac is like this.
- `00:55:02,333 → 00:55:04,066` **Tasha:** 有没有魔芋丝 / Is there konjac noodles?
- `00:55:03,233 → 00:55:03,966` **Alice:** 魔芋丝 / Konjac noodles.
- `00:55:04,066 → 00:55:08,266` **Tasha:** 魔芋丝魔芋丝或者魔芋皮都行 / Konjac noodles or konjac skin, both are fine.
- `00:55:17,566 → 00:55:19,433` **Alice:** 这种比较原生 / This one is more natural.
- `00:55:19,633 → 00:55:21,100` **Tasha:** 行那再拿个这个吧 / Okay, then let's take this one.
- `00:55:21,700 → 00:55:23,366` **Alice:** 3块9可以 / 3.9 yuan is okay.
- `00:55:31,933 → 00:55:34,000` **Alice:** 买这个金针菇吧这个便宜 / Let's buy these enoki mushrooms, they're cheaper.
- `00:55:34,566 → 00:55:35,300` **Jake:** 好 / Okay.
- `00:55:36,766 → 00:55:39,066` **Alice:** : 呃把这个有机的给它放回去 / Uh, put the organic one back.
- `00:55:39,166 → 00:55:41,633` **Jake:** 对咱就不要那么有机了 / Yeah, let's not be so organic.
- `00:55:44,266 → 00:55:45,800` **Alice:** 这有娃娃菜吗 / Do they have baby bok choy here?
- `00:55:46,000 → 00:55:47,000` **Alice:** 让我找找 / Let me look for it.
- `00:55:48,000 → 00:55:49,400` **Jake:** 哎你问的好 / Oh, good question.
- `00:57:52,466 → 00:57:53,133` **Jake:** 跟丢了 / Lost track.
- `00:57:52,700 → 00:57:54,666` **Katrina:** 感觉他的脑袋一口 / Feels like his head got bitten off.
- `00:57:53,166 → 00:57:54,933` **Shure:** 完了跟丢了去搜寻吧 / We lost them, let's search.
- `00:58:50,433 → 00:58:51,466` **Jake:** 找你们去了 / Came to find you all.
- `00:58:51,766 → 00:58:52,733` **Tasha:** 烤地瓜吗 / Roasted sweet potato?
- `00:58:52,933 → 00:58:55,100` **Jake:** 烤就放烤箱 / If roasting, put it in the oven.
- `00:58:54,433 → 00:58:55,133` **Alice:** 放吧另一个呢 / Put it in, where's the other one?
- `00:58:56,000 → 00:58:57,400` **Tasha:** 另什么另一个 / The other what?
- `00:58:56,333 → 00:58:57,333` **Jake:** 放烤箱是吧 / Put it in the oven, right?
- `00:58:56,766 → 00:58:58,833` **Tasha:** 噢噢烟薯是吗 / Oh, oh, the smoky sweet potato?
- `00:58:58,700 → 00:58:59,633` **Jake:** 土豆 / Potato.
- `00:58:59,266 → 00:59:00,500` **Tasha:** 好吧我想吃 / Okay, I want to eat it.
- `00:59:00,500 → 00:59:01,300` **Jake:** 有土豆吗 / Do we have potatoes?
- `00:59:02,000 → 00:59:03,400` **Jake:** 我不用我不用 / I don't need it, I don't need it.
- `00:59:03,433 → 00:59:04,166` **Jake:** 我乱说的 / I was just talking nonsense.
- `00:59:04,266 → 00:59:05,733` **Jake:** 因为刚刚不是提到了吗 / Because it was mentioned earlier.
- `00:59:14,266 → 00:59:15,066` **Alice:** 两个 / Two.
- `00:59:16,133 → 00:59:17,233` **Jake:** 给你加俩土豆 / I'll add two potatoes for you.

- `00:10:12,466 → 00:10:13,266` **Jake:** 大肠杆菌 / E. coli.
- `00:10:14,866 → 00:10:15,466` **Jake:** 大肠杆菌 / E. coli.

- `00:10:36,066 → 00:10:36,866` **Tasha:** 旺仔牛奶 / Want Want milk.

- `00:10:41,866 → 00:10:42,566` **Jake:** 好的 / Got it.

- `00:11:46,066 → 00:11:52,133` 芝士皮 生菜和番茄洋葱就行了 / Tasha: Cheese slices, lettuce, tomato, and onions, that's all we need.

- `00:12:06,966 → 00:12:07,800` **Tasha:** 你想吃吗 / Do you want to eat it?

- `00:12:09,666 → 00:12:12,233` **Tasha:** 你不想吃算了 KFC给你买得了 / If you don't want it, forget it. I'll get you KFC instead.
- `00:12:12,500 → 00:12:14,466` **Jake:** 好啊好啊在哪里啊 / Sure, sure. Where is it?

- `00:12:15,500 → 00:12:18,566` **Jake:** 收银区去了收银区见啊 / At the cash register. See you there.
- `00:12:21,966 → 00:12:23,733` **Tasha:** 你想要我帮你打个折吗 / Do you want me to get you a discount?

- `00:12:25,966 → 00:12:26,566` **Tasha:** 怎么折 / How?

- `00:13:11,833 → 00:13:14,366` **Lucia:** 盒马是必须要用APP才能结账的 / At Hema, you have to use the app to check out.
- `00:13:14,366 → 00:13:16,366` **Lucia:** 我刚回国的时候跟我爸逛了一趟 / When I first came back to the country, I went shopping with my dad.
- `00:13:16,366 → 00:13:17,333` **Lucia:** 发现只能用APP结账 / And found out you can only check out using the app.

- `00:14:09,766 → 00:14:11,066` **Lucia:** 肯定会看到我家地址 / I will definitely see my home address.

- `00:14:16,333 → 00:14:18,566` **Lucia:** 我今天打开淘宝查材料包的时候也是 / When I opened Taobao to check for material packs today, it was the same.

- `00:15:04,566 → 00:15:06,433` **Jake:** 我们这些东西怎么带走啊 / How are we going to take all this stuff?

- `00:15:15,566 → 00:15:18,300` **Lucia:** 有种给自己脑子接口一样的感觉 / Feels like I'm plugging my brain in.
