- `00:17:26,066 → 00:17:27,500` **Jake:** 我每次一塑料袋100新币 / I spend 100 SGD for each plastic bag.
- `00:17:28,066 → 00:17:29,000` **Shure:** 杨老板就是大气 / Jake, you are so generous.
- `00:17:28,666 → 00:17:30,033` **Lucia:** 感觉不值 没到一车呢 / It doesn't seem worth it, it's not even one car.
- `00:17:30,666 → 00:17:31,566` **Lucia:** 这边才装了 / It’s only filled here.
- `00:17:31,666 → 00:17:32,566` **Jake:** OK OK / OK, OK.
- `00:17:32,566 → 00:17:34,133` **Jake:** 那可能是400金币 / That might be 400 gold coins.
- `00:17:34,166 → 00:17:35,333` **Jake:** 我估计 / I guess.
- `00:17:35,333 → 00:17:36,700` **Tasha:** 这个结过账的 / This one has been paid.
- `00:17:36,700 → 00:17:38,566` **Lucia:** 对这这里也都结了 / Yes, everything here is paid.
- `00:17:38,666 → 00:17:40,066` **Lucia:** 这里这里好像还没结吧 / This one here seems unpaid.
- `00:17:39,766 → 00:17:40,933` **Katrina:** 没有放台上 / It's not put on the counter.
- `00:17:40,066 → 00:17:41,366` **Alice:** 这怎么放呢 / How do we put this?
- `00:17:41,366 → 00:17:42,933` **Lucia:** 咋要不 先把这的都挪到这 / How about we move everything here first?
- `00:17:43,566 → 00:17:44,233` **Jake:** 再来一个车 / Let's get another car.
- `00:17:44,233 → 00:17:45,400` **Jake:** 我再来一辆车 / I'll get another car.
- `00:17:45,566 → 00:17:46,200` **Jake:** 再来一辆车 / Another car.
- `00:17:46,200 → 00:17:49,833` **Jake:** 不用用了哦OK好嘞 / No need, oh OK, got it.
- `00:17:47,300 → 00:17:49,733` **Lucia:** 就把这个都挪过来然后 / Just move all of this over and then...
- `00:17:50,866 → 00:17:52,900` **Lucia:** 那边装完的放到这个车里 / Put the filled ones in this car.
- `00:17:56,566 → 00:17:58,233` **Katrina:** 就是没结是吗 / So, it's not paid yet?
- `00:17:58,233 → 00:18:01,566` **Jake:** 结账要先下载好盒马APP才能结账 / You need to download the Hema app to check out.
- `00:18:03,000 → 00:18:04,366` **Katrina:** 在那边装袋吧 / Let's pack the bags over there.
- `00:18:07,433 → 00:18:09,366` **Tasha:** 新加坡没有盒马是吗 / There's no Hema in Singapore, right?
- `00:18:09,700 → 00:18:10,566` **Jake:** 应该没有 / Probably not.
- `00:18:11,666 → 00:18:13,133` **Jake:** 我探索的也不多 / I haven't explored much.
- `00:18:13,133 → 00:18:15,300` **Katrina:** 那新加坡那边是什么样的超市 / What kind of supermarkets are there in Singapore?
- `00:18:15,300 → 00:18:16,966` **Lucia:** 就这种天生纤维的这种 / The kind with natural fibers.
- `00:18:19,066 → 00:18:20,066` **Lucia:** 全食吗 / Whole Foods?
- `00:18:21,233 → 00:18:24,000` **Jake:** 不是就是他们有一家fire price / No, they have a fire price.
- `00:18:24,000 → 00:18:25,900` **Jake:** 应该是政府给了很多补贴的 / The government must have given them a lot of subsidies.
- `00:18:26,466 → 00:18:27,766` **Jake:** 哦叫fire price / Oh, it's called fire price.
- `00:18:27,766 → 00:18:28,866` **Jake:** 很便宜对对对 / Very cheap, yeah, yeah, yeah.
- `00:18:31,633 → 00:18:34,800` **Lucia:** 这个线老是让我弄得很奇怪 / This wire always makes me feel weird.
- `00:18:32,966 → 00:18:34,200` **Jake:** 哎我帮你 / Hey, let me help you.
- `00:18:34,800 → 00:18:36,400` **Jake:** 要不就是这个放你后面 / How about we put it behind you?
- `00:18:35,566 → 00:18:36,833` **Shure:** 再来一个袋 / Another bag, please.
- `00:18:36,866 → 00:18:37,633` **Shure:** 你好你好 / Hello, hello.
- `00:18:38,000 → 00:18:38,766` **Shure:** 再来一个袋 / Another bag, please.
- `00:18:38,500 → 00:18:39,766` **Lucia:** 有点卷插头 / The plug is a bit tangled.
- `00:18:41,566 → 00:18:42,833` **Lucia:** 给他弄直一点 / Straighten it out a bit.
- `00:18:42,866 → 00:18:43,633` **Lucia:** 啊对 / Ah, yes.
- `00:18:43,666 → 00:18:44,466` **Lucia:** 谢谢 / Thanks.
- `00:18:44,966 → 00:18:46,633` **Lucia:** 要不然好像我动着动着 / Otherwise, it feels like I'm moving it around.
- `00:18:46,700 → 00:18:48,500` **Lucia:** 他就快要把我缠起来了 / And it almost wraps around me.
- `00:18:50,300 → 00:18:53,466` **Jake:** 这个是久经验证的插电姿势 / This is a well-tested way of plugging it in.
- `00:18:54,300 → 00:18:57,733` **Jake:** 对其实在后面绕过来最好后面贴个胶 / Yes, actually, it's best to loop it around the back and stick some tape.
- `00:18:57,733 → 00:19:00,133` **Lucia:** 你是做过多少次这样类似的实验了 / How many times have you done similar experiments?
- `00:19:00,866 → 00:19:02,833` **Jake:** 对这个1月份到的 / This arrived in January.
- `00:19:03,433 → 00:19:04,800` **Jake:** 我带着这个到处 / I've been taking it around.
- `00:19:05,333 → 00:19:06,700` **Jake:** 就看比赛啊 / To watch games and stuff.
- `00:19:07,200 → 00:19:08,800` **Jake:** 进城都带着这个东西 / I take this thing everywhere in the city.
- `00:19:09,000 → 00:19:11,233` **Lucia:** 哦看比赛还可以那 / Oh, so you can watch games with it?
- `00:19:11,666 → 00:19:13,066` **Lucia:** 那会把比赛整个 / Does it record the whole game?
- `00:19:13,200 → 00:19:14,666` **Jake:** 不会不会 / No, no.
- `00:19:13,200 → 00:19:14,666` Lucia: 记录建模下来吗 / Jake: No, no.
- `00:19:14,833 → 00:19:17,466` **Jake:** 这个不会这个就版权的问题 / No, it can't, due to copyright issues.
- `00:19:17,466 → 00:19:19,466` **Jake:** 只能扫扫进场的仪式 / It can only scan the entrance ceremony.
- `00:19:18,733 → 00:19:19,466` **Tasha:** 那 / So
- `00:19:19,566 → 00:19:22,833` **Tasha:** 那看电影的时候能把电影记下来吗 / Can it record movies when watching in theaters?
- `00:19:20,333 → 00:19:21,633` **Alice:** 这个放别的地 / Put this somewhere else.
- `00:19:23,166 → 00:19:23,966` **Lucia:** 那也不行吧 / That's not okay either, right?
- `00:19:23,966 → 00:19:25,266` **Lucia:** 也牵涉版权哦 / It involves copyright too.
- `00:19:24,966 → 00:19:26,100` **Tasha:** 这不就是枪版 / Isn't that just a bootleg?
- `00:19:26,733 → 00:19:27,933` **Jake:** 哈哈哈这就是枪版 / Hahaha, that's a bootleg.
- `00:19:27,566 → 00:19:29,233` **Lucia:** 3D枪版 / 3D bootleg.
- `00:19:28,233 → 00:19:29,966` **Jake:** 这就是枪版 / That's a bootleg.
- `00:19:29,400 → 00:19:31,100` **Lucia:** 给你秀一个 实景的  坐在电影院的体验 / I'll show you a real-life experience of sitting in a theater.
- `00:19:31,666 → 00:19:32,533` **Jake:** 哎是的是的 / Yes, yes.
- `00:19:32,733 → 00:19:34,600` **Jake:** 我带一个这个进去给你打出来 / I'll take this in and print it out for you.
- `00:19:35,333 → 00:19:36,733` **Jake:** 哈哈我就这个装备进电影院 / Haha, I'm taking this gear into the cinema.
- `00:19:44,166 → 00:19:45,533` **Jake:** 感觉两条腿都要断 / Feels like my legs are going to break.
- `00:19:47,966 → 00:19:50,200` **Shure:** 再去拿两个袋 / Go grab two more bags.
- `00:19:51,533 → 00:19:52,466` **Jake:** 耍赖吗 / Are you cheating?
- `00:19:53,400 → 00:19:54,766` **Jake:** 这边有冰块 / There are ice packs here.
- `00:19:56,233 → 00:19:57,233` **Jake:** 保鲜冰块 / Fresh-keeping ice packs.
- `00:19:57,233 → 00:19:58,500` **Jake:** 保鲜冰块能吃吗 / Can we eat these fresh-keeping ice packs?
- `00:19:58,700 → 00:20:00,066` **Alice:** 呃不能吃 / Uh, no, you can't eat them.
- `00:20:00,266 → 00:20:01,100` **Jake:** 好吧好吧好吧 / Okay, okay, okay.
- `00:20:01,300 → 00:20:02,333` **Tasha:** 但是你可以带 / But you can bring them.
- `00:20:02,566 → 00:20:03,466` **Tasha:** 呃也不用吧 / Uh, no need actually.
- `00:20:03,466 → 00:20:04,600` **Jake:** 我们用不着吧 / We don't need them, do we?
- `00:20:05,166 → 00:20:05,833` **Jake:** 我们 / We...
- `00:20:13,933 → 00:20:14,866` **Jake:** 编辑一下 / Edit it a bit.
- `00:20:15,300 → 00:20:16,200` **Lucia:** 矫正视力 / Correct the vision.
- `00:20:17,533 → 00:20:19,500` **Jake:** 我们视力都有问题 / We all have vision problems.
- `00:20:19,533 → 00:20:22,200` **Jake:** 就是我们6个人一样的问题 / All six of us have the same problem.
- `00:20:22,433 → 00:20:23,233` **Jake:** 病友 / We're like patients.
- `00:20:27,766 → 00:20:28,366` **Jake:** 呃对 / Uh, right.
- `00:20:30,666 → 00:20:33,433` **Jake:** 对呃这是全景 / Yeah, this is the panoramic view.
- `00:20:34,966 → 00:20:36,733` **Tasha:** 博主探店的 / The blogger visiting the store.
- `00:20:35,866 → 00:20:37,166` **Jake:** 博主博主 / Blogger, blogger.
- `00:20:37,166 → 00:20:39,000` **Jake:** 对对6人博主 / Yeah, a blogger with six people.
- `00:20:40,366 → 00:20:42,266` **Jake:** 对网上见就是好吧 / Yeah, seen online, okay.
- `00:20:44,033 → 00:20:46,800` **Jake:** 哈哈哈妈的 自己开个超市是吧 / Hahaha, damn it, are we opening our own supermarket?
- `00:20:46,866 → 00:20:47,366` **Jake:** 自己开个盒马 / Opening our own Hema.
- `00:20:47,833 → 00:20:49,533` **Lucia:** 同时探店这个也做美食 / While visiting the store, also doing food reviews.
- `00:20:49,533 → 00:20:52,866` **Lucia:** 哈哈我们有美食专家 / Haha, we have a food expert.
- `00:20:50,500 → 00:20:50,900` **Jake:** 对 / Right.
- `00:20:56,433 → 00:20:58,800` **Tasha:** 哎等一下别再扫了 / Hey, wait, don't scan anymore.
- `00:20:58,800 → 00:21:00,433` **Tasha:** 还还可以用下一个券呢 / We can still use the next coupon.
- `00:21:00,500 → 00:21:01,766` **Alice:** 还没到500呢 / We haven't reached 500 yet.
- `00:21:01,766 → 00:21:02,800` **Tasha:** 不用到500 / No need to reach 500.
- `00:21:02,800 → 00:21:04,233` **Tasha:** 满20 哈哈哈 / Spend 20, hahaha.
- `00:21:05,233 → 00:21:05,933` **Alice:** 来最后一个 / Okay, last one.
- `00:21:05,933 → 00:21:06,700` **Alice:** 随便拿几个券 / Just grab a few coupons.
- `00:21:06,200 → 00:21:09,866` **Jake:** 哈哈哈满20满20 / Hahaha, spend 20, spend 20.
- `00:21:07,233 → 00:21:08,266` **Tasha:** 我不确定满多少 / I'm not sure how much.
- `00:21:08,266 → 00:21:08,900` **Tasha:** 我看一下 / Let me check.
- `00:21:09,866 → 00:21:11,633` **Alice:** 你先你先把那个满500的那个10块钱用了 / You first, you first use that $10 off on $500.
- `00:21:09,866 → 00:21:11,933` **Jake:** 凑到了500 / Reached $500.
- `00:21:11,666 → 00:21:13,733` **Tasha:** 满49和满29哎 / $49 off and $29 off, hey.
- `00:21:14,566 → 00:21:15,466` **Jake:** 先用49的 / Use the $49 off first.
- `00:21:14,700 → 00:21:16,333` **Tasha:** 满29的水果券 / $29 off fruit coupon.
- `00:21:15,466 → 00:21:16,533` **Jake:** 先用49的 / Use the $49 off first.
- `00:21:18,866 → 00:21:19,933` **Shure:** 哎差不多差不多 / Yeah, that's about right.
- `00:21:19,933 → 00:21:21,066` **Shure:** 无所谓无所谓 / It doesn't matter.
- `00:21:21,166 → 00:21:23,033` **Jake:** 他一天能扫这么多 / He can scan so many in a day.
- `00:21:22,300 → 00:21:23,566` **Tasha:** 还有一个海鲜券 / There's also a seafood coupon.
- `00:21:23,800 → 00:21:24,933` **Alice:** 有海鲜吗 / Do we have seafood?
- `00:21:24,000 → 00:21:26,033` **Shure:** 给杨老板省了不到一新币 / Saved Jake less than one SGD.
- `00:21:25,700 → 00:21:27,166` **Katrina:** 海鲜是不是扫完了 / Is the seafood scanned already?
- `00:21:27,966 → 00:21:29,333` **Alice:** 我们买海鲜了吗 / Did we buy seafood?
- `00:21:29,000 → 00:21:30,133` **Katrina:** 没有买海鲜 / No, we didn't buy seafood.
- `00:21:29,700 → 00:21:31,266` **Jake:** 一新币很重要的 / One SGD is very important.
- `00:21:31,266 → 00:21:32,833` **Jake:** 一新币可以 / One SGD can...
- `00:21:32,933 → 00:21:34,133` **Shure:** 可口可乐能买吗 / Can it buy Coca-Cola?
- `00:21:34,033 → 00:21:34,666` **Jake:** 买不了哈哈哈 / No, it can't, haha.
- `00:21:35,500 → 00:21:36,866` **Tasha:** 虾滑算海鲜吗 / Does shrimp paste count as seafood?
- `00:21:36,866 → 00:21:39,033` **Tasha:** 完了那部分被我 / Oh no, that part was...
- `00:21:39,233 → 00:21:40,133` **Shure:** 没事的没事的 / It's okay, it's okay.
- `00:21:39,666 → 00:21:40,466` **Katrina:** 算了吧 / Forget it.
- `00:21:40,133 → 00:21:40,766` **Shure:** 就这样吧 / Let's leave it at that.
- `00:21:42,300 → 00:21:46,333` **Jake:** 1新币能干嘛呢 / What can one SGD do?
- `00:21:47,066 → 00:21:48,200` **Tasha:** 这个是不是扫过了 / Has this been scanned?
- `00:21:47,566 → 00:21:50,166` **Alice:** 1新币能坐公交或许 / One SGD can take the bus, maybe.
- `00:21:50,033 → 00:21:51,433` **Jake:** 坐不了哈哈 / Can't take the bus, haha.
- `00:21:55,133 → 00:21:56,500` **Jake:** 呃得一块七 / Uh, it costs 1.7.
- `00:21:56,966 → 00:21:57,766` **Jake:** 对 / Yes.
- `00:21:58,333 → 00:21:59,400` **Shure:** 你又加了一个 / You added another one.
- `00:21:59,200 → 00:22:00,000` **Jake:** 对 / Yes.
- `00:22:00,766 → 00:22:02,866` **Katrina:** 那个但是他是这个吗 / But is it this one though?
- `00:22:03,700 → 00:22:05,600` **Katrina:** 他们那边公交路上 / On their bus route...
- `00:22:04,800 → 00:22:05,266` **Shure:** 没有没有 / No, no.
- `00:22:05,466 → 00:22:06,200` **Shure:** 不是不是 / It's not.
- `00:22:06,666 → 00:22:07,733` **Jake:** 不知道耶 / I don't know.
- `00:22:08,466 → 00:22:10,066` **Katrina:** 这没扫吧 / This hasn't been scanned, right?
- `00:22:08,633 → 00:22:09,433` **Jake:** 可能 / Maybe.
- `00:22:12,100 → 00:22:13,000` **Jake:** 国内也是两块钱 / It's two RMB in China too.
- `00:22:13,000 → 00:22:13,800` **Jake:** 没错没错 / That's right, that's right.
- `00:22:13,800 → 00:22:14,400` **Jake:** 这边也是两 / It's two here as well.
- `00:22:14,400 → 00:22:15,233` **Jake:** 那边也两块钱 / And two over there as well.
- `00:22:15,233 → 00:22:16,766` **Alice:** 国内刷支付宝只要这 / Using Alipay domestically only costs this much.
- `00:22:17,466 → 00:22:18,633` **Alice:** 这个装巨多饮料 / This can hold a ton of drinks.
- `00:22:20,433 → 00:22:21,533` 小薇 : 扫过了这都是扫过的 / Tasha: Scanned already, all these are scanned.
- `00:22:22,100 → 00:22:23,633` **Katrina:** 桌上都是扫过的 / Everything on the table is scanned.
- `00:22:24,766 → 00:22:26,233` **Tasha:** 这个真的还能装沉的吗 / Can this really hold that much weight?
- `00:22:30,800 → 00:22:32,466` **Tasha:** 别别别压他了别压他了 / Don't press it down, don't press it down.
- `00:22:32,633 → 00:22:35,600` **Tasha:** 好了万一托着底抱托着底抱 / Okay, just support it from the bottom, support it from the bottom.
- `00:22:39,866 → 00:22:41,066` **Tasha:** 能扎上吗 / Can it be tied?
- `00:22:41,200 → 00:22:42,100` **Jake:** 我来扎我来扎 / I'll tie it, I'll tie it.
- `00:22:41,866 → 00:22:43,333` **Lucia:** 这个这把这个拿起来 / Take this, lift it up.
- `00:22:43,333 → 00:22:44,766` **Lucia:** 然后把这个放在这扎 / And then put this here to tie it.
- `00:22:44,766 → 00:22:45,866` **Lucia:** 可能好扎一些 / It might be easier to tie.
- `00:22:46,866 → 00:22:48,433` **Jake:** 哎呦进不去 / Oh no, it won't go in.
- `00:22:48,433 → 00:22:50,433` **Lucia:** 这个好重这个袋子 / This bag is really heavy.
- `00:22:50,866 → 00:22:52,133` **Jake:** 分两袋吧 / Let's split it into two bags.
- `00:22:52,766 → 00:22:53,866` **Tasha:** 分两车吧 / Let's split it into two cars.
- `00:22:55,266 → 00:22:56,100` **Jake:** 再来辆车 / Get another car.
- `00:22:56,100 → 00:22:57,033` **Jake:** 我去开车 / I'll go drive.
- `00:22:58,033 → 00:22:59,000` **Alice:** 你去吧 / Go ahead.
- `00:23:30,500 → 00:23:31,266` **Jake:** 车到了 / The car is here.
- `00:23:31,433 → 00:23:33,566` **Lucia:** 要要不要再拿瓶饮料出来 / Do you want to take out another bottle of drink?
- `00:23:41,533 → 00:23:42,833` **Katrina:** 他没有布袋子吗 / Doesn't he have a cloth bag?
- `00:23:42,833 → 00:23:44,333` **Katrina:** 只有这种袋子 / Only these kinds of bags?
- `00:23:45,733 → 00:23:47,700` **Tasha:** 能不能租个推车回去 / Can we rent a cart to take back?
- `00:23:49,200 → 00:23:50,000` **Jake:** 哦 / Oh.
- `00:23:51,000 → 00:23:52,033` **Lucia:** 谢谢会不会有点重啊 / Thanks, isn't it a bit heavy?
- `00:23:52,400 → 00:23:54,600` **Tasha:** 你不会在思考这个可行性吧 / Are you seriously considering the feasibility of this?
- `00:23:52,666 → 00:23:53,633` **Jake:** 确实 / Indeed.
- `00:23:54,466 → 00:23:56,433` **Jake:** 对 可以吗 / Right. Is it okay?
- `00:23:56,900 → 00:23:57,766` **Tasha:** 不可以 / No.
- `00:23:57,966 → 00:23:59,200` **Jake:** 真的吗问问 / Really? Ask around.
- `00:23:59,200 → 00:24:01,533` **Jake:** 哎呦sorry不好意思不好意思 / Oh, sorry, my bad, my bad.
- `00:24:02,533 → 00:24:03,500` **Lucia:** 这都是一些怕压的东西 / These are all things that can't be pressed.
- `00:24:07,166 → 00:24:08,500` **Jake:** 这能租推车吗 / Can we rent a cart?
- `00:24:11,566 → 00:24:12,633` **Jake:** 那那好吧 / Well, okay then.
- `00:24:13,033 → 00:24:14,566` **Jake:** 那上哪租你知道吗 / Do you know where to rent it?
- `00:24:15,166 → 00:24:15,966` **Jake:** 不知道 / No idea.
- `00:24:17,666 → 00:24:18,466` **Jake:** 好吧 / Okay
- `00:24:23,633 → 00:24:24,733` **Jake:** 他这不行 / This won't work
- `00:24:25,833 → 00:24:27,000` **Tasha:** 你摄像头呢 / Where's your camera?
- `00:24:27,133 → 00:24:28,766` **Jake:** 呃换一个 / Uh, change it
- `00:24:28,766 → 00:24:29,666` **Jake:** 这个没电了 / This one's out of battery
- `00:24:29,733 → 00:24:31,100` **Tasha:** 这些先放车里吧 / Let's put these in the car first
- `00:24:31,100 → 00:24:31,900` **Jake:** 头掉了 / The head fell off
- `00:24:32,066 → 00:24:33,233` **Katrina:** 来吧放这去 / Come on, put it here
- `00:24:47,566 → 00:24:49,366` **Lucia:** 我们还有袋子吗 / Do we have any more bags?
- `00:24:49,366 → 00:24:51,100` **Lucia:** 没有的话应该也能装下 / If not, we can still fit it
- `00:24:51,100 → 00:24:52,600` **Lucia:** 对要不把这个 / Yeah, maybe use this
- `00:25:09,700 → 00:25:10,466` **Katrina:** 付钱喽 / Time to pay
- `00:25:10,500 → 00:25:11,300` **Jake:** 送的呀 / It's free
- `00:25:11,566 → 00:25:12,433` **Alice:** 付钱喽 / Time to pay
- `00:25:12,166 → 00:25:12,766` **Jake:** 要买 / We have to buy it
- `00:25:13,533 → 00:25:16,300` **Tasha:** 9块钱要买厨房湿巾吗 / Do we need to buy kitchen wipes for 9 yuan?
- `00:25:16,300 → 00:25:17,233` **Shure:** 来拿着 / Here, take it
- `00:25:18,466 → 00:25:19,900` **Tasha:** 因为不是吃火锅吗 / Because we're having hotpot, right?
- `00:25:20,100 → 00:25:20,466` **Shure:** 好 / Okay
- `00:25:21,166 → 00:25:21,600` **Jake:** 行 / Alright
- `00:25:26,066 → 00:25:27,033` **Tasha:** 9块钱好像 / 9 yuan seems like a lot
- `00:25:32,066 → 00:25:33,666` **Tasha:** 我真的还有这么多钱吗 / Do I really still have that much money?
- `00:25:34,900 → 00:25:36,633` **Shure:** 我相信你是个大富婆 / I believe you're really rich
- `00:25:38,833 → 00:25:39,566` **Tasha:** 你信错了 / You believe wrong
- `00:25:49,366 → 00:25:51,200` **Lucia:** 这还有有包葱 / There's a bag of green onions here
- `00:25:52,333 → 00:25:53,433` **Tasha:** 这结了吗 / Is this settled?
- `00:25:53,200 → 00:25:54,233` **Lucia:** 这是蒜 / This is garlic
- `00:25:54,900 → 00:25:55,966` **Alice:** 这不是葱吗 / Isn't this green onion?
- `00:25:56,033 → 00:25:57,866` **Lucia:** 这不是大蒜苗吗 / This is garlic sprouts
- `00:25:59,566 → 00:26:00,433` **Alice:** 一样的一样的 / Same thing, same thing
- `00:26:00,466 → 00:26:01,266` **Tasha:** 无所谓 / Whatever
- `00:26:01,466 → 00:26:03,466` **Shure:** 谁不是谁把葱 / Who cares, who brought the green onions?
- `00:26:04,800 → 00:26:05,633` **Lucia:** 再买一个吧 / Let's buy another one
- `00:26:05,633 → 00:26:06,333` **Tasha:** 可以可以买 / Sure, we can buy it
- `00:26:05,733 → 00:26:07,766` **Lucia:** 啊不是我觉得他是葱 / Ah no, I think this is green onion
- `00:26:09,633 → 00:26:10,933` **Tasha:** 蒜苗就是蒜苗 / Garlic sprouts are garlic sprouts
- `00:26:10,933 → 00:26:11,733` **Shure:** 蒜苗就是蒜苗 / Garlic sprouts are garlic sprouts
- `00:26:11,733 → 00:26:13,133` **Tasha:** 这这是蒜苗呀 / This, this is garlic sprouts
- `00:26:14,100 → 00:26:14,633` **Alice:** 是是蒜苗 / Yes, it's garlic sprouts
- `00:26:15,066 → 00:26:16,633` **Shure:** 完全不是一个味 / They taste completely different
- `00:26:17,900 → 00:26:19,333` **Alice:** 无所谓把它当葱用吧 / Doesn't matter, just use it like green onions.
- `00:26:20,266 → 00:26:22,066` **Shure:** 咱俩完全不是一个味啊 / We totally have different tastes.
- `00:26:22,200 → 00:26:23,400` **Tasha:** 不是一个味啊 / Different tastes.
- `00:26:23,633 → 00:26:24,800` **Shure:** 蒜苗是蒜的味 / Garlic sprouts taste like garlic.
- `00:26:24,900 → 00:26:26,166` **Lucia:** 我刚才拿起来突然一看 / I just picked it up and suddenly saw.
- `00:26:26,300 → 00:26:27,433` **Lucia:** 这是到底是什么东西 / What is this thing exactly?
- `00:26:26,600 → 00:26:28,900` **Shure:** 另一张另一张账单在谁那呢 / Where is the other bill?
- `00:26:27,433 → 00:26:28,600` **Tasha:** 再再买个葱吗 / Should we buy more green onions?
- `00:26:28,600 → 00:26:29,400` **Jake:** 可以啊 / Sure.
- `00:26:30,366 → 00:26:31,433` **Katrina:** 就两个呀 / Just two?
- `00:26:31,566 → 00:26:32,866` **Shure:** 还有一张账单啊 / There's another bill.
- `00:26:34,066 → 00:26:35,500` **Tasha:** 第一个打了 / The first one is done.
- `00:26:35,500 → 00:26:36,700` **Tasha:** 谁拿着呢 / Who has it?
- `00:26:37,166 → 00:26:38,400` **Jake:** 我我没拿呀 / I don't have it.
- `00:26:38,566 → 00:26:40,333` **Shure:** 忘忘塞到哪了 / Forgot where it was put.
- `00:26:40,100 → 00:26:41,000` **Jake:** 我可能没拿吧 / I probably didn't take it.
- `00:26:41,000 → 00:26:41,933` **Tasha:** 我没拿呀 / I didn't take it.
- `00:26:42,100 → 00:26:42,733` **Jake:** 我应该没拿 / I should not have taken it.
- `00:26:42,700 → 00:26:43,633` **Tasha:** 我应该没拿呀 / I should not have taken it.
- `00:26:43,633 → 00:26:45,033` **Jake:** 那可能就是拿了吧 / Then maybe someone took it.
- `00:26:44,233 → 00:26:45,333` **Alice:** 这不两条吗 / Aren't there two?
- `00:26:46,266 → 00:26:48,100` **Jake:** 两条吗我去 / Two? Oh my.
- `00:26:47,500 → 00:26:49,200` **Lucia:** 哎那怎么又变成两条了 / Hey, how did it become two?
- `00:26:48,433 → 00:26:50,833` **Shure:** 本来就两条 / There were two to begin with.
- `00:26:49,200 → 00:26:50,700` **Lucia:** 还有一条 / There’s another one.
- `00:26:50,466 → 00:26:51,300` **Jake:** 应该连起来 / Should be combined.
- `00:26:51,666 → 00:26:52,433` **Lucia:** 本来就两条 / There were two from the start.
- `00:26:52,666 → 00:26:54,066` **Alice:** 哎哎哎去那边葱 / Hey hey hey, go get the green onions.
- `00:26:54,766 → 00:26:55,766` **Jake:** 订一下 / Order it.
- `00:26:55,033 → 00:26:56,366` **Alice:** 有机葱比较贵 / Organic green onions are more expensive.
- `00:26:56,833 → 00:26:57,633` **Jake:** 哎呦我去 / Oh my.
- `00:27:01,000 → 00:27:02,833` **Lucia:** 结过账的东西要看好 / Watch the things that have already been checked out.
- `00:27:07,866 → 00:27:08,766` **Jake:** 去贴门禁 / Go stick the access control.
- `00:27:09,133 → 00:27:10,633` **Shure:** 布袋是热啊 / The bag is hot.
- `00:27:11,300 → 00:27:14,700` **Katrina:** 绝对不能让任何一个蛋受到伤害 / Absolutely cannot let any egg get damaged.
- `00:27:16,633 → 00:27:17,100` **Katrina:** 封条 / Seal it.
- `00:27:17,200 → 00:27:17,666` **Jake:** 那不多 / Not much.
- `00:27:18,033 → 00:27:18,533` **Shure:** 绶带 / Ribbon.
- `00:27:32,266 → 00:27:33,900` **Jake:** 呃买二得三 / Uh, buy two get three.
- `00:27:33,900 → 00:27:37,100` **Katrina:** 你在英国呆有没有人就是抢 / Have you ever had anyone just snatch things in the UK?
- `00:27:37,100 → 00:27:40,033` **Katrina:** 有没有人就是抢这个 / Is there anyone grabbing this?
- `00:27:44,166 → 00:27:45,933` **Jake:** 总共就两条吧 / There are only two, right?
- `00:27:46,133 → 00:27:48,933` **Lucia:** 哦好像有这种事吧但我没有 / Oh, it seems like that happens, but I haven't.
- `00:27:52,533 → 00:27:54,466` **Lucia:** 据说有我们可以讨论这个吗 / I've heard of it. Can we talk about this?
- `00:27:55,933 → 00:27:56,800` **Jake:** 可以可以可以 / Sure, sure, sure.
- `00:28:02,266 → 00:28:03,600` **Lucia:** 涉嫌什么谈论 / Suspected of talking about what?
- `00:28:03,600 → 00:28:04,600` **Lucia:** 谈论那个 / Talking about that.
- `00:28:06,800 → 00:28:08,300` **Lucia:** 哈哈哈说一些不好的话 / Hahaha, say something bad.
- `00:28:06,800 → 00:28:11,300` **Jake:** 没关系就这个翻译给他乱翻译一个 / It's fine, just translate it randomly for him.
- `00:28:13,433 → 00:28:15,233` **Jake:** American is the best / American is the best.
- `00:28:17,066 → 00:28:18,200` **Lucia:** 会翻译成这样吗 / Will it be translated like that?
- `00:28:18,666 → 00:28:21,266` **Alice:** 这怎么拿回去啊 / How can we take this back?
- `00:28:21,766 → 00:28:22,600` **Katrina:** 一人一个 / One for each person.
- `00:28:29,133 → 00:28:31,766` **Jake:** 翻译翻过去就是美国的东西很便宜 / Translate it as American stuff is cheap.
- `00:28:32,933 → 00:28:35,800` **Jake:** 大家喜欢买便宜的东西 / Everyone likes to buy cheap things.
- `00:28:36,466 → 00:28:38,900` **Lucia:** 大家喜欢花最低的价格买东西 / People like to buy things at the lowest price.
- `00:28:37,900 → 00:28:39,366` **Jake:** 对对对 / Yeah, yeah, yeah.
- `00:28:40,933 → 00:28:42,033` **Jake:** 经济很好 / The economy is great.
- `00:28:51,166 → 00:28:52,866` **Jake:** 让眼镜记一下 / Let the glasses guy note this.
- `00:29:30,633 → 00:29:32,033` **Tasha:** 我帮你贴上吗 / Want me to stick it on for you?
- `00:29:32,666 → 00:29:34,466` **Jake:** 好啊哈哈哈 / Sure, hahaha.
- `00:29:34,866 → 00:29:35,200` **Jake:** 你就你就 / You can, you can.
- `00:29:35,466 → 00:29:36,933` **Jake:** 你就确实可以当 / You can really do it.
- `00:29:36,933 → 00:29:38,566` **Jake:** 绶带现在这个长度很好 / The sash length is just right.
- `00:29:38,733 → 00:29:40,033` **Jake:** 就这样 / Just like this.
- `00:29:40,333 → 00:29:41,133` **Alice:** 走吧 / Let's go.
- `00:29:43,100 → 00:29:43,900` **Jake:** 走 / Let's go.
- `00:29:48,333 → 00:29:49,766` **Jake:** 这车能推到哪啊 / Where can we push this cart?
- `00:29:50,300 → 00:29:51,033` **Shure:** 门口 / To the door.
- `00:29:51,300 → 00:29:52,133` **Jake:** 门口 / The door.
- `00:29:52,133 → 00:29:53,400` **Jake:** 哪个门口外边 / Which door, outside?
- `00:30:05,466 → 00:30:07,000` **Tasha:** 肯定也会在网上买的吧 / Surely they'll buy it online too, right?
- `00:30:19,500 → 00:30:21,900` **Tasha:** 车不能推到商场门口吗 / Can't the cart be pushed to the mall entrance?
- `00:30:24,200 → 00:30:25,500` **Jake:** 能租车 / You can rent a car.
- `00:30:28,466 → 00:30:30,233` **Shure:** 杨老板先把车打上吧 / Jake, start the car first.
- `00:30:30,400 → 00:30:31,033` **Jake:** 好的 / Okay.
- `00:30:35,566 → 00:30:37,266` **Jake:** 不再买点咖啡什么的 / Should we buy some coffee or something?
- `00:30:38,633 → 00:30:39,833` **Jake:** 现在有点晚了是吧 / It's a bit late now, right?
- `00:30:41,000 → 00:30:42,966` **Katrina:** 我们直接去吃饭是吗 / Are we going straight to eat?
- `00:30:41,333 → 00:30:42,133` **Jake:** 好的 / Okay
- `00:30:43,400 → 00:30:44,566` **Tasha:** 对吃什么呢 / What are we going to eat?
- `00:30:44,933 → 00:30:46,433` **Shure:** 你们拎着这么多东西 / You guys are carrying so much stuff.
- `00:30:46,433 → 00:30:47,600` **Alice:** 这地能有放的吗 / Is there any place to put this?
- `00:30:48,333 → 00:30:49,266` **Jake:** 确实啊 / Yeah, seriously.
- `00:30:49,400 → 00:30:51,166` **Shure:** 肯定得回趟家 / We definitely need to go home first.
- `00:30:50,466 → 00:30:53,566` **Lucia:** 拿这么多东西去吃饭有点 / It's a bit much to take all this stuff out to eat.
- `00:30:51,733 → 00:30:54,933` **Tasha:** 要不就点外卖吧 / How about we just order takeout?
- `00:30:55,600 → 00:30:56,733` **Jake:** 行回去点外卖吧 / Sure, let's go home and order takeout.
- `00:30:57,133 → 00:30:57,566` **Jake:** 可以吧 / Is that okay?
- `00:30:58,266 → 00:30:59,000` **Lucia:** 都可以一起出去也可以 / We can all go out together too.
- `00:31:00,700 → 00:31:03,533` **Jake:** 在这是就是有冻的东西对吧 / The thing is, we have frozen items here, right?
- `00:31:04,200 → 00:31:05,600` **Jake:** 是吧是有冷冻的 / Right, we do have frozen items.
- `00:31:04,200 → 00:31:06,733` **Shure:** 是吧而且还有要要冻的东西 / Yeah, and we also have stuff that needs to be frozen.
- `00:31:06,733 → 00:31:07,533` **Jake:** 冷冻的东西 / Frozen items.
- `00:31:07,066 → 00:31:09,800` **Shure:** 然后大家看一眼那个哪些是冷冻 / So everyone take a look and see which items are frozen.
- `00:31:09,800 → 00:31:10,733` **Shure:** 哪些是冷藏 / And which ones are refrigerated.
- `00:31:11,466 → 00:31:12,433` **Shure:** 比如说那毛肚什么的 / For example, like the tripe.
- `00:31:12,433 → 00:31:14,500` **Shure:** 他就是说让0-4度 / It needs to be kept at 0-4 degrees.
- `00:31:13,566 → 00:31:14,600` **Tasha:** 你要放下来吗 / Do you want to put it down?
- `00:31:15,033 → 00:31:16,166` **Jake:** 啊再来一辆车你要不 / Yeah, do you need another car?
- `00:31:17,100 → 00:31:18,733` **Shure:** 他只能到这儿了 / It can only go up to here.
- `00:31:18,966 → 00:31:20,266` **Tasha:** 他只能到这儿了 / It can only go up to here.
- `00:31:20,733 → 00:31:22,733` **Shure:** 爸爸只能送你到这儿了 / Dad can only take you this far.
- `00:31:23,166 → 00:31:25,166` **Shure:** 未来的路要靠你自己了 / The rest of the journey is up to you.
- `00:31:28,833 → 00:31:29,633` **Shure:** 听到了吗 / Did you hear that?
- `00:31:31,133 → 00:31:33,300` **Shure:** 他现在向你发出回应 / Now he is responding to you.
- `00:31:34,200 → 00:31:36,166` **Katrina:** 这样我们的冰箱就满了 / So now our fridge is full.
- `00:31:38,133 → 00:31:39,633` **Lucia:** 冰箱能不能放下呀 / Can the fridge handle all this?
- `00:31:42,000 → 00:31:42,966` **Katrina:** 哈哈哈全喝光 / Hahaha, drink it all up.
- `00:31:43,233 → 00:31:43,566` **Jake:** 回头 / Later.
- `00:31:45,433 → 00:31:47,733` **Jake:** 就是这个数据比较重要 / This data is quite important.
- `00:31:47,833 → 00:31:49,833` **Jake:** 就是回头你们收拾的时候 / When you guys are organizing later
- `00:31:49,833 → 00:31:51,133` **Jake:** 会和超市 / it will connect with the supermarket.
- `00:31:51,133 → 00:31:52,966` **Jake:** 你们的一些行为建立一些联系 / Some of your actions will be linked.
- `00:31:54,900 → 00:31:57,033` **Jake:** 就是比方说你把这东西放到冰箱 / For instance, when you put this stuff in the fridge
- `00:31:57,033 → 00:32:00,400` **Jake:** 然后他只能找回到你在哪找到的那个 / it can only trace back to where you found it.
- `00:32:00,866 → 00:32:04,233` **Lucia:** 哦那就跟亚马逊那个无人采购超市 / Oh, like Amazon's cashier-less stores.
- `00:32:04,233 → 00:32:05,966` **Lucia:** 一样的那种感觉 / It feels like that.
- `00:32:05,633 → 00:32:06,733` **Jake:** 对对对溯源 / Yeah, exactly, traceability.
- `00:32:06,733 → 00:32:09,133` **Jake:** 对就是溯源这种rechieve / Yes, it's about traceability, this rechieve
- `00:32:09,966 → 00:32:10,900` **Shure:** 你们上链的吗 / Are you guys going on-chain?
- `00:32:11,633 → 00:32:12,333` **Katrina:** 当心哦 / Be careful
- `00:32:14,133 → 00:32:15,500` **Lucia:** 阿莫总开了好几个那种 / Amos has opened several of those
- `00:32:15,500 → 00:32:16,033` **Lucia:** 那种超市 / Those supermarkets
- `00:32:16,033 → 00:32:17,433` **Lucia:** 就是你出门不用结账 / Where you don't need to check out
- `00:32:17,433 → 00:32:18,666` **Lucia:** 他知道你买了什么 / It knows what you bought
- `00:32:18,666 → 00:32:20,433` **Lucia:** 然后自动在你的账户里付款 / And it automatically charges your account
- `00:32:21,766 → 00:32:23,033` **Lucia:** 就是他根据你的重量 / Based on the weight
- `00:32:23,033 → 00:32:25,200` **Lucia:** 好像就是你放进去然后东西多重 / Like you put stuff in and it calculates the weight
- `00:32:25,200 → 00:32:27,566` **Lucia:** 对他会精确到多少多少毫克 / Yes, it can be accurate to the milligram
- `00:32:27,566 → 00:32:30,066` **Lucia:** 然后呢他就会自动把这钱扣走 / And then it automatically deducts the money
- `00:32:30,566 → 00:32:31,166` **Lucia:** 就跟我们一样 / Just like us
- `00:32:31,166 → 00:32:32,433` **Jake:** 那我要稍微哎 / Then I need to, uh
- `00:32:31,600 → 00:32:33,133` **Lucia:** 就是它有很多摄像头 / It has lots of cameras
- `00:32:33,633 → 00:32:34,933` **Lucia:** 就是能看到你拿了什么 / It can see what you took
- `00:32:34,966 → 00:32:37,033` **Lucia:** 他监测你有没有在货架上拿东西 / It monitors if you took stuff from the shelves
- `00:32:37,133 → 00:32:39,933` **Lucia:** 包括你进出的时候的各种数据 / Including all kinds of data when you enter and exit
- `00:32:39,933 → 00:32:41,600` **Lucia:** 反正很神奇的一个东西 / It's a really amazing thing
- `00:32:41,600 → 00:32:43,433` **Lucia:** 不过就是偶尔有算错的可能性 / But there's a chance it makes mistakes sometimes
- `00:32:46,733 → 00:32:48,133` **Shure:** 因为每一个我都这么拿 / Because I take every single one like this
- `00:32:49,966 → 00:32:52,233` **Lucia:** 哎那个好像他也能监测的到 / Hmm, it seems like it can monitor that too
- `00:32:52,866 → 00:32:54,866` **Lucia:** 呵呵不过有时候是会算错的 / Haha, but sometimes it does make mistakes
- `00:32:55,233 → 00:32:56,200` **Shure:** 我打个雨伞呐 / I'll grab an umbrella
- `00:32:57,733 → 00:32:58,733` **Lucia:** 去实验一下 / Go test it out
- `00:33:01,900 → 00:33:03,333` **Shure:** 就是这是他的玻璃 / This is his glass
- `00:33:02,500 → 00:33:04,133` **Jake:** 玻璃好歹还在人家 / At least the glass is still there
- `00:33:03,333 → 00:33:04,633` **Jake:** 好歹还算算法错误 / At least it's a calculation error
- `00:33:04,633 → 00:33:06,133` **Shure:** 就是我就爱打伞 / I just love using umbrellas
- `00:33:06,133 → 00:33:06,900` **Shure:** 在室内 / Indoors
- `00:33:07,933 → 00:33:08,800` **Shure:** 是吧 / Right?
- `00:33:10,133 → 00:33:12,100` **Jake:** 他有可能是商品上面贴条了 / It might have RFID tags on the products
- `00:33:12,100 → 00:33:13,066` **Jake:** 贴无线电 / Wireless tags
- `00:33:22,400 → 00:33:23,333` **Jake:** 他还有6分钟到 / He'll arrive in 6 minutes

- `00:29:29,700 → 00:29:30,700` **Alice:** 他喜欢土豆 / He likes potatoes.

- `00:29:32,433 → 00:29:36,533` **Tasha:** 是的只要花298就可以加入我的土门 / Yes, you just have to spend 298 to join my Potato Clan.
- `00:29:33,400 → 00:29:33,733` **Jake:** 嗯 / Hmm.
- `00:29:36,766 → 00:29:38,500` **Tasha:** 哈哈哈 / Hahaha.
- `00:29:38,500 → 00:29:40,933` **Lucia:** 但是因为这个所以叫土豆侠子吗 / But is that why he's called Potato Hero?
- `00:29:38,733 → 00:29:40,233` **Jake:** 天天都可以吃土豆 / He can eat potatoes every day.
- `00:29:40,933 → 00:29:42,700` **Lucia:** 啊就是是因为 / Ah, so it's because...
- `00:29:42,700 → 00:29:43,966` **Lucia:** 你是因为喜欢吃土豆 / You like to eat potatoes?
- `00:29:43,966 → 00:29:45,466` **Lucia:** 所以叫土豆侠子的吗 / So you're called Potato Hero?
- `00:29:45,200 → 00:29:45,466` **Tasha:** 是的 / Yes.
- `00:29:47,966 → 00:29:48,200` **Tasha:** 哈哈 / Haha.
- `00:29:48,033 → 00:29:49,033` **Lucia:** 怎么觉得听起来有点像 / Why does it sound like...
- `00:29:49,033 → 00:29:50,266` **Lucia:** 一个编出来的理由 / A made-up reason.
- `00:29:50,266 → 00:29:51,100` **Lucia:** 哈哈 / Haha.
- `00:29:52,166 → 00:29:56,033` **Jake:** 门店热销土豆红烧肉盖饭就这个吧 / The store's best-selling braised pork with potatoes rice bowl. Let's go with that.
- `00:29:56,066 → 00:29:58,466` **Tasha:** 啊每个人吃自己的饭是吗 / Ah, everyone eats their own meal, right?
- `00:29:58,933 → 00:30:00,300` **Tasha:** 哦也可以哦 / Oh, that's fine too.
- `00:30:00,300 → 00:30:02,166` **Jake:** 你说点这种大菜是吧 / So you want to order a big dish, right?
- `00:30:02,166 → 00:30:03,633` **Tasha:** 哦我以为是点大菜 / Oh, I thought we were ordering a big dish.
- `00:30:03,633 → 00:30:05,266` **Jake:** 哦那也行啊点大菜 / Oh, that works too, let's get a big dish.
- `00:30:05,633 → 00:30:07,300` **Jake:** 那每人点一个吧 / How about everyone orders one dish?
- `00:30:07,500 → 00:30:08,633` **Tasha:** 每人点一个 / Everyone orders one dish?
- `00:30:09,433 → 00:30:10,500` **Tasha:** 嗯四个女生 / Hmm, four girls...
- `00:30:10,500 → 00:30:14,633` **Tasha:** 我感觉吃两到三个菜就差不多了 / I think two or three dishes should be enough.
- `00:30:14,966 → 00:30:16,166` **Jake:** 行 / Okay.
- `00:30:16,166 → 00:30:17,166` **Lucia:** 对嗯差不多 / Yeah, that sounds about right.
- `00:30:17,266 → 00:30:18,366` **Jake:** 那你先点一个 / You go ahead and order one first.
- `00:30:18,666 → 00:30:20,166` **Jake:** 你先点个土豆吧 / You order a potato dish first.
- `00:30:20,766 → 00:30:22,266` **Tasha:** 不 / No.
- `00:30:22,266 → 00:30:25,066` **Jake:** 啊你找一个土豆店不点土豆 / Huh, you come to a potato place and don't order potatoes?
- `00:30:27,100 → 00:30:29,066` **Lucia:** 点一个里面带土豆的菜 / Order a dish with potatoes in it.
- `00:30:30,300 → 00:30:32,100` **Katrina:** 点那种什么肉蟹煲 / Order something like a crab pot.
- `00:30:32,100 → 00:30:33,900` **Katrina:** 什么什么煲里面很多土豆 / Those kinds of pots have a lot of potatoes.
- `00:30:34,500 → 00:30:36,666` **Katrina:** 就是他根本就没有那么多肉 / They barely have any meat in them.
- `00:30:34,800 → 00:30:35,100` **Jake:** 嗯 / Hmm.
- `00:30:36,666 → 00:30:38,466` **Tasha:** 哇感觉好好吃 / Wow, it sounds so delicious.
- `00:30:38,700 → 00:30:39,300` **Jake:** 哈哈 / Haha.
- `00:30:39,300 → 00:30:41,166` **Alice:** 哪个 / Which one?
- `00:30:41,166 → 00:30:43,366` **Jake:** 想象出来的肉蟹煲 / The imagined crab pot.
- `00:30:45,133 → 00:30:46,300` **Jake:** 土豆包 / Potato pot.
- `00:30:46,300 → 00:30:46,900` **Alice:** 吓我一跳 / You scared me.
- `00:30:47,166 → 00:30:47,966` **Tasha:** 记得备注一下不要香菜 / Remember to note no cilantro.
- `00:30:48,033 → 00:30:48,500` **Alice:** 你说啥呀 / What did you say?
- `00:30:49,166 → 00:30:49,633` **Tasha:** 不要啥菜啊 / No cilantro.
- `00:30:49,633 → 00:30:50,233` **Alice:** 你点的啥 / What did you order?
- `00:30:50,233 → 00:30:51,566` **Alice:** 我点了个水煮肉 / I ordered boiled meat.
- `00:33:12,766 → 00:33:13,066` **Jake:** 哎对 / Oh, right.
- `00:33:13,066 → 00:33:15,366` **Jake:** 你们晚上是propose的什么活动来着 / What activity did you propose for tonight?
- `00:33:16,266 → 00:33:18,966` **Tasha:** propose了 / Propose.
- `00:33:17,433 → 00:33:20,066` **Jake:** 你们是逛超市的时候 / When you guys were shopping at the supermarket.
- `00:33:20,066 → 00:33:21,366` **Jake:** propose的两个什么 / What were the two things you proposed?
- `00:33:21,566 → 00:33:22,933` **Jake:** 要做菜做甜点 / Cooking dishes and making desserts.
- `00:33:23,066 → 00:33:24,133` **Jake:** 做半个小时甜点 / Making desserts for half an hour.
- `00:33:24,266 → 00:33:25,533` **Tasha:** 可以啊 / Sounds good.
- `00:33:25,533 → 00:33:25,933` **Jake:** OK / OK.
- `00:33:27,833 → 00:33:28,700` **Alice:** 你为啥不点土豆啊 / Why didn't you order potatoes?
- `00:33:28,700 → 00:33:29,733` **Alice:** 我给你点上吧 / Let me order it for you.
- `00:33:29,933 → 00:33:32,466` **Alice:** 哈哈排骨土豆炖豆角 / Haha, ribs with potatoes and beans.
- `00:33:30,000 → 00:33:31,033` **Tasha:** 哈哈 / Haha
- `00:33:32,466 → 00:33:33,633` **Alice:** 还有啥土豆啊 / What other potatoes?
- `00:33:34,466 → 00:33:35,766` **Tasha:** 都可以都可以 / Anything is fine.
- `00:33:36,066 → 00:33:36,900` **Alice:** 排骨土豆 / Pork ribs with potatoes.
- `00:33:36,900 → 00:33:38,266` **Tasha:** 要不要吃回锅肉 / How about twice-cooked pork?
- `00:33:38,866 → 00:33:39,966` **Alice:** 那你不要你的土豆了 / Then you don't want your potatoes?
- `00:33:39,966 → 00:33:40,666` **Tasha:** 可以不要 / It's okay not to have them.
- `00:33:40,666 → 00:33:42,266` **Tasha:** 哈哈回锅肉 / Haha, twice-cooked pork.
- `00:33:42,666 → 00:33:44,333` **Tasha:** 锅包肉锅包肉 / Sweet and sour pork!
- `00:33:46,133 → 00:33:47,533` **Tasha:** 番茄牛腩 / Tomato beef brisket.
- `00:33:48,900 → 00:33:49,933` **Jake:** 白板担当 / The whiteboard person.
- `00:33:50,166 → 00:33:51,366` **Tasha:** 嗯都可以 / Yeah, anything is fine.
- `00:33:51,366 → 00:33:52,433` **Alice:** 这个或者 / This one or...
- `00:33:51,666 → 00:33:51,866` **Jake:** 留的 / Reserved.
- `00:33:53,233 → 00:33:53,933` **Alice:** 哎你刚说哪个 / Hey, which one did you just say?
- `00:33:53,933 → 00:33:55,500` **Tasha:** 锅包肉回锅肉 / Sweet and sour pork, twice-cooked pork.
- `00:33:55,733 → 00:33:56,766` **Tasha:** 哈哈 / Haha.
- `00:33:57,000 → 00:33:57,933` **Jake:** 要两种肉 / Want two kinds of meat.
- `00:33:58,900 → 00:34:01,166` **Alice:** 麻辣的孜然的 / Spicy cumin.
- `00:34:01,433 → 00:34:02,700` **Alice:** 这是酸辣的 / This one is sour and spicy.
- `00:34:09,233 → 00:34:09,966` **Katrina:** 是我们四个 / It's the four of us.
- `00:34:09,966 → 00:34:11,466` **Katrina:** 还带他一起吗 / Are we taking him along?
- `00:34:13,633 → 00:34:15,900` **Lucia:** 对我们一起四个菜 / Yeah, the four of us together, four dishes.
- `00:34:16,066 → 00:34:17,133` **Katrina:** 三荤一素 / Three meat, one veggie.
- `00:34:16,700 → 00:34:16,833` **Tasha:** 三 / Three.
- `00:34:18,100 → 00:34:19,333` **Tasha:** 哦有素菜吗 / Oh, is there a veggie dish?
- `00:34:19,333 → 00:34:20,566` **Tasha:** 我们没有点过素菜 / We haven't ordered a veggie dish.
- `00:34:20,566 → 00:34:21,900` **Tasha:** 哈哈哈 / Hahaha.
- `00:34:20,966 → 00:34:21,666` **Jake:** 呵呵 / Hehe.
- `00:34:21,900 → 00:34:23,700` **Alice:** 你俩想吃哪个你点两个啊 / Which one do you two want? You pick two.
- `00:34:23,700 → 00:34:24,966` **Tasha:** 哦 不 我要 / Oh, no, I want...
- `00:34:23,766 → 00:34:25,566` **Lucia:** 或者或者或者番茄牛腩 / Or, or, or tomato beef brisket.
- `00:34:25,566 → 00:34:26,366` **Lucia:** 里面有番茄 / It has tomatoes.
- `00:34:26,366 → 00:34:27,700` **Lucia:** 哈哈 / Haha.
- `00:34:27,700 → 00:34:29,866` **Lucia:** 姑且算是半个素菜 / Let's count it as half a veggie dish.
- `00:34:29,866 → 00:34:31,700` **Lucia:** 那那素菜 / Then, the veggie dish...
- `00:34:31,700 → 00:34:34,233` **Tasha:** 素菜里面要一个手撕包菜 / For the veggie dish, let's have hand-torn cabbage.
- `00:34:34,233 → 00:34:36,433` **Tasha:** 或者要一个酸辣土豆丝 / Or spicy shredded potatoes.
- `00:34:36,666 → 00:34:39,366` **Alice:** 哈哈可以土豆在这等着呢啊 / Haha, the potatoes are waiting here.
- `00:35:16,500 → 00:35:17,266` **Tasha:** 还差个汤 / We're still missing a soup.
- `00:35:17,266 → 00:35:18,300` **Tasha:** 现在就开始煮吧 / Let's start cooking now.
- `00:35:18,466 → 00:35:20,566` **Tasha:** 哈哈现在开始煮汤 / Haha, let's start making the soup now.
- `00:35:20,866 → 00:35:21,933` **Alice:** 今天晚上就喝吧 / Let's drink it tonight.
- `00:35:23,800 → 00:35:24,400` **Alice:** 是吧 / Right?
- `00:35:25,033 → 00:35:26,633` **Tasha:** 是今天晚上开始做汤吗 / Are we making the soup tonight?
- `00:35:27,933 → 00:35:29,633` **Tasha:** 反正吃家常菜吗 / Anyway, we're having homemade food, right?
- `00:35:30,133 → 00:35:32,433` **Jake:** 可以啊你说等的时候做汤吗 / Sure, are you saying make the soup while we wait?
- `00:35:32,433 → 00:35:32,666` **Tasha:** 嗯 / Yeah.
- `00:35:32,433 → 00:35:33,466` **Jake:** 可以 / Sure.
- `00:35:34,366 → 00:35:35,766` **Jake:** 他要送多久啊 / How long will it take?
- `00:35:35,766 → 00:35:36,900` **Jake:** 40分钟 / 40 minutes.
- `00:35:36,900 → 00:35:37,300` **Alice:** 可能吧 / Maybe.
- `00:35:37,300 → 00:35:38,266` **Katrina:** 送密封袋 / Delivering the sealed bags.
- `00:35:38,433 → 00:35:40,433` **Tasha:** 现在是7点半 / It's 7:30 now.
- `00:35:39,333 → 00:35:40,300` **Jake:** 密封哦 / Sealed, okay.
- `00:35:41,066 → 00:35:41,666` **Katrina:** 那我试验一下 / Let me test it.
- `00:35:41,700 → 00:35:43,066` **Tasha:** 那我现在开始在做甜品的话 / If I start making the dessert now
- `00:35:43,100 → 00:35:44,933` **Tasha:** 8点半还能开会哦 / We can still have a meeting at 8:30.
- `00:35:44,966 → 00:35:45,633` **Jake:** 好 / Okay.
- `00:35:47,933 → 00:35:48,833` **Katrina:** 今天晚上开会吗 / Are we having a meeting tonight?
- `00:35:48,833 → 00:35:49,566` **Alice:** 点这个吧 / Tap this.
- `00:35:49,500 → 00:35:50,266` **Jake:** 她要开会 / She has a meeting.
- `00:35:49,566 → 00:35:50,166` **Alice:** 我不太想吃猪 / I don't really want to eat pork.
- `00:35:50,166 → 00:35:50,833` **Alice:** 可以吗 / Is that okay?
- `00:35:50,833 → 00:35:51,566` **Tasha:** 可以啊可以 / Sure, sure.
- `00:35:51,566 → 00:35:53,033` **Jake:** 他自己要开会 / She has her own meeting.
- `00:35:53,100 → 00:35:54,633` **Katrina:** 我可能有点事 / I might have something to do.
- `00:35:53,966 → 00:35:54,733` **Jake:** 他学校 / It's her school.
- `00:35:55,133 → 00:35:56,100` **Alice:** 那我们开始做吧 / Let's start then.
- `00:35:55,300 → 00:35:55,800` **Jake:** ok / Ok.
- `00:35:56,100 → 00:35:57,033` **Alice:** 你让他点 / Let him choose.
- `00:35:57,166 → 00:35:58,900` **Katrina:** 摘两片芍药花瓣 / Pick two peony petals.
- `00:36:02,033 → 00:36:02,966` **Tasha:** 神圣的交接仪 / The sacred handover ceremony.
- `00:36:02,666 → 00:36:03,866` **Alice:** 来吧我们开始吧 / Come on, let's start.
- `00:36:03,933 → 00:36:05,033` **Tasha:** 好我们开始吧 / Okay, let's start.
- `00:36:05,033 → 00:36:06,900` **Alice:** 我们几个人做汤的话需要 / If we're making soup, we need a few people.
- `00:36:07,533 → 00:36:08,433` **Tasha:** 你需要一个配手 / You need a helper.
- `00:36:08,433 → 00:36:09,366` **Tasha:** 我需要一个配手 / I need a helper.
- `00:36:09,366 → 00:36:10,066` **Tasha:** 是吗 / Right?
- `00:36:10,066 → 00:36:10,666` **Alice:** 我不需要哦 / I don't need one.
- `00:36:10,666 → 00:36:12,233` **Alice:** 我自己给自己打下手 / I'm helping myself out.
- `00:36:12,666 → 00:36:13,466` **Jake:** 牛啊 / Impressive.
- `00:36:13,566 → 00:36:14,133` **Tasha:** 好 / Okay.
- `00:36:14,266 → 00:36:15,966` **Katrina:** 好 需要我帮忙吗 / Alright, do you need my help?
- `00:36:16,633 → 00:36:18,466` **Alice:** 我们需要做多少量呢 / How much do we need to make?
- `00:36:19,500 → 00:36:20,866` **Tasha:** 嗯一人一碗的量就行 / Hmm, just enough for one bowl per person.
- `00:36:20,866 → 00:36:21,366` **Tasha:** 哈哈 / Haha.
- `00:36:21,366 → 00:36:21,733` **Alice:** 一人一碗是 / One bowl per person is...
- `00:36:21,733 → 00:36:24,233` **Alice:** 哎我不知道大家的饭量 / Uh, I don't know how much everyone eats.
- `00:36:24,366 → 00:36:25,366` **Alice:** 嗯对汤就汤多 / Hmm, for soup, just make more.
- `00:36:25,500 → 00:36:26,333` **Lucia:** 汤嘛 / Soup?
- `00:36:26,400 → 00:36:29,400` **Alice:** 对 就汤多 / Yeah, just more soup.
- `00:36:29,700 → 00:36:31,066` **Alice:** 多一点应该叶没事 / A bit more should be fine.
- `00:36:30,700 → 00:36:32,166` **Lucia:** 这个多少应该 / How much should this be?
- `00:36:32,366 → 00:36:33,933` **Alice:** 那我把我戒指取下来 / Then I'll take off my ring.
- `00:36:36,966 → 00:36:38,333` **Alice:** 不行都放不掉 / No, it won't come off.
- `00:36:39,066 → 00:36:40,266` **Lucia:** 需要我做什么呢 / What do you need me to do?
- `00:36:42,333 → 00:36:44,633` **Lucia:** 哈哈哈等待指派中 / Hahaha, waiting for instructions.
- `00:36:44,933 → 00:36:45,933` **Tasha:** 等一下 / Wait a second.
- `00:36:45,933 → 00:36:47,366` **Jake:** 我问一下谢明珠来不来 / I'll ask if Xie Mingzhu is coming.
- `00:36:47,366 → 00:36:48,333` **Jake:** 他有可能来 / He might come.
- `00:36:48,466 → 00:36:50,066` **Jake:** 来的话就多加一个人 / If he comes, we'll add one more person.
- `00:36:50,133 → 00:36:50,933` **Tasha:** 哎米饭是不是没点 / Hey, did we order the rice?
- `00:36:50,933 → 00:36:52,333` **Tasha:** 谁来谢冰珠 / Who's coming, Xie Bingzhu?
- `00:36:52,366 → 00:36:53,000` **Tasha:** 米饭掉了吗 / Did the rice get ordered?
- `00:36:53,000 → 00:36:54,066` **Jake:** 就是那个 / It's that one.
- `00:36:54,866 → 00:36:56,066` **Jake:** 没有没点 / No, we didn't order.
- `00:36:56,066 → 00:36:57,066` **Tasha:** 为什么不点米饭 / Why didn't you order rice?
- `00:36:57,133 → 00:36:58,266` **Jake:** 因为我还没下单呢 / Because I haven't placed the order yet.
- `00:36:58,266 → 00:36:59,133` **Tasha:** 哦那就行 / Oh, that's fine then.
- `00:36:59,333 → 00:37:00,066` **Lucia:** 哈哈哈 / Hahaha.
- `00:36:59,500 → 00:37:01,400` **Jake:** 呵呵呵呵呵呵 / Hehehehehe.
- `00:37:00,266 → 00:37:01,433` **Alice:** 那我现在开始吧 / Then I'll start now.
- `00:37:01,433 → 00:37:01,633` **Jake:** 害怕 / Scared.
- `00:37:01,633 → 00:37:03,233` **Tasha:** 好的你先开始 / Okay, you start first.
- `00:37:03,233 → 00:37:03,566` **Jake:** 害怕 / Scared.
- `00:37:04,966 → 00:37:06,633` **Lucia:** 我要不简单的 / I want something simple.
- `00:37:10,866 → 00:37:13,266` **Lucia:** 做饭的话我要不要换个洞 / If I cook, should I change the hole?
- `00:37:13,266 → 00:37:14,700` **Lucia:** 我能把它先摘下来一下吗 / Can I take it off first?
- `00:37:14,700 → 00:37:15,300` **Lucia:** 我换个衣服 / I'll change clothes.
- `00:37:14,766 → 00:37:15,300` **Jake:** 可以可以 / Sure, sure.
- `00:37:15,366 → 00:37:16,266` **Lucia:** 这样好做一点 / This way it's easier.
- `00:37:15,700 → 00:37:16,566` **Jake:** 唉，好行 / Yeah, okay, fine.
- `00:37:17,266 → 00:37:18,233` **Lucia:** 不用关吧 / No need to turn it off, right?
- `00:37:18,000 → 00:37:20,566` **Jake:** 你就放在这里对对对嗯嗯 / Just put it here, yeah, yeah, right.
- `00:37:18,300 → 00:37:19,400` **Lucia:** 我在这里放一下吧 / I'll put it here for now.
- `00:37:19,433 → 00:37:20,233` **Lucia:** 然后一会再下去 / Then we'll move it later.
- `00:37:21,366 → 00:37:22,966` **Alice:** 哎呀冻得梆梆硬 / Oh my, it's frozen rock solid.
- `00:37:24,566 → 00:37:25,633` **Alice:** 放上边吧 / Put it on top.
- `00:37:26,866 → 00:37:28,500` **Lucia:** 我还以为我一直连着电线呢 / I thought I was still plugged in.
- `00:37:28,500 → 00:37:29,300` **Lucia:** 居然没有 / But apparently not.
- `00:37:32,333 → 00:37:34,333` **Jake:** 好那就趁机充个电 / Well, let's take the chance to charge it.
- `00:37:35,300 → 00:37:36,333` **Lucia:** 哎有道理 / Ah, good idea.
- `00:37:37,133 → 00:37:38,233` **Lucia:** 先把它连上 / Let's connect it first.
- `00:37:47,766 → 00:37:50,466` **Alice:** 怎么会把它放在这里面呢 / Why did we put it in here?
- `00:37:50,500 → 00:37:52,966` **Alice:** 我们是买了两个金针菇还是一个呀 / Did we buy two enoki mushrooms or just one?
- `00:37:53,633 → 00:37:54,400` **Tasha:** 一个好像 / Just one, I think.
- `00:37:54,533 → 00:37:55,900` **Alice:** 一个 / Just one.
- `00:37:55,966 → 00:37:56,433` **Tasha:** 一袋 / One bag.

- `00:23:52,933 → 00:23:53,933` **Jake:** 应该有吧 / There should be.

- `00:24:01,633 → 00:24:02,900` **Jake:** 给你个46怎么样 / How about a 46 for you?

- `00:24:04,333 → 00:24:04,966` **Jake:** 你是什么 / What do you have?
- `00:24:04,966 → 00:24:07,933` **Tasha:** 我想要自带线的好苹果 / I want a good Apple with a built-in cable
- `00:24:07,000 → 00:24:10,366` **Jake:** 哎哎哎 / Hey, hey, hey

- `00:24:48,366 → 00:24:50,233` **Alice:** 呃没事再重拉一个 / Uh, no worries, just make a new one

- `00:24:55,300 → 00:25:00,100` **Jake:** 额刚刚我刚刚我头在下面一直一刀下去 / Uh, just now, just now, my head was down, and a knife came down
